```rust
/// The ids `of`, as the store gives them.
fn ids(of: &[&String]) -> Vec<String> {
    of.iter().map(|id| id.to_string()).collect()
}

impl Fixture {
    /// A workflow run of `new`, started a moment after the one before: runs started in one
    /// millisecond would sort by their random ids.
    async fn started(&self, new: &NewWorkflowRun) -> WorkflowRun {
        tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(5)).await;
        self.store.create_workflow_run(new).await.unwrap()
    }

    /// Moves the workflow run `id` to `status`.
    async fn now(&self, id: &str, status: WorkflowRunStatus) {
        self.store
            .set_workflow_run(
                id,
                &WorkflowRunChange {
                    status: Some(status),
                    ..Default::default()
                },
            )
            .await
            .unwrap();
    }

    /// The ids of the runs `filter` picks, in its order.
    async fn picked(&self, filter: WorkflowRunFilter) -> Vec<String> {
        let runs = self.store.find_workflow_runs(&filter).await.unwrap();
        runs.into_iter().map(|wr| wr.id).collect()
    }

    /// The ids of the workflow runs the dot runs `origins` started, in their order.
    async fn from(&self, origins: &[&String]) -> Vec<String> {
        let origins: Vec<String> = origins.iter().map(|id| id.to_string()).collect();
        let runs = self.store.workflow_runs_from(&origins).await.unwrap();
        runs.into_iter().map(|wr| wr.id).collect()
    }

    /// A chat run of the fixture's dot.
    async fn chat(&self) -> String {
        self.store
            .create_run(&NewRun::new(&self.dot, TriggerKind::Chat))
            .await
            .unwrap()
            .id
    }
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn workflow_runs_are_found_by_team_workflow_status_and_limit_newest_first() {
    let f = fixture().await;
    let other = f
        .store
        .create_workflow(
            &f.new.team_id,
            &dots_core::model::WorkflowSpec {
                name: "Other".into(),
                definition: f.new.definition.clone(),
                enabled: true,
            },
        )
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let of_other = NewWorkflowRun {
        workflow_id: other.id.clone(),
        ..f.new.clone()
    };
    let running = f.started(&f.new).await.id;
    let waiting = f.started(&f.new).await.id;
    f.now(&waiting, WorkflowRunStatus::Waiting).await;
    let done = f.started(&f.new).await.id;
    f.now(&done, WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded).await;
    let elsewhere = f.started(&of_other).await.id;

    let all = ids(&[&elsewhere, &done, &waiting, &running]);
    assert_eq!(f.picked(WorkflowRunFilter::default()).await, all);
    let team = WorkflowRunFilter {
        team_id: Some(f.new.team_id.clone()),
        ..Default::default()
    };
    assert_eq!(f.picked(team.clone()).await, all);
    let fix = WorkflowRunFilter {
        workflow_id: Some(f.new.workflow_id.clone()),
        ..team
    };
    assert_eq!(
        f.picked(fix.clone()).await,
        ids(&[&done, &waiting, &running])
    );
    let in_flight = WorkflowRunFilter {
        statuses: vec![WorkflowRunStatus::Running, WorkflowRunStatus::Waiting],
        ..Default::default()
    };
    assert_eq!(
        f.picked(in_flight.clone()).await,
        ids(&[&elsewhere, &waiting, &running])
    );
    assert_eq!(
        f.picked(WorkflowRunFilter {
            limit: Some(1),
            ..in_flight
        })
        .await,
        ids(&[&elsewhere])
    );
    assert_eq!(
        f.picked(WorkflowRunFilter {
            statuses: vec![WorkflowRunStatus::Waiting],
            ..fix.clone()
        })
        .await,
        ids(&[&waiting])
    );
    assert_eq!(
        f.picked(WorkflowRunFilter {
            limit: Some(2),
            ..fix
        })
        .await,
        ids(&[&done, &waiting])
    );
    assert!(f
        .picked(WorkflowRunFilter {
            team_id: Some("no-team".into()),
            ..Default::default()
        })
        .await
        .is_empty());
    assert!(f
        .picked(WorkflowRunFilter {
            statuses: vec![WorkflowRunStatus::Failed],
            ..Default::default()
        })
        .await
        .is_empty());
    // A filter as the app sends it: every field may be left out.
    let sent: WorkflowRunFilter =
        serde_json::from_value(json!({"statuses": ["waiting"], "limit": 5})).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(sent.team_id, None);
    assert_eq!(f.picked(sent).await, ids(&[&waiting]));
    // The team's list is the filter with a team and a limit.
    let listed = f
        .store
        .list_workflow_runs(Some(&f.new.team_id), 2)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let listed: Vec<_> = listed.iter().map(|wr| &wr.id).collect();
    assert_eq!(listed, [&elsewhere, &done]);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn the_runs_a_chat_started_are_found_by_its_runs_oldest_first() {
    let f = fixture().await;
    let (one, two, quiet) = (f.chat().await, f.chat().await, f.chat().await);
    let from = |origin: &str| NewWorkflowRun {
        trigger: WorkflowTrigger::Chat,
        origin_run_id: Some(origin.to_string()),
        ..f.new.clone()
    };
    let first = f.started(&from(&one)).await.id;
    let manual = f.started(&f.new).await.id;
    let second = f.started(&from(&two)).await.id;
    let again = f.started(&from(&one)).await.id;
    f.now(&again, WorkflowRunStatus::Cancelled).await;

    assert_eq!(
        f.from(&[&two, &one, &quiet]).await,
        ids(&[&first, &second, &again])
    );
    assert_eq!(f.from(&[&two]).await, ids(&[&second]));
    assert!(f.from(&[&quiet]).await.is_empty());
    assert!(
        f.from(&[&manual]).await.is_empty(),
        "a workflow run's id is no origin"
    );
    assert!(f.from(&[]).await.is_empty());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_workflow_says_how_many_runs_are_in_flight_and_which_started_last() {
    let f = fixture().await;
    let id = f.new.workflow_id.clone();
    let fix = f.store.get_workflow(&id).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!((fix.runs_in_flight, fix.last_run), (0, None));

    let first = f.started(&f.new).await;
    let fix = f.store.get_workflow(&id).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(fix.runs_in_flight, 1);
    assert_eq!(
        fix.last_run,
        Some(LastRun {
            id: first.id.clone(),
            status: WorkflowRunStatus::Running,
            started_at: first.started_at.clone(),
            test: false,
        })
    );
    let test = f
        .started(&NewWorkflowRun {
            test: true,
            ..f.new.clone()
        })
        .await;
    f.now(&test.id, WorkflowRunStatus::Waiting).await;
    let listed = f.store.list_workflows(&f.new.team_id).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(listed.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(listed[0].runs_in_flight, 2);
    assert_eq!(
        listed[0].last_run,
        Some(LastRun {
            id: test.id.clone(),
            status: WorkflowRunStatus::Waiting,
            started_at: test.started_at.clone(),
            test: true,
        })
    );
    // Ended runs are not in flight; the newest stays the last run, whatever its status.
    f.now(&first.id, WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded).await;
    f.now(&test.id, WorkflowRunStatus::Failed).await;
    let saved = f.store.update_workflow(&id, &fix.spec).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(saved.runs_in_flight, 0);
    let last = saved.last_run.clone().expect("a last run");
    assert_eq!((last.id, last.status), (test.id, WorkflowRunStatus::Failed));
    // Serialized beside the rest of the workflow.
    let value = serde_json::to_value(&saved).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(value["runs_in_flight"], json!(0));
    assert_eq!(value["last_run"]["status"], json!("failed"));
    assert_eq!(value["last_run"]["test"], json!(true));
}
```

```rust
/// The problem of `message` about the step `step_id`.
fn problem(step_id: Option<&str>, message: &str) -> WorkflowProblem {
    WorkflowProblem {
        step_id: step_id.map(str::to_string),
        message: message.to_string(),
    }
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_workflow_is_checked_as_it_is_edited_by_the_rules_a_save_uses() {
    let (_dir, store) = temp_store().await;
    let helper = dot(&store, "helper").await;
    let dev = dot(&store, "dev").await;
    let teams = Teams::new(store.clone(), new_bus());
    let t = teams.create(&team("T", &[&helper])).await.unwrap();
    let saved = teams
        .create_workflow(&t.id, &flow("Fix", &helper))
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        teams
            .check_workflow(&t.id, &flow("Fix", &helper))
            .await
            .unwrap(),
        None
    );

    let named = |name: &str| flow(name, &helper);
    let with = |change: &dyn Fn(&mut Definition)| {
        let mut spec = flow("Fix", &helper);
        change(&mut spec.definition);
        spec
    };
    let check_after = Step {
        id: "check".into(),
        name: "Your check".into(),
        kind: StepKind::Check {
            send_back_to: Some("later".into()),
        },
    };
    let cases = [
        (
            named(" "),
            problem(None, "a workflow name must be 1 to 64 characters"),
        ),
        (
            with(&|d| d.trigger = Trigger::Chat { starters: vec![] }),
            problem(
                None,
                "a chat trigger needs at least one member who starts it",
            ),
        ),
        (
            with(&|d| d.steps.clear()),
            problem(None, "a workflow needs at least one step"),
        ),
        (
            flow("Fix", &dev),
            problem(
                Some("a"),
                &format!("step \"Work\": {dev} is not a member of the team"),
            ),
        ),
        (
            with(&|d| {
                let mut twin = d.steps[0].clone();
                twin.name = "Twin".into();
                d.steps.push(twin);
            }),
            problem(Some("a"), "step \"Twin\": another step has the id \"a\""),
        ),
        (
            with(&|d| d.steps.push(check_after.clone())),
            problem(
                Some("check"),
                "step \"Your check\": work can be sent back only to an agent step before it, \
                 not to \"later\"",
            ),
        ),
        (
            with(&|d| d.budget.max_minutes = 0),
            problem(None, "every budget limit must be at least 1"),
        ),
    ];
    for (spec, want) in cases {
        let got = teams.check_workflow(&t.id, &spec).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(got.as_ref(), Some(&want), "{spec:?}");
        // A save is refused with the same message, and changes nothing.
        match teams.update_workflow(&saved.id, &spec).await {
            Err(Error::Invalid(m)) => assert_eq!(m, want.message),
            other => panic!("{spec:?} was not refused: {other:?}"),
        }
        match teams
            .create_workflow(&t.id, &named_like(&spec, "New"))
            .await
        {
            Err(Error::Invalid(m)) => assert_eq!(m, want.message),
            other => panic!("{spec:?} was not refused: {other:?}"),
        }
    }
    assert_eq!(teams.get_workflow(&saved.id).await.unwrap(), saved);
    // As JSON, for the builder.
    let json = serde_json::to_value(
        teams
            .check_workflow(&t.id, &flow("Fix", &dev))
            .await
            .unwrap(),
    )
    .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        json,
        serde_json::json!({
            "step_id": "a",
            "message": format!("step \"Work\": {dev} is not a member of the team"),
        })
    );
    // A name another workflow has is left to the save, which knows which one is edited.
    let other = teams
        .create_workflow(&t.id, &flow("Other", &helper))
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        teams.check_workflow(&t.id, &named("Fix")).await.unwrap(),
        None
    );
    assert!(matches!(
        teams.update_workflow(&other.id, &named("Fix")).await,
        Err(Error::Conflict(m)) if m == "the team has a workflow named \"Fix\""
    ));
    // The members are the team's now.
    teams
        .update(&t.id, &team("T", &[&helper, &dev]))
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        teams
            .check_workflow(&t.id, &flow("Fix", &dev))
            .await
            .unwrap(),
        None
    );
    assert!(matches!(
        teams.check_workflow("nope", &named("Fix")).await,
        Err(Error::NotFound(_))
    ));
}

/// `spec` under another name, unless its own name is the problem.
fn named_like(spec: &WorkflowSpec, name: &str) -> WorkflowSpec {
    let mut spec = spec.clone();
    if !spec.name.trim().is_empty() {
        spec.name = name.into();
    }
    spec
}

/// What `events` has had since it was last read: `team` and `workflow <name>` for the team and
/// workflow events, by the names in `workflows`.
fn announced(
    events: &mut tokio::sync::broadcast::Receiver<RuntimeEvent>,
    workflows: &[(&str, &str)],
) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut seen = Vec::new();
    while let Ok(e) = events.try_recv() {
        seen.push(match e {
            RuntimeEvent::TeamUpdated { .. } => "team".to_string(),
            RuntimeEvent::WorkflowUpdated { workflow_id, .. } => {
                let name = workflows.iter().find(|(id, _)| *id == workflow_id);
                format!("workflow {}", name.map_or("?", |(_, n)| n))
            }
            other => format!("{other:?}"),
        });
    }
    seen
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_change_of_members_announces_each_workflow_of_the_team() {
    let (_dir, store) = temp_store().await;
    let helper = dot(&store, "helper").await;
    let dev = dot(&store, "dev").await;
    let bus = new_bus();
    let teams = Teams::new(store.clone(), bus.clone());
    let t = teams.create(&team("T", &[&helper, &dev])).await.unwrap();
    let fix = teams
        .create_workflow(&t.id, &flow("Fix", &helper))
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let ship = teams
        .create_workflow(&t.id, &flow("Ship", &dev))
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let names = [(fix.id.as_str(), "Fix"), (ship.id.as_str(), "Ship")];
    let mut events = bus.subscribe();

    // Other roles, another order, a new name: the same members.
    let mut same = team("Crew", &[&dev, &helper]);
    same.members[0].role = "lead".into();
    teams.update(&t.id, &same).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(announced(&mut events, &names), ["team"]);

    // A member removed: the workflow that names it gets a problem, and both are announced.
    teams
        .update(&t.id, &team("Crew", &[&helper]))
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        announced(&mut events, &names),
        ["team", "workflow Fix", "workflow Ship"]
    );
    assert_eq!(teams.get_workflow(&fix.id).await.unwrap().problem, None);
    assert!(teams
        .get_workflow(&ship.id)
        .await
        .unwrap()
        .problem
        .is_some());

    // A member added back.
    teams
        .update(&t.id, &team("Crew", &[&helper, &dev]))
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        announced(&mut events, &names),
        ["team", "workflow Fix", "workflow Ship"]
    );
    assert_eq!(teams.get_workflow(&ship.id).await.unwrap().problem, None);

    // Deleting the team announces each workflow it removed, then the team.
    teams.delete(&t.id).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        announced(&mut events, &names),
        ["workflow Fix", "workflow Ship", "team"]
    );
}
```

```rust
#[tokio::test]
async fn the_workflow_s_url_is_the_one_its_webhook_answers_on() {
    let t = Rt::start().await;
    let (workflow, token) = t.hooked(Some(TRIGGER_TASK), Some("Dots")).await;
    let url = t.rt.workflow_webhook_url(&workflow.id).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        url,
        format!(
            "http://127.0.0.1:{}/workflows/{}/trigger",
            t.rt.port(),
            workflow.id
        )
    );
    let resp = t.http.post(&url).bearer_auth(&token).send().await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(resp.status().as_u16(), 202);
    assert!(matches!(
        t.rt.workflow_webhook_url("nope").await,
        Err(dots_core::Error::NotFound(m)) if m == "workflow nope"
    ));
    t.rt.shutdown();
}
```

```rust
impl World {
    /// Starts a run of `workflow` as `start` does, a moment after the one before (runs started
    /// in one millisecond would sort by their random ids), from the chat run `origin` and as a
    /// test run when asked.
    async fn start_from(&self, workflow: &str, origin: Option<&str>, test: bool) -> WorkflowRun {
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(5)).await;
        self.workflows
            .start(StartRequest {
                workflow_id: workflow.into(),
                project_id: self.project.clone(),
                task: "The tray icon is invisible in dark mode.".into(),
                trigger: if origin.is_some() {
                    WorkflowTrigger::Chat
                } else {
                    WorkflowTrigger::Manual
                },
                origin_run_id: origin.map(str::to_string),
                test,
            })
            .await
            .unwrap()
    }

    /// The ids of the workflow runs `filter` lists, in its order.
    async fn listed(&self, filter: WorkflowRunFilter) -> Vec<String> {
        let runs = self.workflows.list(&filter).await.unwrap();
        runs.into_iter().map(|wr| wr.id).collect()
    }
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn workflow_runs_are_listed_by_workflow_and_status_and_found_by_the_chat_that_started_them() {
    let w = world().await;
    let fix = w.workflow("Fix", vec![check("check")]).await;
    let other = w.workflow("Other", vec![check("check")]).await;
    let chat = w
        .store
        .create_run(&NewRun::new(&w.helper.id, TriggerKind::Chat))
        .await
        .unwrap()
        .id;
    let stopped = w.start_from(&fix, None, false).await;
    w.until(&stopped.id, waits_on(WaitingOn::Check)).await;
    w.workflows.stop(&stopped.id).await.unwrap();
    let carded = w.start_from(&fix, Some(&chat), false).await;
    let elsewhere = w.start_from(&other, None, true).await;
    for wr in [&carded, &elsewhere] {
        w.until(&wr.id, waits_on(WaitingOn::Check)).await;
    }

    let fix_runs = WorkflowRunFilter {
        team_id: Some(w.team.clone()),
        workflow_id: Some(fix.clone()),
        ..Default::default()
    };
    assert_eq!(
        w.listed(fix_runs.clone()).await,
        [carded.id.as_str(), stopped.id.as_str()]
    );
    assert_eq!(
        w.listed(WorkflowRunFilter {
            statuses: vec![WorkflowRunStatus::Waiting],
            ..Default::default()
        })
        .await,
        [elsewhere.id.as_str(), carded.id.as_str()]
    );
    assert_eq!(
        w.listed(WorkflowRunFilter {
            statuses: vec![WorkflowRunStatus::Cancelled],
            ..fix_runs
        })
        .await,
        [stopped.id.as_str()]
    );
    assert_eq!(
        w.listed(WorkflowRunFilter {
            limit: Some(1),
            ..Default::default()
        })
        .await,
        [elsewhere.id.as_str()]
    );

    // The chat card: the runs its chat started, whole.
    let cards = w
        .workflows
        .for_origins(std::slice::from_ref(&chat))
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(cards.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(cards[0].id, carded.id);
    assert_eq!(cards[0].origin_run_id.as_deref(), Some(chat.as_str()));
    assert_eq!(cards[0].waiting_on, Some(WaitingOn::Check));
    assert!(w.workflows.for_origins(&[]).await.unwrap().is_empty());

    // Each workflow says what runs it has in flight and which started last.
    let fix_now = w.store.get_workflow(&fix).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(fix_now.runs_in_flight, 1);
    let last = fix_now.last_run.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        (last.id.as_str(), last.status, last.test),
        (carded.id.as_str(), WorkflowRunStatus::Waiting, false)
    );
    let other_now = w.store.get_workflow(&other).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(other_now.runs_in_flight, 1);
    assert!(other_now.last_run.unwrap().test);
    w.workflows.stop(&carded.id).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(w.store.get_workflow(&fix).await.unwrap().runs_in_flight, 0);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn deleting_a_team_announces_each_workflow_it_removes() {
    let w = world().await;
    w.engine.set(&w.dev.spec.name, vec![Act::Hold]);
    let fix = w
        .workflow("Fix", vec![agent("dev", "Fix it", &w.dev, "Fix.")])
        .await;
    let ship = w.workflow("Ship", vec![check("check")]).await;
    let wr = w.start(&fix).await;
    w.until(&wr.id, |wr| wr.current_step.as_deref() == Some("dev"))
        .await;
    let mut events = w.bus.subscribe();
    w.workflows.delete_team(&w.team).await.unwrap();
    let mut seen = Vec::new();
    while let Ok(e) = events.try_recv() {
        match e {
            // A pass may announce the run before the stop; the stop announces it cancelled.
            RuntimeEvent::WorkflowRunUpdated { run }
                if run.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Cancelled =>
            {
                seen.push("run cancelled".to_string())
            }
            RuntimeEvent::WorkflowUpdated {
                team_id,
                workflow_id,
            } => {
                assert_eq!(team_id, w.team);
                let name = if workflow_id == fix { "Fix" } else { "?" };
                let name = if workflow_id == ship { "Ship" } else { name };
                seen.push(format!("workflow {name}"));
            }
            RuntimeEvent::TeamUpdated { team_id } => {
                assert_eq!(team_id, w.team);
                seen.push("team".into());
            }
            _ => {}
        }
    }
    assert_eq!(
        seen,
        ["run cancelled", "workflow Fix", "workflow Ship", "team"]
    );
}
```

```rust
impl WorkflowSpec {
    /// Its name and its definition against the team's `members` (dot ids). Refuses with
    /// `problem`'s message.
    pub fn validate(&self, members: &[String]) -> Result<()> {
        match self.problem(members) {
            Some(p) => Err(Error::Invalid(p.message)),
            None => Ok(()),
        }
    }

    /// The first refusal `validate` gives, with the step it names: its name's, then its
    /// definition's (`Definition::problem`). `None` when it gives none.
    pub fn problem(&self, members: &[String]) -> Option<crate::workflow::WorkflowProblem> {
        if let Err(e) = check_name("workflow", &self.name) {
            return Some(crate::workflow::WorkflowProblem::new(None, e));
        }
        self.definition.problem(members)
    }
```

```rust
    /// The workflow runs that the dot runs `run_ids` started (`origin_run_id`), oldest first.
    pub async fn workflow_runs_from(&self, run_ids: &[String]) -> Result<Vec<WorkflowRun>> {
        let ids: Vec<String> = sqlx::query_scalar(
            "SELECT id FROM workflow_runs \
             WHERE origin_run_id IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(?)) ORDER BY started_at, id",
        )
        .bind(serde_json::to_string(run_ids)?)
        .fetch_all(&self.pool)
        .await?;
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        for id in ids {
            out.push(self.get_workflow_run(&id).await?);
        }
        Ok(out)
    }

    /// The workflow's newest run, as the team page shows it.
    pub async fn last_workflow_run(&self, workflow_id: &str) -> Result<Option<LastRun>> {
        let row = sqlx::query(
            "SELECT id, status, started_at, test FROM workflow_runs WHERE workflow_id = ? \
             ORDER BY started_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1",
        )
        .bind(workflow_id)
        .fetch_optional(&self.pool)
        .await?;
        row.map(|row| {
            Ok(LastRun {
                id: row.try_get("id")?,
                status: WorkflowRunStatus::parse(&row.try_get::<String, _>("status")?)?,
                started_at: row.try_get("started_at")?,
                test: row.try_get::<i64, _>("test")? != 0,
            })
        })
        .transpose()
    }
```

```rust
    /// Announces each workflow of the team: its members changed, so its `problem` may have.
    /// Called after the change, which stands whatever this finds: a failed read is logged.
    async fn workflows_updated(&self, team_id: &str) {
        match self.store.list_workflows(team_id).await {
            Ok(workflows) => workflows
                .iter()
                .for_each(|w| self.workflow_updated(team_id, &w.id)),
            Err(e) => {
                tracing::warn!(team = %team_id, "announcing the team's workflows failed: {e}")
            }
        }
    }

    /// Announces the teams `team_ids` of a dot that was deleted, read before it was: it left
    /// them, so each team and each of its workflows changed.
    pub(crate) async fn dot_deleted(&self, team_ids: &[String]) {
        for team_id in team_ids {
            self.team_updated(team_id);
            self.workflows_updated(team_id).await;
        }
    }

    /// Announces the teams of the dot `dot_id`, which was renamed: a team shows its members by
    /// name.
    pub(crate) async fn dot_renamed(&self, dot_id: &str) {
        match self.store.teams_of_dot(dot_id).await {
            Ok(team_ids) => team_ids.iter().for_each(|id| self.team_updated(id)),
            Err(e) => tracing::warn!(dot = %dot_id, "announcing the dot's teams failed: {e}"),
        }
    }
```

```rust
/// The first rule of section 5.8 a workflow breaks, and the step it is about, so the builder
/// shows it on that step's card and keeps Save off until it is fixed.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct WorkflowProblem {
    /// The id of the step the message names; `None` for the workflow's name, its trigger, its
    /// budget or its list of steps as a whole.
    pub step_id: Option<String>,
    /// The refusal a save would give.
    pub message: String,
}

impl WorkflowProblem {
    /// The problem `e` (a refusal), about the step `step_id`.
    pub(crate) fn new(step_id: Option<&str>, e: Error) -> Self {
        Self {
            step_id: step_id.map(str::to_string),
            message: match e {
                Error::Invalid(m) => m,
                e => e.to_string(),
            },
        }
    }
}
```

```rust
/// The rules of a step's name and id; `ids` are the ids of the steps before it in `all()`, and
/// gets its own.
fn validate_name_and_id<'a>(step: &'a Step, ids: &mut HashSet<&'a str>) -> Result<()> {
    let name = step.name.trim();
    if name.is_empty() || name.chars().count() > MAX_NAME {
        return invalid(format!(
            "step {:?}: a name must be 1 to {MAX_NAME} characters",
            step.id
        ));
    }
    if !id_ok(&step.id) {
        return invalid(format!(
            "step \"{name}\": its id must be 1 to 40 of a-z, 0-9, '-' and '_'"
        ));
    }
    if !ids.insert(step.id.as_str()) {
        return invalid(format!(
            "step \"{name}\": another step has the id {:?}",
            step.id
        ));
    }
    Ok(())
}
```

```rust
    /// Checks the definition against the team's `members` (dot ids): section 5.8's rules. The
    /// message names the step it is about. Refuses with `problem`'s message.
    pub fn validate(&self, members: &[String]) -> Result<()> {
        match self.problem(members) {
            Some(p) => invalid(p.message),
            None => Ok(()),
        }
    }

    /// The first of section 5.8's rules the definition breaks against the team's `members`
    /// (dot ids), with the id of the step it is about; `None` when it breaks none. The one
    /// source of `validate`'s refusals and of the builder's live check.
    pub fn problem(&self, members: &[String]) -> Option<WorkflowProblem> {
        let member = |id: &str| members.iter().any(|m| m == id);
        let whole = |e| Some(WorkflowProblem::new(None, e));
        if let Err(e) = self.validate_trigger(&member) {
            return whole(e);
        }
        if self.steps.is_empty() {
            return whole(Error::Invalid("a workflow needs at least one step".into()));
        }
```

### Task 5: Waiting workflow runs count in Needs you and the Inbox, and step runs stay out of Runs to review

Decisions 5 and 6, core half. `Inbox` gains `workflows`, one `WaitingWorkflowRun` per waiting run (one query, newest first, the step name from the run's own copy of the definition), and `inbox_count` counts them. `dot_counts`'s `needs_you` adds the waiting runs a dot's chat started. `unreviewed_thread_ends` leaves out a lineage whose newest run is a step run. `Inbox` is a strict UI schema, so `types.ts`, the contract sample, the fixture and every UI Inbox literal change in this commit. `Layout.tsx` and `InboxScreen.tsx` are Task 14's.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/dots-core/src/{model.rs,runtime.rs,store/dots.rs,store/runs.rs,store/workflow_runs.rs}`, `apps/dots-app/src/contract.rs`, `ui/src/api/types.ts`, `ui/src/api/__fixtures__/contract.json` (by the command)
- Test: `crates/dots-core/tests/{store_workflow_runs_test,runtime_seen_test}.rs`, `ui/src/api/contract.test.ts`; brought up to date (`workflows: []` in each Inbox literal): `ui/src/test/fixtures.ts`, `ui/src/app/Look.test.tsx`, `ui/src/lib/runtime-events.test.ts`, `ui/src/screens/{BrowserApproval,DotEditorScreen,InboxAppReviews,InboxHeld,InboxScreen,InboxWatchers}.test.tsx`, `ui/src/screens/home/{Instructions,OpenConversation}.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: plan A's `WaitingOn` (with `WaitingOn::ALL`), `Runtime::{inbox, inbox_count}`, `Store::{dot_counts, unreviewed_thread_ends}`; Task 4's nothing.
- Produces: `model::{Inbox.workflows: Vec<WaitingWorkflowRun> (#[serde(default)], newest first), WaitingWorkflowRun { id, team_id, team_name, workflow_id: Option<String>, workflow_name: Option<String>, task (its first line, trimmed), waiting_on: WaitingOn, step_name: Option<String>, origin_dot_id: Option<String>, test: bool, started_at }}`; `Store::waiting_workflow_runs(&self) -> Result<Vec<WaitingWorkflowRun>>`; the contract's `Enums.WaitingOn` and one `Inbox.workflows` sample (waiting on `check` at "Review"); TS `WaitingOn = z.enum(["check", "budget", "send_backs", "step_failed"])`, `WaitingWorkflowRun` (strict), `Inbox.workflows: z.array(z.lazy(() => WaitingWorkflowRun))`; `contract.test.ts`'s `WaitingOn: T.WaitingOn` and its test `knows every WaitingOn the core has`. `InboxScreen.test.tsx`'s `InboxSpec` makes `workflows` optional, as `team_scope`.

```rust
/// A workflow run that waits for the person is in the Inbox and its count, and one a chat
/// started also needs the chat's dot. Once that dot is deleted (and the chat's run with it),
/// the run is in the Inbox only. A failed run of a workflow step is not a run to review.
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_waiting_workflow_run_is_in_the_inbox_and_needs_the_dot_whose_chat_started_it() {
    let t = start().await;
    let member = t.dot("member").await;
    let chatter = t.dot("chatter").await;
    let team =
        t.rt.teams()
            .create(&TeamSpec {
                org_id: None,
                workspace_id: "default".into(),
                name: "crew".into(),
                description: String::new(),
                members: vec![MemberSpec {
                    dot_id: member.id.clone(),
                    role: "dev".into(),
                }],
            })
            .await
            .unwrap();
    let project = ProjectSpec {
        name: "Dots".into(),
        repo_path: t._dir.path().to_string_lossy().to_string(),
        default_branch: None,
        remote: "origin".into(),
    };
    let project_id = t.store().add_project(&team.id, &project).await.unwrap().id;
    let definition = Definition {
        trigger: Trigger::Manual,
        steps: vec![Step {
            id: "a".into(),
            name: "Work".into(),
            kind: StepKind::Agent {
                member: member.id.clone(),
                job: "Fix it.".into(),
                can_send_back_to: vec![],
                outcomes: vec![],
                delegate: None,
            },
        }],
        budget: Budget::default(),
    };
    let workflow = t
        .store()
        .create_workflow(
            &team.id,
            &WorkflowSpec {
                name: "Fix".into(),
                definition: definition.clone(),
                enabled: true,
            },
        )
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let chat = t
        .ended(&chatter, TriggerKind::Chat, RunStatus::Succeeded)
        .await;
    let wr = t
        .store()
        .create_workflow_run(&NewWorkflowRun {
            workflow_id: workflow.id.clone(),
            team_id: team.id.clone(),
            definition,
            project_id,
            project,
            task: "Fix the tray icon.".into(),
            trigger: WorkflowTrigger::Chat,
            origin_run_id: Some(chat.id.clone()),
            test: false,
        })
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let set = |status: WorkflowRunStatus| WorkflowRunChange {
        status: Some(status),
        waiting_on: Some(Some(WaitingOn::StepFailed)),
        current_step: Some(Some("a".into())),
        ..Default::default()
    };
    // A failed run of the step: the workflow run's page shows it, not "Runs to review".
    let step = t
        .store()
        .create_step_run(&wr.id, "a", 1, StepRunStatus::Failed)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let mut failed = NewRun::new(&member.id, TriggerKind::Manual);
    failed.workflow_step_run_id = Some(step.id);
    let failed = t.store().create_run(&failed).await.unwrap();
    t.store()
        .finish_run(&failed.id, RunStatus::Failed, None, Some("down"))
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert!(t.rt.inbox().await.unwrap().workflows.is_empty());
    assert_eq!(t.rt.inbox_count().await.unwrap(), 0);

    t.store()
        .set_workflow_run(&wr.id, &set(WorkflowRunStatus::Waiting))
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let inbox = t.rt.inbox().await.unwrap();
    assert!(inbox.review_runs.is_empty());
    let shown = |inbox: &dots_core::model::Inbox| {
        inbox
            .workflows
            .iter()
            .map(|w| {
                (
                    w.id.clone(),
                    w.team_name.clone(),
                    w.task.clone(),
                    w.waiting_on,
                    w.step_name.clone(),
                    w.origin_dot_id.clone(),
                )
            })
            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
    };
    let item = |origin: Option<&str>| {
        vec![(
            wr.id.clone(),
            "crew".to_string(),
            "Fix the tray icon.".to_string(),
            WaitingOn::StepFailed,
            Some("Work".to_string()),
            origin.map(str::to_string),
        )]
    };
    assert_eq!(shown(&inbox), item(Some(&chatter.id)));
    assert_eq!(t.rt.inbox_count().await.unwrap(), 1);
    assert_eq!(t.counts(&chatter).await, (1, 1));
    assert_eq!(t.counts(&member).await, (0, 0));

    t.rt.delete_dot(&chatter.id).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        t.store()
            .get_workflow_run(&wr.id)
            .await
            .unwrap()
            .origin_run_id,
        None
    );
    assert_eq!(shown(&t.rt.inbox().await.unwrap()), item(None));
    assert_eq!(t.rt.inbox_count().await.unwrap(), 1);
    assert_eq!(t.counts(&member).await, (0, 0));

    // Stopped, it no longer waits.
    t.store()
        .set_workflow_run(&wr.id, &set(WorkflowRunStatus::Cancelled))
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert!(t.rt.inbox().await.unwrap().workflows.is_empty());
    assert_eq!(t.rt.inbox_count().await.unwrap(), 0);
}
```

```rust
impl Fixture {
    /// Puts the workflow run `id` in wait on `on`, at the step `step`.
    async fn wait(&self, id: &str, on: WaitingOn, step: Option<&str>) {
        self.store
            .set_workflow_run(
                id,
                &WorkflowRunChange {
                    status: Some(WorkflowRunStatus::Waiting),
                    waiting_on: Some(Some(on)),
                    current_step: Some(step.map(str::to_string)),
                    ..Default::default()
                },
            )
            .await
            .unwrap();
    }

    /// A run of the fixture's dot for the step run `step_run_id`, with `branch`, ended with
    /// `status`.
    async fn step_run_ended(
        &self,
        step_run_id: &str,
        branch: Option<&str>,
        status: RunStatus,
    ) -> dots_core::model::Run {
        let mut new = NewRun::new(&self.dot, TriggerKind::Manual);
        new.workflow_step_run_id = Some(step_run_id.to_string());
        new.branch = branch.map(str::to_string);
        let run = self.store.create_run(&new).await.unwrap();
        self.store
            .finish_run(&run.id, status, None, None)
            .await
            .unwrap()
    }
}

/// The Inbox lists every workflow run that waits for the person, newest first, with its team,
/// its workflow, its task's first line, what it waits on and at which step, and the dot whose
/// chat started it. A run that runs or has ended is not listed.
#[tokio::test]
async fn the_waiting_workflow_runs_are_listed_for_the_inbox_newest_first() {
    let f = fixture().await;
    let chatter = f
        .store
        .create_dot(&spec("chatter"), None, "default")
        .await
        .unwrap()
        .id;
    let chat = f
        .store
        .create_run(&NewRun::new(&chatter, TriggerKind::Chat))
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let _running = f.started(&f.new).await;
    let check = f.started(&f.new).await;
    f.wait(&check.id, WaitingOn::Check, Some("a")).await;
    let budget = f
        .started(&NewWorkflowRun {
            task: "  Ship the fix.\nThen tell the team.".into(),
            origin_run_id: Some(chat.id.clone()),
            test: true,
            ..f.new.clone()
        })
        .await;
    f.wait(&budget.id, WaitingOn::Budget, None).await;
    let ended = f.started(&f.new).await;
    f.wait(&ended.id, WaitingOn::StepFailed, Some("a")).await;
    f.now(&ended.id, WorkflowRunStatus::Failed).await;

    let item = |wr: &WorkflowRun| WaitingWorkflowRun {
        id: wr.id.clone(),
        team_id: f.new.team_id.clone(),
        team_name: "T".into(),
        workflow_id: Some(f.new.workflow_id.clone()),
        workflow_name: Some("Fix".into()),
        task: "The tray icon is invisible.".into(),
        waiting_on: WaitingOn::Check,
        step_name: Some("Work".into()),
        origin_dot_id: None,
        test: false,
        started_at: wr.started_at.clone(),
    };
    let budget_item = WaitingWorkflowRun {
        task: "Ship the fix.".into(),
        waiting_on: WaitingOn::Budget,
        step_name: None,
        origin_dot_id: Some(chatter.clone()),
        test: true,
        ..item(&budget)
    };
    assert_eq!(
        f.store.waiting_workflow_runs().await.unwrap(),
        vec![budget_item.clone(), item(&check)]
    );
    let value = serde_json::to_value(&budget_item).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(value["waiting_on"], json!("budget"));
    assert_eq!(value["step_name"], json!(null));
    assert_eq!(value["origin_dot_id"], json!(chatter));

    // A deleted workflow and a deleted chat leave the run listed, without them.
    f.store.delete_workflow(&f.new.workflow_id).await.unwrap();
    f.store.delete_dot(&chatter).await.unwrap();
    let listed = f.store.waiting_workflow_runs().await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        listed
            .iter()
            .map(|w| (
                w.id.as_str(),
                w.workflow_id.as_deref(),
                w.workflow_name.as_deref()
            ))
            .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
        vec![
            (budget.id.as_str(), None, None),
            (check.id.as_str(), None, None)
        ]
    );
    assert_eq!(listed[0].origin_dot_id, None);
}

/// A waiting workflow run that a chat started needs the chat's dot, only while it waits; one
/// started another way needs no dot.
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_waiting_workflow_run_needs_the_dot_whose_chat_started_it() {
    let f = fixture().await;
    let other = f
        .store
        .create_dot(&spec("other"), None, "default")
        .await
        .unwrap()
        .id;
    let chat = f.chat().await;
    let started = f
        .started(&NewWorkflowRun {
            origin_run_id: Some(chat),
            ..f.new.clone()
        })
        .await;
    let manual = f.started(&f.new).await;
    f.wait(&manual.id, WaitingOn::Check, Some("a")).await;
    assert_eq!(f.store.dot_counts(&f.dot).await.unwrap(), (0, 0));

    f.wait(&started.id, WaitingOn::Check, Some("a")).await;
    assert_eq!(f.store.dot_counts(&f.dot).await.unwrap(), (1, 1));
    assert_eq!(f.store.dot_counts(&other).await.unwrap(), (0, 0));
    // Approved, it runs again; held by the budget, it waits again; stopped, it has ended.
    f.now(&started.id, WorkflowRunStatus::Running).await;
    assert_eq!(f.store.dot_counts(&f.dot).await.unwrap(), (0, 0));
    f.wait(&started.id, WaitingOn::Budget, Some("a")).await;
    assert_eq!(f.store.dot_counts(&f.dot).await.unwrap(), (1, 1));
    f.now(&started.id, WorkflowRunStatus::Cancelled).await;
    assert_eq!(f.store.dot_counts(&f.dot).await.unwrap(), (0, 0));
}

/// A run of a workflow step is never a run to review, failed or with a branch, and nor is a
/// run that continues it while the step is open; the person's own runs still are. The step's
/// approvals still need its dot.
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_step_s_runs_are_never_runs_to_review() {
    let f = fixture().await;
    let wr = f.started(&f.new).await;
    let step = f
        .store
        .create_step_run(&wr.id, "a", 1, StepRunStatus::Running)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let failed = f.step_run_ended(&step.id, None, RunStatus::Failed).await;
    f.step_run_ended(&step.id, Some("dots/work-1"), RunStatus::Succeeded)
        .await;
    let mut reply = NewRun::new(&f.dot, TriggerKind::Chat);
    reply.parent_run_id = Some(failed.id.clone());
    reply.root_run_id = Some(failed.root_run_id.clone());
    let reply = f.store.create_run(&reply).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(reply.workflow_step_run_id, Some(step.id.clone()));
    f.store
        .finish_run(&reply.id, RunStatus::Failed, None, Some("down"))
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let own = f
        .store
        .create_run(&NewRun::new(&f.dot, TriggerKind::Manual))
        .await
        .unwrap();
    f.store
        .finish_run(&own.id, RunStatus::Failed, None, Some("down"))
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let listed: Vec<String> = f
        .store
        .unreviewed_thread_ends()
        .await
        .unwrap()
        .into_iter()
        .map(|r| r.id)
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(listed, vec![own.id]);

    let mut parked = NewRun::new(&f.dot, TriggerKind::Manual);
    parked.workflow_step_run_id = Some(step.id.clone());
    let parked = f.store.create_run(&parked).await.unwrap();
    f.store
        .create_approval(&parked.id, "Bash", &json!({"command": "ls"}))
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(f.store.dot_counts(&f.dot).await.unwrap(), (1, 1));
}
```

```rust
/// A workflow run that waits for the person, as the Inbox lists it.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct WaitingWorkflowRun {
    /// The workflow run's id.
    pub id: String,
    pub team_id: String,
    pub team_name: String,
    /// `None` once the workflow is deleted: the run goes on with its copy of the definition.
    pub workflow_id: Option<String>,
    pub workflow_name: Option<String>,
    /// The first line of the run's task.
    pub task: String,
    pub waiting_on: WaitingOn,
    /// The step it waits at (the check, the failed step, the step the budget or the send-back
    /// cap holds), by name; `None` before its first step.
    pub step_name: Option<String>,
    /// The dot whose chat started the run; `None` for any other start, or once that chat's run
    /// is deleted.
    pub origin_dot_id: Option<String>,
    pub test: bool,
    pub started_at: String,
}
```

```rust
    /// The workflow runs that wait for the person, as the Inbox lists them, newest first.
    pub async fn waiting_workflow_runs(&self) -> Result<Vec<WaitingWorkflowRun>> {
        let rows = sqlx::query(
            "SELECT w.id, w.team_id, t.name AS team_name, w.workflow_id, f.name AS workflow_name, \
             w.definition, w.task, w.waiting_on, w.current_step, o.dot_id AS origin_dot_id, \
             w.test, w.started_at FROM workflow_runs w JOIN teams t ON t.id = w.team_id \
             LEFT JOIN workflows f ON f.id = w.workflow_id \
             LEFT JOIN runs o ON o.id = w.origin_run_id \
             WHERE w.status = 'waiting' AND w.waiting_on IS NOT NULL \
             ORDER BY w.started_at DESC, w.id DESC",
        )
        .fetch_all(&self.pool)
        .await?;
        rows.iter()
            .map(|row| {
                let definition: Definition =
                    serde_json::from_str(&row.try_get::<String, _>("definition")?)?;
                let current: Option<String> = row.try_get("current_step")?;
                let task: String = row.try_get("task")?;
                Ok(WaitingWorkflowRun {
                    id: row.try_get("id")?,
                    team_id: row.try_get("team_id")?,
                    team_name: row.try_get("team_name")?,
                    workflow_id: row.try_get("workflow_id")?,
                    workflow_name: row.try_get("workflow_name")?,
                    task: task
                        .trim()
                        .lines()
                        .next()
                        .unwrap_or_default()
                        .trim()
                        .to_string(),
                    waiting_on: WaitingOn::parse(&row.try_get::<String, _>("waiting_on")?)?,
                    step_name: current.and_then(|id| definition.step(&id).map(|s| s.name.clone())),
                    origin_dot_id: row.try_get("origin_dot_id")?,
                    test: row.try_get::<i64, _>("test")? != 0,
                    started_at: row.try_get("started_at")?,
                })
            })
            .collect()
    }
```

```ts
export type TeamScopeIssue = z.infer<typeof TeamScopeIssue>;
// What a waiting workflow run waits for: the person's check, more budget, a send-back past the
// cap, or a decision about a failed step (retry or stop).
export const WaitingOn = z.enum(["check", "budget", "send_backs", "step_failed"]);
export type WaitingOn = z.infer<typeof WaitingOn>;
// A workflow run that waits for the person, as the Inbox lists it. The workflow is null once it
// is deleted; the step is null before the first one; the origin dot is the one whose chat
// started the run.
export const WaitingWorkflowRun = z.strictObject({
  id: z.string(),
  team_id: z.string(),
  team_name: z.string(),
  workflow_id: nullable(z.string()),
  workflow_name: nullable(z.string()),
  task: z.string(),
  waiting_on: WaitingOn,
  step_name: nullable(z.string()),
  origin_dot_id: nullable(z.string()),
  test: z.boolean(),
  started_at: z.string(),
});
export type WaitingWorkflowRun = z.infer<typeof WaitingWorkflowRun>;

```

### Task 6: Commands for teams, projects, workflows and workflow runs, and their types in the contract

29 commands in `commands.rs`, one line each over the core, registered in `lib.rs`. `open_project_folder` opens a project's repo or a ready branch's worktree by id, through the new core `workflows/folder.rs`. The contract samples (`contract/teams.rs`) have a `Team`, a `Workflow` of each trigger kind holding every step kind and deploy mode, a `WorkflowRun` in each wait and a finished one, a `CheckView`, `WorkflowProblem`s and the three events. The UI gets their strict schemas (steps recursive through `z.lazy`), 30 wrappers, the query keys and the events' invalidation.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/dots-app/src/contract/teams.rs`, `crates/dots-core/src/workflows/folder.rs`
- Modify: `apps/dots-app/src/{commands.rs,contract.rs,lib.rs}`, `crates/dots-core/src/workflows.rs`, `ui/src/api/{types.ts,commands.ts}`, `ui/src/lib/{query.ts,runtime-events.ts}`, `ui/src/api/__fixtures__/contract.json` (by the command)
- Test: `crates/dots-core/tests/workflows_test.rs`, `ui/src/api/teams.test.ts` (new), `ui/src/api/{commands,contract}.test.ts`, `ui/src/lib/runtime-events.test.ts`, `ui/src/test/fixtures.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 3 to 5 (`check`, `approve_check`, `branch_diff`, `list`, `for_origins`, `check_workflow`, `workflow_webhook_url`, `WaitingOn`); plan A and B's `Teams`, `Workflows::{start, get, send_back_check, add_budget, allow_send_back, retry_step, stop, delete_team}`, `Teams::regenerate_workflow_token`; the app's `CmdError` mapping and `open_run_folder`'s opener.
- Produces: the commands `teams_list() -> Team[]`, `team_get(id)`, `team_create(spec: TeamSpec)`, `team_update(id, spec)`, `team_delete(id)` (`Workflows::delete_team`), `project_add(team_id, spec: ProjectSpec)`, `project_update(id, spec)`, `project_delete(id)`, `open_project_folder(project_id: Option<String>, workflow_run_id: Option<String>, branch: Option<String>)` (exactly `(Some, None, None)` or `(None, Some, Some)`, else `invalid` `name a project, or a workflow run and its branch`), `workflows_list(team_id)`, `workflow_get(id)`, `workflow_create(team_id, spec: WorkflowSpec)`, `workflow_update(id, spec)`, `workflow_delete(id)`, `workflow_check_spec(team_id, spec) -> Option<WorkflowProblem>`, `workflow_webhook_url(id) -> String`, `regenerate_workflow_token(id) -> String`, `workflow_runs_list(filter: WorkflowRunFilter)`, `workflow_runs_for_origins(run_ids: Vec<String>)`, `workflow_run_get(id)`, `workflow_run_start(workflow_id, project_id, task, test: bool)` (trigger `manual`, no origin), `workflow_check(id) -> CheckView`, `workflow_branch_diff(id, branch, commit) -> WorktreeDiff`, `workflow_approve(id, note: Option<String>, tips: Vec<CheckTip>)`, `workflow_send_back(id, note)`, `workflow_add_budget(id)`, `workflow_allow_send_back(id)`, `workflow_retry_step(id)`, `workflow_stop(id)`; core `Workflows::{project_folder(&self, project_id: &str) -> Result<PathBuf>, branch_folder(&self, id: &str, branch: &str) -> Result<PathBuf>}` (`NotFound("project <id>")`, `NotFound("folder <path> no longer exists")`, `Invalid("workflow run <id> has no ready branch <branch>")`); `contract::teams::teams_samples(&mut Value)` (`pub(super)`); TS schemas (each const with a same-named type) `MemberSpec`, `TeamSpec`, `TeamMember`, `ProjectSpec`, `Project`, `Team`, enums `WorkflowTrigger`, `WorkflowRunStatus`, `StepRunStatus`, `MergeMethod`, `BranchOn`, `SentBy`, `Trigger` (on `kind`), `Delegate`, `AgentStep`, `CheckStep`, `DeployStep` (on `mode`), `BranchStep`, `BranchPath`, `Step` (`z.union`, typed by hand as `z.ZodType`), type `DeployMode`, `Budget`, `Definition`, `WorkflowSpec`, `LastRun`, `Workflow`, `WorkflowProblem`, `WorkflowRunFilter`, `BranchRef`, `MemberHandoff`, `Handoff`, `CheckTip`, `StepDecision` (`{decision: "approved", note, branches?}`, `{decision: "sent_back", note}`, `{path}`), `DeployedBranch`, `DeployResult` (`error` optional), `StepRun`, `SendBack`, `WorkflowRun`, `ShownBranch`, `CheckView`, and the `RuntimeEvent` variants `team_updated {team_id}`, `workflow_updated {team_id, workflow_id}`, `workflow_run_updated {run}`; TS wrappers in camelCase (Tauri maps top-level camelCase arguments; fields inside a spec or filter stay snake_case), `workflowRunsList(filter: Partial<WorkflowRunFilter>)`, `openProjectFolder(projectId)`, `openBranchFolder(workflowRunId, branch)`; the keys of Global Constraints (all but `workflowDotRuns`), lists invalidated by prefix (`["teams"]`, `["workflow-runs"]`); `team_updated` also marks `keys.inbox`, `workflow_run_updated` also `keys.inbox` and `keys.overview`; `fixtures.ts` `sample.{teams, workflows, workflowRuns, check, workflowProblems}` and `installDefaults()` answering `teams_list`, `workflow_runs_list`, `workflow_runs_for_origins` with `[]` and `workflow_webhook_url` with `http://127.0.0.1:47321/workflows/<id>/trigger`.


**with:**

```rust
#[tokio::test]
async fn open_folder_finds_a_project_s_repo_and_a_ready_branch_s_worktree_by_id_only() {
    let w = world().await;
    w.shipping_to_origin("ok");
    let flow = w.ship_flow(DeployMode::Push).await;
    let no_ready =
        |id: &str, branch: &str| format!("workflow run {id} has no ready branch {branch}");

    assert_eq!(
        w.workflows.project_folder(&w.project).await.unwrap(),
        w.repo
    );
    assert!(matches!(
        w.workflows.project_folder("nope").await,
        Err(Error::NotFound(m)) if m == "project nope"
    ));

    // A test run's deploy lists the branch as ready; until then, nothing is.
    let (wr, branch, _) = w.at_the_check(&flow, true).await;
    assert!(matches!(
        w.workflows.branch_folder(&wr.id, &branch).await,
        Err(Error::Invalid(m)) if m == no_ready(&wr.id, &branch)
    ));
    w.approve(&wr.id, None).await.unwrap();
    let wr = w
        .until(&wr.id, |wr| wr.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded)
        .await;
    let worktree =
        PathBuf::from(&newest(&wr, "dev").handoff.as_ref().unwrap().branches[0].worktree_path);
    assert!(worktree.is_dir());
    assert_eq!(
        w.workflows.branch_folder(&wr.id, &branch).await.unwrap(),
        worktree
    );
    // Only a branch the run lists as ready: never another ref or a path.
    for other in ["main", "HEAD", "..", worktree.to_str().unwrap()] {
        assert!(matches!(
            w.workflows.branch_folder(&wr.id, other).await,
            Err(Error::Invalid(m)) if m == no_ready(&wr.id, other)
        ));
    }

    // A pushed branch is not ready: its folder is not offered.
    let (pushed, pushed_branch, _) = w.at_the_check(&flow, false).await;
    w.approve(&pushed.id, None).await.unwrap();
    let pushed = w
        .until(&pushed.id, |wr| wr.status == WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded)
        .await;
    assert!(
        !newest(&pushed, "ship")
            .deploy_result
            .as_ref()
            .unwrap()
            .ready
    );
    assert!(matches!(
        w.workflows.branch_folder(&pushed.id, &pushed_branch).await,
        Err(Error::Invalid(m)) if m == no_ready(&pushed.id, &pushed_branch)
    ));

    // A folder that is gone is said to be gone.
    std::fs::remove_dir_all(&worktree).unwrap();
    assert!(matches!(
        w.workflows.branch_folder(&wr.id, &branch).await,
        Err(Error::NotFound(m)) if m == format!("folder {} no longer exists", worktree.display())
    ));
}
```

**Create `ui/src/api/teams.test.ts`:**

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import fixture from "./__fixtures__/contract.json";
import * as T from "./types";

/// Every step of `steps`, those inside branches included, in order.
function all(steps: T.Step[]): T.Step[] {
  return steps.flatMap((s) =>
    s.kind === "branch"
      ? [s, ...all(s.paths.flatMap((p) => p.steps)), ...all(s.otherwise)]
      : [s],
  );
}

const sorted = (values: Iterable<string>) => [...new Set(values)].sort();

const agent = (id: string): T.Step => ({
  id,
  name: "Work",
  kind: "agent",
  member: "01J0DOT",
  job: "Do the task.",
  can_send_back_to: [],
  outcomes: [],
  delegate: null,
});

/// A branch on outcome whose "fixed" path holds a branch on project: two levels of paths.
const nested: T.Step = {
  id: "outer",
  name: "Fixed?",
  kind: "branch",
  on: "outcome",
  paths: [
    {
      label: "fixed",
      steps: [
        {
          id: "inner",
          name: "Which project",
          kind: "branch",
          on: "project",
          paths: [{ label: "Dots", steps: [agent("dots")] }],
          otherwise: [
            { id: "ship", name: "Ship", kind: "deploy", mode: "pr_merge", merge_method: "rebase" },
          ],
        },
      ],
    },
  ],
  otherwise: [agent("design")],
};

describe("the teams and workflows schemas", () => {
  it("has a workflow sample of every step kind, deploy mode and trigger kind", () => {
    const workflows = T.Workflow.array().parse(fixture.Workflow);
    const steps = workflows.flatMap((w) => all(w.definition.steps));
    expect(sorted(steps.map((s) => s.kind))).toEqual(["agent", "branch", "check", "deploy"]);
    const modes = T.DeployStep.options.map((o) => o.shape.mode.value);
    expect(sorted(steps.flatMap((s) => (s.kind === "deploy" ? [s.mode] : [])))).toEqual(
      sorted(modes),
    );
    const triggers = T.Trigger.options.map((o) => o.shape.kind.value);
    expect(sorted(workflows.map((w) => w.definition.trigger.kind))).toEqual(sorted(triggers));
    // A delegate, a check that names where it sends back and one that does not, and a branch
    // with an otherwise and one without.
    expect(steps.some((s) => s.kind === "agent" && s.delegate !== null)).toBe(true);
    const checks = steps.flatMap((s) => (s.kind === "check" ? [s.send_back_to] : []));
    expect(checks).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(["build", null]));
    const branches = steps.flatMap((s) => (s.kind === "branch" ? [s] : []));
    expect(branches.map((b) => b.otherwise.length > 0).sort()).toEqual([false, true]);
    // The workflow the builder edits is the spec it saves: the same fields, flattened.
    expect(T.WorkflowSpec.array().parse(fixture.WorkflowSpec)).toEqual(
      workflows.map((w) => ({ name: w.name, definition: w.definition, enabled: w.enabled })),
    );
  });

  it("has a workflow run in each wait, with members, decisions, a deploy and a held send-back", () => {
    const runs = T.WorkflowRun.array().parse(fixture.WorkflowRun);
    expect(sorted(runs.flatMap((r) => (r.waiting_on ? [r.waiting_on] : [])))).toEqual(
      sorted(T.WaitingOn.options),
    );
    const steps = runs.flatMap((r) => r.steps);
    expect(steps.some((s) => (s.handoff?.members.length ?? 0) > 0)).toBe(true);
    expect(steps.some((s) => s.handoff?.members.some((m) => m.branch === null))).toBe(true);
    const decisions = steps.flatMap((s) => (s.decision ? [s.decision] : []));
    expect(decisions.map((d) => ("decision" in d ? d.decision : "path")).sort()).toEqual(
      expect.arrayContaining(["approved", "path", "sent_back"]),
    );
    const results = steps.flatMap((s) => (s.deploy_result ? [s.deploy_result] : []));
    expect(results.some((d) => d.ready)).toBe(true);
    expect(results.some((d) => d.error !== undefined)).toBe(true);
    expect(results.some((d) => d.branches.some((b) => b.pr_url !== null && b.auto_merge))).toBe(
      true,
    );
    expect(runs.some((r) => r.send_back?.held === true)).toBe(true);
    expect(steps.some((s) => s.send_back !== null)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("reads a branch step inside a branch's path, and refuses an unknown field at any depth", () => {
    expect(T.Step.parse(nested)).toEqual(nested);
    expect(all([T.Step.parse(nested)]).map((s) => s.id)).toEqual([
      "outer",
      "inner",
      "dots",
      "ship",
      "design",
    ]);
    const deep = structuredClone(nested) as Extract<T.Step, { kind: "branch" }>;
    const inner = deep.paths[0].steps[0] as Extract<T.Step, { kind: "branch" }>;
    Object.assign(inner.paths[0].steps[0], { extra: 1 });
    expect(T.Step.safeParse(deep).success).toBe(false);
  });

  it("refuses a step of a kind, a deploy of a mode or a trigger it does not know", () => {
    expect(T.Step.safeParse({ ...agent("a"), kind: "wait" }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(T.Step.safeParse({ id: "s", name: "Ship", kind: "deploy", mode: "tag" }).success).toBe(
      false,
    );
    // A mode's own fields belong to it alone.
    const push = { id: "s", name: "Ship", kind: "deploy", mode: "push" };
    expect(T.Step.safeParse({ ...push, merge_method: "squash" }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(T.Trigger.safeParse({ kind: "email" }).success).toBe(false);
  });

  it("reads a deploy that succeeded without an error, and an approval kept before its tips", () => {
    expect(T.DeployResult.parse({ branches: [], ready: true })).toEqual({
      branches: [],
      ready: true,
    });
    expect(T.StepDecision.parse({ decision: "approved", note: null })).toEqual({
      decision: "approved",
      note: null,
    });
    expect(T.StepDecision.safeParse({ decision: "approved", note: null, by: "x" }).success).toBe(
      false,
    );
  });

  it("knows what the builder's live check and the check page answer", () => {
    const problems = T.WorkflowProblem.array().parse(fixture.WorkflowProblem);
    expect(problems.map((p) => p.step_id)).toEqual(["work", null]);
    const view = T.CheckView.parse(fixture.CheckView);
    // Approve sends back the branches and commits the page showed.
    expect(T.CheckTip.array().parse(fixture.CheckTip)).toEqual(
      view.branches.map(({ branch, commit }) => ({ branch, commit })),
    );
  });
});
```

```ts
  it("marks the teams, the team's drafts' checks and the inbox stale on a team update", () => {
    const qc = createQueryClient();
    const [team] = sample.teams;
    const [{ name, definition, enabled }] = sample.workflows;
    const draft = { name, definition, enabled };
    // A change of members can change a draft's problem; the Inbox lists members outside the
    // team's account or workspace.
    const lists = [
      keys.teams,
      keys.team(team.id),
      keys.workflowProblem(team.id, draft),
      keys.inbox,
    ];
    const untouched = [
      keys.workflowProblem("another-team", draft),
      keys.workflows(team.id),
      keys.workflow(sample.workflows[0].id),
      keys.overview,
    ];
    for (const key of [...lists, ...untouched]) qc.setQueryData(key, []);

    applyRuntimeEvent(qc, { type: "team_updated", team_id: team.id });
    for (const key of lists) expect(stale(qc, key), key.join("/")).toBe(true);
    for (const key of untouched) expect(stale(qc, key), key.join("/")).toBe(false);
  });

  it("marks the team's workflows and the workflow stale on a workflow update", () => {
    const qc = createQueryClient();
    const [workflow] = sample.workflows;
    const lists = [keys.workflows(workflow.team_id), keys.workflow(workflow.id)];
    const untouched = [
      keys.workflows("another-team"),
      keys.workflow("another-workflow"),
      keys.teams,
      keys.workflowRuns({ team_id: workflow.team_id }),
    ];
    for (const key of [...lists, ...untouched]) qc.setQueryData(key, []);

    applyRuntimeEvent(qc, {
      type: "workflow_updated",
      team_id: workflow.team_id,
      workflow_id: workflow.id,
    });
    for (const key of lists) expect(stale(qc, key), key.join("/")).toBe(true);
    for (const key of untouched) expect(stale(qc, key), key.join("/")).toBe(false);
  });

  it("marks the run, every run list, its workflow and what needs you stale on a run update", () => {
    const qc = createQueryClient();
    const [run] = sample.workflowRuns;
    const [tip] = sample.check.branches;
    const lists = [
      keys.workflowRun(run.id),
      keys.workflowCheck(run.id),
      keys.workflowBranchDiff(run.id, tip.branch, tip.commit ?? ""),
      keys.workflowRuns({ team_id: run.team_id }),
      keys.workflowRuns({ workflow_id: "another-workflow" }),
      keys.workflowRunsForOrigins([run.origin_run_id ?? ""]),
      // Its runs in flight and last run: no workflow event says they changed.
      keys.workflows(run.team_id),
      keys.workflow(run.workflow_id ?? ""),
      // A waiting run is in the Inbox and needs the dot whose chat started it.
      keys.inbox,
      keys.overview,
      keys.unreadTotal,
    ];
    const untouched = [
      keys.workflowRun("another-run"),
      keys.workflows("another-team"),
      keys.workflow("another-workflow"),
      keys.teams,
      keys.runs(sample.dot.id),
    ];
    for (const key of [...lists, ...untouched]) qc.setQueryData(key, []);

    applyRuntimeEvent(qc, { type: "workflow_run_updated", run });
    for (const key of lists) expect(stale(qc, key), key.join("/")).toBe(true);
    for (const key of untouched) expect(stale(qc, key), key.join("/")).toBe(false);
  });

  it("refreshes a run whose workflow was deleted without touching any workflow", () => {
    const qc = createQueryClient();
    const run = sample.workflowRuns.find((r) => r.workflow_id === null)!;
    const workflow = keys.workflow(sample.workflows[0].id);
    for (const key of [keys.workflowRun(run.id), keys.workflows(run.team_id), workflow]) {
      qc.setQueryData(key, []);
    }

    applyRuntimeEvent(qc, { type: "workflow_run_updated", run });
    expect([
      stale(qc, keys.workflowRun(run.id)),
      stale(qc, keys.workflows(run.team_id)),
      stale(qc, workflow),
    ]).toEqual([true, true, false]);
  });
});

```

```rust
/// Every team with its members and projects (teams spec section 10.1).
#[tauri::command]
pub async fn teams_list(state: State<'_, AppState>) -> Res<Vec<Team>> {
    Ok(state.rt()?.teams().list().await?)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn team_get(state: State<'_, AppState>, id: String) -> Res<Team> {
    Ok(state.rt()?.teams().get(&id).await?)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn team_create(state: State<'_, AppState>, spec: TeamSpec) -> Res<Team> {
    Ok(state.rt()?.teams().create(&spec).await?)
}

/// Its name, description and members; its account and workspace stay as they are.
#[tauri::command]
pub async fn team_update(state: State<'_, AppState>, id: String, spec: TeamSpec) -> Res<Team> {
    Ok(state.rt()?.teams().update(&id, &spec).await?)
}

/// Stops the team's runs in flight, then deletes it with its projects and workflows.
#[tauri::command]
pub async fn team_delete(state: State<'_, AppState>, id: String) -> Res<()> {
    Ok(state.rt()?.workflows().delete_team(&id).await?)
}

/// Adds a project to the team; the core checks that its folder is a git repo.
#[tauri::command]
pub async fn project_add(
    state: State<'_, AppState>,
    team_id: String,
    spec: ProjectSpec,
) -> Res<Project> {
    Ok(state.rt()?.teams().add_project(&team_id, &spec).await?)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn project_update(
    state: State<'_, AppState>,
    id: String,
    spec: ProjectSpec,
) -> Res<Project> {
    Ok(state.rt()?.teams().update_project(&id, &spec).await?)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn project_delete(state: State<'_, AppState>, id: String) -> Res<()> {
    Ok(state.rt()?.teams().delete_project(&id).await?)
}

/// Opens a folder in Explorer: the project `project_id`'s repo, or, with `workflow_run_id` and
/// `branch`, the worktree of a branch that run's deploy listed as ready. The path comes from
/// the core's records, never the UI.
#[tauri::command]
pub async fn open_project_folder(
    app: AppHandle,
    state: State<'_, AppState>,
    project_id: Option<String>,
    workflow_run_id: Option<String>,
    branch: Option<String>,
) -> Res<()> {
    let rt = state.rt()?;
    let path = match (project_id, workflow_run_id, branch) {
        (Some(project), None, None) => rt.workflows().project_folder(&project).await?,
        (None, Some(run), Some(branch)) => rt.workflows().branch_folder(&run, &branch).await?,
        _ => {
            return Err(CmdError::from(dots_core::Error::Invalid(
                "name a project, or a workflow run and its branch".into(),
            )))
        }
    };
    app.opener()
        .open_path(path.to_string_lossy(), None::<&str>)
        .map_err(|e| CmdError::internal(format!("could not open {}: {e}", path.display())))
}

/// Raises each limit the waiting run reached by its default amount, once.

pub async fn workflow_add_budget(state: State<'_, AppState>, id: String) -> Res<WorkflowRun> {
    Ok(state.rt()?.workflows().add_budget(&id).await?)
}

/// Lets the send-back held at the cap go on: one more.
#[tauri::command]
pub async fn workflow_allow_send_back(state: State<'_, AppState>, id: String) -> Res<WorkflowRun> {
    Ok(state.rt()?.workflows().allow_send_back(&id).await?)
}

/// Runs the failed step the workflow run waits on again.
#[tauri::command]
pub async fn workflow_retry_step(state: State<'_, AppState>, id: String) -> Res<WorkflowRun> {
    Ok(state.rt()?.workflows().retry_step(&id).await?)
}

/// Stops the workflow run and its dot runs.
#[tauri::command]
pub async fn workflow_stop(state: State<'_, AppState>, id: String) -> Res<WorkflowRun> {
    Ok(state.rt()?.workflows().stop(&id).await?)
}
```
