**Create `apps/dots-app/src/contract/teams.rs`:**

```rust
//! The samples of teams and workflows (teams spec sections 3 to 11): teams with their members
//! and projects, workflows with every step kind, deploy mode and trigger kind, workflow runs in
//! each wait with their step runs, hand-offs, decisions and deploy results, a check, the
//! builder's problems, and their events.

use dots_core::deploy::{DeployResult, DeployedBranch};
use dots_core::events::RuntimeEvent;
use dots_core::model::*;
use dots_core::schedule::Schedule;
use dots_core::workflow::{
    BranchOn, BranchPath, Budget, Definition, Delegate, DeployMode, MergeMethod, Step, StepKind,
    Trigger, WorkflowProblem,
};
use dots_core::workflows::{CheckView, ShownBranch};
use serde_json::{json, Value};

use super::{to, TS};

const TEAM: &str = "01J0TEAM";
const DEV: &str = "01J0DOT";
const REVIEWER: &str = "01J0DOT2";
const PROJECT: &str = "01J0PRJ";
const REPO: &str = "C:/Users/u/src/dots";
const TASK: &str = "The tray icon is invisible on a light taskbar.\nIt shows on a dark one.";

fn project_spec() -> ProjectSpec {
    ProjectSpec {
        name: "Dots".into(),
        repo_path: REPO.into(),
        default_branch: Some("main".into()),
        remote: "origin".into(),
    }
}

fn project() -> Project {
    Project {
        id: PROJECT.into(),
        team_id: TEAM.into(),
        created_at: TS.into(),
        updated_at: TS.into(),
        spec: project_spec(),
    }
}

fn team_spec() -> TeamSpec {
    TeamSpec {
        name: "crew".into(),
        description: "Fixes and ships Dots.".into(),
        members: vec![
            MemberSpec {
                dot_id: DEV.into(),
                role: "dev agent".into(),
            },
            MemberSpec {
                dot_id: REVIEWER.into(),
                role: String::new(),
            },
        ],
        org_id: None,
        workspace_id: "default".into(),
    }
}

/// A Personal team with two members and two projects (one finds its default branch), and an
/// org's team with none.
fn teams() -> Vec<Team> {
    vec![
        Team {
            id: TEAM.into(),
            name: "crew".into(),
            description: "Fixes and ships Dots.".into(),
            members: vec![
                TeamMember {
                    dot_id: DEV.into(),
                    dot_name: "nightly-tests".into(),
                    role: "dev agent".into(),
                },
                TeamMember {
                    dot_id: REVIEWER.into(),
                    dot_name: "reviewer".into(),
                    role: String::new(),
                },
            ],
            projects: vec![
                project(),
                Project {
                    id: "01J0PRJ2".into(),
                    spec: ProjectSpec {
                        name: "Site".into(),
                        repo_path: "C:/Users/u/src/site".into(),
                        default_branch: None,
                        remote: "upstream".into(),
                    },
                    ..project()
                },
            ],
            org_id: None,
            workspace_id: "default".into(),
            created_at: TS.into(),
            updated_at: TS.into(),
        },
        Team {
            id: "01J0TEAM2".into(),
            name: "platform".into(),
            description: String::new(),
            members: vec![],
            projects: vec![],
            org_id: Some("acme-eng".into()),
            workspace_id: "platform".into(),
            created_at: TS.into(),
            updated_at: TS.into(),
        },
    ]
}

fn agent(id: &str, name: &str, member: &str, job: &str) -> Step {
    Step {
        id: id.into(),
        name: name.into(),
        kind: StepKind::Agent {
            member: member.into(),
            job: job.into(),
            can_send_back_to: vec![],
            outcomes: vec![],
            delegate: None,
        },
    }
}

fn check(id: &str, send_back_to: Option<&str>) -> Step {
    Step {
        id: id.into(),
        name: "Review".into(),
        kind: StepKind::Check {
            send_back_to: send_back_to.map(Into::into),
        },
    }
}

fn deploy(id: &str, mode: DeployMode) -> Step {
    Step {
        id: id.into(),
        name: "Ship".into(),
        kind: StepKind::Deploy { mode },
    }
}

/// A lead that may start members and reports an outcome, then a branch on it: "fixed" goes
/// to a check and a pull request that merges itself, anything else to a designer who may send
/// the work back to the lead.
fn chat_definition() -> Definition {
    Definition {
        trigger: Trigger::Chat {
            starters: vec![DEV.into()],
        },
        steps: vec![
            Step {
                id: "build".into(),
                name: "Build".into(),
                kind: StepKind::Agent {
                    member: DEV.into(),
                    job: "Fix the bug and say whether it is fixed.".into(),
                    can_send_back_to: vec![],
                    outcomes: vec!["fixed".into(), "needs_design".into()],
                    delegate: Some(Delegate {
                        may_start: vec![REVIEWER.into()],
                        at_once: 2,
                        total: 4,
                    }),
                },
            },
            Step {
                id: "route".into(),
                name: "Fixed?".into(),
                kind: StepKind::Branch {
                    on: BranchOn::Outcome,
                    paths: vec![BranchPath {
                        label: "fixed".into(),
                        steps: vec![
                            check("review", Some("build")),
                            deploy(
                                "ship",
                                DeployMode::PrMerge {
                                    merge_method: MergeMethod::Squash,
                                },
                            ),
                        ],
                    }],
                    otherwise: vec![Step {
                        id: "design".into(),
                        name: "Design".into(),
                        kind: StepKind::Agent {
                            member: REVIEWER.into(),
                            job: "Propose a design.".into(),
                            can_send_back_to: vec!["build".into()],
                            outcomes: vec![],
                            delegate: None,
                        },
                    }],
                },
            },
        ],
        budget: Budget::default(),
    }
}

fn workflow(id: &str, name: &str, definition: Definition) -> Workflow {
    Workflow {
        id: id.into(),
        team_id: TEAM.into(),
        created_at: TS.into(),
        updated_at: TS.into(),
        problem: None,
        has_webhook_token: false,
        runs_in_flight: 0,
        last_run: None,
        spec: WorkflowSpec {
            name: name.into(),
            definition,
            enabled: true,
        },
    }
}

/// One workflow per trigger kind; between them every step kind and deploy mode, a branch on
/// outcome with an otherwise and one on project without, and a delegate.
fn workflows() -> Vec<Workflow> {
    let work = || agent("work", "Work", DEV, "Do the task.");
    let schedule: Schedule = serde_json::from_value(
        json!({"rule": {"kind": "daily", "time": "09:00"}, "catch_up": false, "until": null}),
    )
    .expect("sample schedule");
    vec![
        Workflow {
            runs_in_flight: 1,
            last_run: Some(LastRun {
                id: "01J0WFR".into(),
                status: WorkflowRunStatus::Waiting,
                started_at: TS.into(),
                test: false,
            }),
            ..workflow("01J0WFL", "Fix and ship", chat_definition())
        },
        Workflow {
            problem: Some("step \"Work\": 01J0GONE is not a member of the team".into()),
            last_run: Some(LastRun {
                id: "01J0WFR4".into(),
                status: WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded,
                started_at: TS.into(),
                test: true,
            }),
            spec: WorkflowSpec {
                name: "Push it".into(),
                definition: Definition {
                    trigger: Trigger::Manual,
                    steps: vec![
                        agent("work", "Work", "01J0GONE", "Do the task."),
                        check("check", None),
                        deploy("push", DeployMode::Push),
                    ],
                    budget: Budget {
                        max_runs: 4,
                        max_tokens: 200_000,
                        max_minutes: 30,
                        max_send_backs: 1,
                    },
                },
                enabled: false,
            },
            ..workflow("01J0WFL2", "", chat_definition())
        },
        workflow(
            "01J0WFL3",
            "Nightly",
            Definition {
                trigger: Trigger::Schedule {
                    schedule,
                    task: "Update the dependencies.".into(),
                    project: "Dots".into(),
                },
                steps: vec![
                    work(),
                    check("check", None),
                    Step {
                        id: "by-project".into(),
                        name: "Which project".into(),
                        kind: StepKind::Branch {
                            on: BranchOn::Project,
                            paths: vec![
                                BranchPath {
                                    label: "Dots".into(),
                                    steps: vec![deploy("pr", DeployMode::Pr)],
                                },
                                BranchPath {
                                    label: "Site".into(),
                                    steps: vec![deploy("keep", DeployMode::Branch)],
                                },
                            ],
                            otherwise: vec![],
                        },
                    },
                ],
                budget: Budget::default(),
            },
        ),
        Workflow {
            has_webhook_token: true,
            ..workflow(
                "01J0WFL4",
                "On issue",
                Definition {
                    trigger: Trigger::Webhook {
                        task: Some("Look at the newest issue.".into()),
                        project: None,
                    },
                    steps: vec![
                        work(),
                        check("check", None),
                        deploy(
                            "release",
                            DeployMode::Member {
                                member: REVIEWER.into(),
                                job: "Release what was approved.".into(),
                            },
                        ),
                    ],
                    budget: Budget::default(),
                },
            )
        },
    ]
}

fn branch_ref(run_id: &str, branch: &str) -> BranchRef {
    BranchRef {
        run_id: run_id.into(),
        branch: branch.into(),
        worktree_path: format!("C:/Users/u/AppData/Local/dots/worktrees/{run_id}"),
        base_commit: Some("1111111111111111111111111111111111111111".into()),
    }
}

fn step_run(id: &str, step_id: &str, status: StepRunStatus) -> StepRun {
    StepRun {
        id: id.into(),
        workflow_run_id: "01J0WFR".into(),
        step_id: step_id.into(),
        attempt: 1,
        status,
        handoff: None,
        decision: None,
        error: None,
        outcome: None,
        notice: None,
        send_back: None,
        deploy_result: None,
        started_at: TS.into(),
        ended_at: status.is_terminal().then(|| TS.into()),
    }
}

/// The lead's hand-off: its own branch and the member branch it did not merge, with what each
/// member it started handed back.
fn lead_handoff() -> Handoff {
    Handoff {
        summary: "Made the icon dark on a light taskbar.".into(),
        branches: vec![
            branch_ref("01J0RUN5", "dots/nightly-tests/01J0RUN5"),
            branch_ref("01J0RUN6", "dots/reviewer/01J0RUN6"),
        ],
        outcome: Some("fixed".into()),
        members: vec![
            MemberHandoff {
                dot_id: REVIEWER.into(),
                task: "Check the icon on Windows 10.".into(),
                status: RunStatus::Succeeded,
                summary: "It shows on both.".into(),
                branch: Some("dots/reviewer/01J0RUN6".into()),
            },
            MemberHandoff {
                dot_id: REVIEWER.into(),
                task: "Check the high-contrast theme.".into(),
                status: RunStatus::Failed,
                summary: "No high-contrast theme on this PC.".into(),
                branch: None,
            },
        ],
    }
}

fn workflow_run(id: &str) -> WorkflowRun {
    WorkflowRun {
        id: id.into(),
        workflow_id: Some("01J0WFL".into()),
        team_id: TEAM.into(),
        definition: chat_definition(),
        project_id: Some(PROJECT.into()),
        project: project_spec(),
        task: TASK.into(),
        trigger: WorkflowTrigger::Chat,
        origin_run_id: Some("01J0RUN".into()),
        test: false,
        status: WorkflowRunStatus::Running,
        waiting_on: None,
        current_step: Some("build".into()),
        budget: Budget::default(),
        send_backs: 0,
        send_back: None,
        spent_runs: 3,
        spent_tokens: 120_000,
        spent_cache_read: 900_000,
        active_ms: 300_000,
        error: None,
        started_at: TS.into(),
        ended_at: None,
        steps: vec![],
    }
}

/// Waiting on a check, after a lead with members and a branch on its outcome; one that went
/// back once and was approved and shipped; one holding a send-back at the cap; a test run whose
/// deploy failed part way; one started from the team page and held by the budget, whose workflow
/// and project were deleted since.
fn workflow_runs() -> Vec<WorkflowRun> {
    let lead = StepRun {
        handoff: Some(lead_handoff()),
        outcome: Some("fixed".into()),
        ..step_run("01J0STP1", "build", StepRunStatus::Succeeded)
    };
    let route = StepRun {
        decision: Some(json!({"path": "fixed"})),
        ..step_run("01J0STP2", "route", StepRunStatus::Succeeded)
    };
    let tips = json!([
        {"branch": "dots/nightly-tests/01J0RUN5", "commit": "2222222222222222222222222222222222222222"},
        {"branch": "dots/reviewer/01J0RUN6", "commit": null},
    ]);
    let pushed = DeployedBranch {
        branch: "dots/nightly-tests/01J0RUN5".into(),
        commit: "2222222222222222222222222222222222222222".into(),
        pushed: true,
        pr_url: Some("https://github.com/Aakshar-D/dots/pull/40".into()),
        auto_merge: true,
    };
    vec![
        WorkflowRun {
            status: WorkflowRunStatus::Waiting,
            waiting_on: Some(WaitingOn::Check),
            current_step: Some("review".into()),
            steps: vec![
                lead.clone(),
                route.clone(),
                step_run("01J0STP3", "review", StepRunStatus::Waiting),
            ],
            ..workflow_run("01J0WFR")
        },
        WorkflowRun {
            status: WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded,
            current_step: None,
            send_backs: 1,
            send_back: Some(SendBack {
                from: "review".into(),
                to: "build".into(),
                note: "Keep the old icon for dark mode.".into(),
                by: SentBy::Person,
                held: false,
            }),
            ended_at: Some(TS.into()),
            steps: vec![
                lead.clone(),
                route.clone(),
                StepRun {
                    decision: Some(json!({
                        "decision": "sent_back",
                        "note": "Keep the old icon for dark mode.",
                    })),
                    ..step_run("01J0STP3", "review", StepRunStatus::Succeeded)
                },
                StepRun {
                    id: "01J0STP9".into(),
                    attempt: 2,
                    notice: Some("Its earlier chat could not go on, so it started fresh.".into()),
                    ..lead.clone()
                },
                StepRun {
                    id: "01J0STPA".into(),
                    ..route.clone()
                },
                StepRun {
                    attempt: 2,
                    handoff: Some(lead_handoff()),
                    decision: Some(json!({"decision": "approved", "note": null, "branches": tips})),
                    ..step_run("01J0STP4", "review", StepRunStatus::Succeeded)
                },
                StepRun {
                    deploy_result: Some(DeployResult {
                        branches: vec![pushed.clone()],
                        ready: false,
                        error: None,
                    }),
                    ..step_run("01J0STP5", "ship", StepRunStatus::Succeeded)
                },
            ],
            ..workflow_run("01J0WFR2")
        },
        WorkflowRun {
            status: WorkflowRunStatus::Waiting,
            waiting_on: Some(WaitingOn::SendBacks),
            current_step: Some("design".into()),
            send_backs: 3,
            send_back: Some(SendBack {
                from: "design".into(),
                to: "build".into(),
                note: "The icon needs a new shape first.".into(),
                by: SentBy::Agent,
                held: true,
            }),
            steps: vec![StepRun {
                outcome: None,
                send_back: Some(
                    json!({"to": "build", "note": "The icon needs a new shape first."}),
                ),
                ..step_run("01J0STP6", "design", StepRunStatus::Waiting)
            }],
            ..workflow_run("01J0WFR3")
        },
        WorkflowRun {
            test: true,
            trigger: WorkflowTrigger::Manual,
            origin_run_id: None,
            status: WorkflowRunStatus::Waiting,
            waiting_on: Some(WaitingOn::StepFailed),
            current_step: Some("ship".into()),
            error: Some("Ship: gh: could not create the pull request".into()),
            steps: vec![StepRun {
                error: Some("gh: could not create the pull request".into()),
                deploy_result: Some(DeployResult {
                    branches: vec![
                        DeployedBranch {
                            pr_url: None,
                            auto_merge: false,
                            ..pushed.clone()
                        },
                        DeployedBranch {
                            branch: "dots/reviewer/01J0RUN6".into(),
                            commit: "3333333333333333333333333333333333333333".into(),
                            pushed: false,
                            pr_url: None,
                            auto_merge: false,
                        },
                    ],
                    ready: false,
                    error: Some("gh: could not create the pull request".into()),
                }),
                ..step_run("01J0STP7", "ship", StepRunStatus::Failed)
            }],
            ..workflow_run("01J0WFR4")
        },
        // Nightly's run on Site, which took the "Site" path to keep its branch.
        WorkflowRun {
            workflow_id: None,
            definition: workflows()[2].spec.definition.clone(),
            project_id: None,
            project: teams()[0].projects[1].spec.clone(),
            trigger: WorkflowTrigger::Manual,
            origin_run_id: None,
            status: WorkflowRunStatus::Waiting,
            waiting_on: Some(WaitingOn::Budget),
            current_step: Some("keep".into()),
            budget: Budget {
                max_runs: 3,
                ..Budget::default()
            },
            steps: vec![StepRun {
                deploy_result: Some(DeployResult {
                    branches: vec![DeployedBranch {
                        pushed: false,
                        pr_url: None,
                        auto_merge: false,
                        ..pushed
                    }],
                    ready: true,
                    error: None,
                }),
                ..step_run("01J0STP8", "keep", StepRunStatus::Succeeded)
            }],
            ..workflow_run("01J0WFR5")
        },
    ]
}

/// What the waiting check of the first run shows: the lead's branch at its tip, and a member
/// branch that is gone.
fn check_view() -> CheckView {
    CheckView {
        step_id: "review".into(),
        summary: "Made the icon dark on a light taskbar.".into(),
        branches: vec![
            ShownBranch {
                branch: "dots/nightly-tests/01J0RUN5".into(),
                run_id: "01J0RUN5".into(),
                commit: Some("2222222222222222222222222222222222222222".into()),
            },
            ShownBranch {
                branch: "dots/reviewer/01J0RUN6".into(),
                run_id: "01J0RUN6".into(),
                commit: None,
            },
        ],
    }
}

/// Adds the samples of teams, workflows and workflow runs, and their events.
pub(super) fn teams_samples(samples: &mut Value) {
    samples["Team"] = to(teams());
    samples["TeamSpec"] = to(team_spec());
    samples["Project"] = to(project());
    samples["ProjectSpec"] = to([
        project_spec(),
        ProjectSpec {
            default_branch: None,
            ..project_spec()
        },
    ]);
    let workflows = workflows();
    samples["WorkflowSpec"] = to(workflows.iter().map(|w| &w.spec).collect::<Vec<_>>());
    samples["Workflow"] = to(&workflows);
    // The builder's live check: a step's problem, then the workflow's.
    samples["WorkflowProblem"] = to([
        WorkflowProblem {
            step_id: Some("work".into()),
            message: "step \"Work\": 01J0GONE is not a member of the team".into(),
        },
        WorkflowProblem {
            step_id: None,
            message: "a workflow name must be 1 to 64 characters".into(),
        },
    ]);
    samples["WorkflowRunFilter"] = to([
        WorkflowRunFilter {
            team_id: Some(TEAM.into()),
            workflow_id: Some("01J0WFL".into()),
            statuses: vec![WorkflowRunStatus::Running, WorkflowRunStatus::Waiting],
            limit: Some(20),
        },
        WorkflowRunFilter::default(),
    ]);
    let runs = workflow_runs();
    samples["WorkflowRun"] = to(&runs);
    samples["CheckView"] = to(check_view());
    samples["CheckTip"] = to(check_view().tips());
    samples["Enums"]["WorkflowTrigger"] = to(WorkflowTrigger::ALL);
    samples["Enums"]["WorkflowRunStatus"] = to(WorkflowRunStatus::ALL);
    samples["Enums"]["StepRunStatus"] = to(StepRunStatus::ALL);
    samples["RuntimeEvent"]
        .as_array_mut()
        .expect("the RuntimeEvent samples")
        .extend([
            to(RuntimeEvent::TeamUpdated {
                team_id: TEAM.into(),
            }),
            to(RuntimeEvent::WorkflowUpdated {
                team_id: TEAM.into(),
                workflow_id: "01J0WFL".into(),
            }),
            to(RuntimeEvent::WorkflowRunUpdated {
                run: runs[0].clone(),
            }),
        ]);
}
```

**Create `crates/dots-core/src/workflows/folder.rs`:**

```rust
//! The folders "Open folder" opens for teams (teams spec sections 3 and 10.3): a project's repo
//! folder, and the worktree of a branch a deploy listed as ready. Each is looked up by id in the
//! store, so the UI never names a path.

use std::path::PathBuf;

use super::Workflows;
use crate::{Error, Result};

/// `path` when it is a folder now; `NotFound` as `Runtime::run_folder` says it otherwise.
fn existing(path: PathBuf) -> Result<PathBuf> {
    if !path.is_dir() {
        return Err(Error::NotFound(format!(
            "folder {} no longer exists",
            path.display()
        )));
    }
    Ok(path)
}

impl Workflows {
    /// The repo folder of the project `project_id`, for the team page's "Open folder".
    pub async fn project_folder(&self, project_id: &str) -> Result<PathBuf> {
        let project = self.store.get_project(project_id).await?;
        existing(PathBuf::from(project.spec.repo_path))
    }

    /// The worktree of `branch`, a branch a deploy step of the workflow run `id` listed as ready
    /// (mode `branch`, or any deploy of a test run): the run page's "Open folder". The folder is
    /// the one the step that made the branch worked in, read from the run's hand-offs. `Invalid`
    /// for a branch no deploy of the run listed as ready.
    pub async fn branch_folder(&self, id: &str, branch: &str) -> Result<PathBuf> {
        let wr = self.store.get_workflow_run(id).await?;
        let ready = wr.steps.iter().any(|s| {
            s.deploy_result
                .as_ref()
                .is_some_and(|d| d.ready && d.branches.iter().any(|b| b.branch == branch))
        });
        // The newest hand-off that carries the branch: a later attempt may have moved it.
        let path = wr
            .steps
            .iter()
            .rev()
            .filter_map(|s| s.handoff.as_ref())
            .flat_map(|h| &h.branches)
            .find(|b| b.branch == branch)
            .map(|b| PathBuf::from(&b.worktree_path));
        match (ready, path) {
            (true, Some(path)) => existing(path),
            _ => Err(Error::Invalid(format!(
                "workflow run {id} has no ready branch {branch}"
            ))),
        }
    }
}
```

**with:**

```ts
  runReleaseHold: (runId: string) => call(T.Run, "run_release_hold", { runId }),

  // Teams, their projects and workflows (teams spec section 10). A refusal carries the core's
  // sentence: kind `invalid` for a rule broken, `conflict` for a taken name or a state that
  // moved on, `not_allowed` for a role that may not.
  teamsList: () => call(T.Team.array(), "teams_list"),
  teamGet: (id: string) => call(T.Team, "team_get", { id }),
  teamCreate: (spec: T.TeamSpec) => call(T.Team, "team_create", { spec }),
  teamUpdate: (id: string, spec: T.TeamSpec) => call(T.Team, "team_update", { id, spec }),
  /// Stops the team's runs in flight, then deletes it with its projects and workflows.
  teamDelete: (id: string) => call(nothing, "team_delete", { id }),
  /// Rejects with kind `invalid` when the folder is not a git repo.
  projectAdd: (teamId: string, spec: T.ProjectSpec) =>
    call(T.Project, "project_add", { teamId, spec }),
  projectUpdate: (id: string, spec: T.ProjectSpec) =>
    call(T.Project, "project_update", { id, spec }),
  projectDelete: (id: string) => call(nothing, "project_delete", { id }),
  /// Opens the project's repo folder in Explorer. Rejects with kind `not_found` once it is gone.
  openProjectFolder: (projectId: string) =>
    call(nothing, "open_project_folder", { projectId, workflowRunId: null, branch: null }),
  /// Opens the worktree of a branch the workflow run's deploy listed as ready.
  openBranchFolder: (workflowRunId: string, branch: string) =>
    call(nothing, "open_project_folder", { projectId: null, workflowRunId, branch }),
  workflowsList: (teamId: string) => call(T.Workflow.array(), "workflows_list", { teamId }),
  workflowGet: (id: string) => call(T.Workflow, "workflow_get", { id }),
  workflowCreate: (teamId: string, spec: T.WorkflowSpec) =>
    call(T.Workflow, "workflow_create", { teamId, spec }),
  /// Saves the builder's draft; runs in flight keep the definition they started with.
  workflowUpdate: (id: string, spec: T.WorkflowSpec) =>
    call(T.Workflow, "workflow_update", { id, spec }),
  workflowDelete: (id: string) => call(nothing, "workflow_delete", { id }),
  /// The builder's live check: the first refusal a save would give, or null.
  workflowCheckSpec: (teamId: string, spec: T.WorkflowSpec) =>
    call(T.WorkflowProblem.nullable(), "workflow_check_spec", { teamId, spec }),
  /// Rejects with kind `conflict` while the local server is not running.
  workflowWebhookUrl: (id: string) => call(text, "workflow_webhook_url", { id }),
  /// A new token, shown this once; the old one stops working.
  regenerateWorkflowToken: (id: string) => call(text, "regenerate_workflow_token", { id }),

  // Workflow runs (teams spec sections 5 to 10).
  /// Newest first; a field left out of `filter` does not narrow the list.
  workflowRunsList: (filter: Partial<T.WorkflowRunFilter>) =>
    call(T.WorkflowRun.array(), "workflow_runs_list", { filter }),
  /// The runs the chat runs `runIds` started, oldest first: a conversation's cards.
  workflowRunsForOrigins: (runIds: string[]) =>
    call(T.WorkflowRun.array(), "workflow_runs_for_origins", { runIds }),
  workflowRunGet: (id: string) => call(T.WorkflowRun, "workflow_run_get", { id }),
  /// "Run now", or "Test run" with `test`: its deploys push nothing.
  workflowRunStart: (workflowId: string, projectId: string, task: string, test: boolean) =>
    call(T.WorkflowRun, "workflow_run_start", { workflowId, projectId, task, test }),
  /// What the check the run waits on shows. Rejects with kind `conflict` when it waits on none.
  workflowCheck: (id: string) => call(T.CheckView, "workflow_check", { id }),
  /// A branch's committed work from the project's default branch, at `commit`.
  workflowBranchDiff: (id: string, branch: string, commit: string) =>
    call(T.WorktreeDiff, "workflow_branch_diff", { id, branch, commit }),
  /// Approves with the tips the check showed. Rejects with kind `conflict` when a branch moved
  /// since: the check is read again.
  workflowApprove: (id: string, note: string | null, tips: T.CheckTip[]) =>
    call(T.WorkflowRun, "workflow_approve", { id, note, tips }),
  workflowSendBack: (id: string, note: string) =>
    call(T.WorkflowRun, "workflow_send_back", { id, note }),
  workflowAddBudget: (id: string) => call(T.WorkflowRun, "workflow_add_budget", { id }),
  /// Lets the send-back held at the cap go on.
  workflowAllowSendBack: (id: string) => call(T.WorkflowRun, "workflow_allow_send_back", { id }),
  workflowRetryStep: (id: string) => call(T.WorkflowRun, "workflow_retry_step", { id }),
  workflowStop: (id: string) => call(T.WorkflowRun, "workflow_stop", { id }),
};
```

**with:**

```ts
export type WaitingWorkflowRun = z.infer<typeof WaitingWorkflowRun>;

// Teams and workflows (teams spec sections 3 to 11). Members and steps name dots by id.
export const MemberSpec = z.strictObject({ dot_id: z.string(), role: z.string() });
export type MemberSpec = z.infer<typeof MemberSpec>;
// A team as it is saved. Its account and workspace are fixed once it is made.
export const TeamSpec = z.strictObject({
  name: z.string(),
  description: z.string(),
  members: z.array(MemberSpec),
  org_id: nullable(z.string()),
  workspace_id: z.string(),
});
export type TeamSpec = z.infer<typeof TeamSpec>;
export const TeamMember = z.strictObject({
  dot_id: z.string(),
  dot_name: z.string(),
  role: z.string(),
});
export type TeamMember = z.infer<typeof TeamMember>;
// A team's local git repo. `default_branch` null: found as worktrees find it.
export const ProjectSpec = z.strictObject({
  name: z.string(),
  repo_path: z.string(),
  default_branch: nullable(z.string()),
  remote: z.string(),
});
export type ProjectSpec = z.infer<typeof ProjectSpec>;
export const Project = z.strictObject({
  id: z.string(),
  team_id: z.string(),
  created_at: z.string(),
  updated_at: z.string(),
  ...ProjectSpec.shape,
});
export type Project = z.infer<typeof Project>;
export const Team = z.strictObject({
  id: z.string(),
  name: z.string(),
  description: z.string(),
  members: z.array(TeamMember),
  projects: z.array(Project),
  org_id: nullable(z.string()),
  workspace_id: z.string(),
  created_at: z.string(),
  updated_at: z.string(),
});
export type Team = z.infer<typeof Team>;

export const WorkflowTrigger = z.enum(["chat", "manual", "schedule", "webhook"]);
export type WorkflowTrigger = z.infer<typeof WorkflowTrigger>;
export const WorkflowRunStatus = z.enum(["running", "waiting", "succeeded", "failed", "cancelled"]);
export type WorkflowRunStatus = z.infer<typeof WorkflowRunStatus>;
export const StepRunStatus = z.enum([
  "running",
  "waiting",
  "succeeded",
  "failed",
  "cancelled",
  "skipped",
]);
export type StepRunStatus = z.infer<typeof StepRunStatus>;
// How `gh pr merge` merges; squash when the JSON leaves it out.
export const MergeMethod = z.enum(["merge", "squash", "rebase"]);
export type MergeMethod = z.infer<typeof MergeMethod>;
// What a branch step compares its labels with: the outcome reported before it, or the project.
export const BranchOn = z.enum(["outcome", "project"]);
export type BranchOn = z.infer<typeof BranchOn>;

// What starts a workflow run (section 5.1). A webhook's task and project are the fallbacks for
// a body without them; a schedule's project is named.
export const Trigger = z.discriminatedUnion("kind", [
  z.strictObject({ kind: z.literal("manual") }),
  z.strictObject({ kind: z.literal("chat"), starters: z.array(z.string()) }),
  z.strictObject({
    kind: z.literal("schedule"),
    schedule: Schedule,
    task: z.string(),
    project: z.string(),
  }),
  z.strictObject({
    kind: z.literal("webhook"),
    task: nullable(z.string()),
    project: nullable(z.string()),
  }),
]);
export type Trigger = z.infer<typeof Trigger>;
// What a delegate step's member may start (section 5.3).
export const Delegate = z.strictObject({
  may_start: z.array(z.string()),
  at_once: z.number().int(),
  total: z.number().int(),
});
export type Delegate = z.infer<typeof Delegate>;

// A step (sections 5.2 to 5.6): its id is made by the builder, unique across the whole tree.
// The kind's fields sit beside `id` and `name`, and a deploy's mode's beside those.
const stepShape = { id: z.string(), name: z.string() };
export const AgentStep = z.strictObject({
  ...stepShape,
  kind: z.literal("agent"),
  member: z.string(),
  job: z.string(),
  // Earlier agent steps it may send work back to, by id.
  can_send_back_to: z.array(z.string()),
  // The labels it may report, for a branch on outcome after it.
  outcomes: z.array(z.string()),
  delegate: nullable(Delegate),
});
export type AgentStep = z.infer<typeof AgentStep>;
// `send_back_to` null: the nearest agent step before it on its path.
export const CheckStep = z.strictObject({
  ...stepShape,
  kind: z.literal("check"),
  send_back_to: nullable(z.string()),
});
export type CheckStep = z.infer<typeof CheckStep>;
const deployShape = { ...stepShape, kind: z.literal("deploy") };
export const DeployStep = z.discriminatedUnion("mode", [
  z.strictObject({ ...deployShape, mode: z.literal("branch") }),
  z.strictObject({ ...deployShape, mode: z.literal("push") }),
  z.strictObject({ ...deployShape, mode: z.literal("pr") }),
  z.strictObject({ ...deployShape, mode: z.literal("pr_merge"), merge_method: MergeMethod }),
  z.strictObject({
    ...deployShape,
    mode: z.literal("member"),
    member: z.string(),
    job: z.string(),
  }),
]);
export type DeployStep = z.infer<typeof DeployStep>;
export type DeployMode = DeployStep["mode"];
// A branch holds steps of its own, so its schemas are typed by hand and read `Step` lazily.
export type BranchPath = { label: string; steps: Step[] };
export type BranchStep = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  kind: "branch";
  on: BranchOn;
  paths: BranchPath[];
  otherwise: Step[];
};
export type Step = AgentStep | CheckStep | DeployStep | BranchStep;
export const BranchPath: z.ZodType<BranchPath> = z.strictObject({
  label: z.string(),
  steps: z.array(z.lazy(() => Step)),
});
export const BranchStep: z.ZodType<BranchStep> = z.strictObject({
  ...stepShape,
  kind: z.literal("branch"),
  on: BranchOn,
  paths: z.array(BranchPath),
  otherwise: z.array(z.lazy(() => Step)),
});
export const Step: z.ZodType<Step> = z.union([AgentStep, CheckStep, DeployStep, BranchStep]);
// The limits of one workflow run (section 5.7).
export const Budget = z.strictObject({
  max_runs: z.number().int(),
  max_tokens: z.number().int(),
  max_minutes: z.number().int(),
  max_send_backs: z.number().int(),
});
export type Budget = z.infer<typeof Budget>;
export const Definition = z.strictObject({
  trigger: Trigger,
  steps: z.array(Step),
  budget: Budget,
});
export type Definition = z.infer<typeof Definition>;
// A workflow as the builder saves it.
export const WorkflowSpec = z.strictObject({
  name: z.string(),
  definition: Definition,
  enabled: z.boolean(),
});
export type WorkflowSpec = z.infer<typeof WorkflowSpec>;
// A workflow's newest run, for the team page.
export const LastRun = z.strictObject({
  id: z.string(),
  status: WorkflowRunStatus,
  started_at: z.string(),
  test: z.boolean(),
});
export type LastRun = z.infer<typeof LastRun>;
// `problem`: why it cannot start now (the team changed under it). Runs in flight keep the
// definition they started with. The webhook token itself is shown once, when it is made.
export const Workflow = z.strictObject({
  id: z.string(),
  team_id: z.string(),
  created_at: z.string(),
  updated_at: z.string(),
  problem: nullable(z.string()),
  has_webhook_token: z.boolean(),
  runs_in_flight: z.number().int(),
  last_run: nullable(LastRun),
  ...WorkflowSpec.shape,
});
export type Workflow = z.infer<typeof Workflow>;
// The first refusal a save of the builder's draft would give, and the step it names (null:
// the name, the trigger, the budget or the list of steps).
export const WorkflowProblem = z.strictObject({
  step_id: nullable(z.string()),
  message: z.string(),
});
export type WorkflowProblem = z.infer<typeof WorkflowProblem>;
// Which workflow runs to list, newest first; a field left out does not narrow them, no
// statuses is any status, no limit is all.
export const WorkflowRunFilter = z.strictObject({
  team_id: nullable(z.string()),
  workflow_id: nullable(z.string()),
  statuses: z.array(WorkflowRunStatus),
  limit: nullable(z.number().int()),
});
export type WorkflowRunFilter = z.infer<typeof WorkflowRunFilter>;

// A branch a step produced, and the dot run whose branch it is (section 6).
export const BranchRef = z.strictObject({
  run_id: z.string(),
  branch: z.string(),
  worktree_path: z.string(),
  base_commit: nullable(z.string()),
});
export type BranchRef = z.infer<typeof BranchRef>;
// What a member a delegate step started handed back; its branch is null once it is gone.
export const MemberHandoff = z.strictObject({
  dot_id: z.string(),
  task: z.string(),
  status: RunStatus,
  summary: z.string(),
  branch: nullable(z.string()),
});
export type MemberHandoff = z.infer<typeof MemberHandoff>;
// What a finished step passes to the next ones (section 6).
export const Handoff = z.strictObject({
  summary: z.string(),
  branches: z.array(BranchRef),
  outcome: nullable(z.string()),
  members: z.array(MemberHandoff),
});
export type Handoff = z.infer<typeof Handoff>;
// A branch and the commit the check page showed (null: the branch is gone), sent with Approve.
export const CheckTip = z.strictObject({ branch: z.string(), commit: nullable(z.string()) });
export type CheckTip = z.infer<typeof CheckTip>;
// A check's decision, or the path a branch step took. An approval recorded before the
// approved tips were kept has no `branches`.
export const StepDecision = z.union([
  z.strictObject({
    decision: z.literal("approved"),
    note: nullable(z.string()),
    branches: z.array(CheckTip).optional(),
  }),
  z.strictObject({ decision: z.literal("sent_back"), note: z.string() }),
  z.strictObject({ path: nullable(z.string()) }),
]);
export type StepDecision = z.infer<typeof StepDecision>;
// What a deploy did to one branch.
export const DeployedBranch = z.strictObject({
  branch: z.string(),
  commit: z.string(),
  pushed: z.boolean(),
  pr_url: nullable(z.string()),
  auto_merge: z.boolean(),
});
export type DeployedBranch = z.infer<typeof DeployedBranch>;
// `ready`: the branches were kept and listed as ready, not pushed. `error` is left out of a
// deploy that succeeded.
export const DeployResult = z.strictObject({
  branches: z.array(DeployedBranch),
  ready: z.boolean(),
  error: z.string().optional(),
});
export type DeployResult = z.infer<typeof DeployResult>;
// One step's execution. `send_back`: what an agent step asked to send back, performed when it
// succeeds; `notice`: what the run page says about the attempt beyond its status.
export const StepRun = z.strictObject({
  id: z.string(),
  workflow_run_id: z.string(),
  step_id: z.string(),
  attempt: z.number().int(),
  status: StepRunStatus,
  handoff: nullable(Handoff),
  decision: nullable(StepDecision),
  error: nullable(z.string()),
  outcome: nullable(z.string()),
  notice: nullable(z.string()),
  send_back: nullable(z.strictObject({ to: z.string(), note: z.string() })),
  deploy_result: nullable(DeployResult),
  started_at: z.string(),
  ended_at: nullable(z.string()),
});
export type StepRun = z.infer<typeof StepRun>;
export const SentBy = z.enum(["person", "agent"]);
export type SentBy = z.infer<typeof SentBy>;
// The last send-back: performed, or `held` at the cap until the person allows one more.
export const SendBack = z.strictObject({
  from: z.string(),
  to: z.string(),
  note: z.string(),
  by: SentBy,
  held: z.boolean(),
});
export type SendBack = z.infer<typeof SendBack>;
// One execution of a workflow with its copy of the definition and its step runs, oldest first.
// The workflow and the project are null once they are deleted. `spent_tokens` counts new input
// and output; cache reads are shown beside it, not counted.
export const WorkflowRun = z.strictObject({
  id: z.string(),
  workflow_id: nullable(z.string()),
  team_id: z.string(),
  definition: Definition,
  project_id: nullable(z.string()),
  project: ProjectSpec,
  task: z.string(),
  trigger: WorkflowTrigger,
  origin_run_id: nullable(z.string()),
  test: z.boolean(),
  status: WorkflowRunStatus,
  waiting_on: nullable(WaitingOn),
  current_step: nullable(z.string()),
  budget: Budget,
  send_backs: z.number().int(),
  send_back: nullable(SendBack),
  spent_runs: z.number().int(),
  spent_tokens: z.number().int(),
  spent_cache_read: z.number().int(),
  active_ms: z.number().int(),
  error: nullable(z.string()),
  started_at: z.string(),
  ended_at: nullable(z.string()),
  steps: z.array(StepRun),
});
export type WorkflowRun = z.infer<typeof WorkflowRun>;
// A branch the waiting check shows, with the commit at its tip now (null: the branch is gone).
export const ShownBranch = z.strictObject({
  branch: z.string(),
  run_id: z.string(),
  commit: nullable(z.string()),
});
export type ShownBranch = z.infer<typeof ShownBranch>;
// What the check a workflow run waits on shows; Approve sends its branches back as `CheckTip`s.
export const CheckView = z.strictObject({
  step_id: z.string(),
  summary: z.string(),
  branches: z.array(ShownBranch),
});
export type CheckView = z.infer<typeof CheckView>;
```

### Task 7: Teams notify when a workflow needs a check, hits a limit, finishes or fails, and step runs stay quiet

The `Notifier` keeps, per workflow run, the last status, wait and current step run it saw (`Moved`), and on a `WorkflowRunUpdated` that differs tells one notice by `WorkflowNotice::of`, titled with the team's name and routed to `/workflow-runs/<id>`. A `RunUpdated` of a step's run is quiet on `succeeded` and `failed` (decision 6, app half). Only `events.rs` changes.

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/dots-app/src/events.rs`
- Test: `apps/dots-app/src/events.rs` (`mod tests`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 6's nothing new; plan A's `RuntimeEvent::{WorkflowRunUpdated, RunUpdated}`, `WorkflowRun`, `budget_reached`, `first_line`, the viewing rule, `rt.teams().get`, `rt.get_run`.
- Produces: `events::{Notice::Workflow { run: Box<WorkflowRun>, about: WorkflowNotice }, WorkflowNotice { Check, Budget, SendBacks, StepFailed, Finished, Failed }, WorkflowNotice::of(status: WorkflowRunStatus, waiting_on: Option<WaitingOn>) -> Option<WorkflowNotice>}`; private `Moved { status, waiting_on, step_run: Option<String> }`, `Moved::of(&WorkflowRun)`, `Notifier.workflows: HashMap<String, Moved>` (cleared by `forget()`), `workflow_text(run, about, team) -> (String, String)`; `notice_route` gives `/workflow-runs/<id>`. Test helpers `workflow_run(id, status, waiting_on)`, `workflow_told(n, id, seq)`, `at_step(run, step_id, step_run_id)`.

The four tests sit in `events.rs`'s own `mod tests`, beside the file's other notice tests. `gen.py` files a path by its folder, so their edits come with the code's in Step 3, and this step has no block of its own:

```rust
    },
    /// A workflow run waits on the person or ended (teams spec section 10.5). Told once each

        about: WorkflowNotice,

/// What a workflow run's notice is about (teams spec section 10.5).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum WorkflowNotice {
    /// It waits on the person's check.
    Check,
    /// It reached a limit of its budget.
    Budget,
    /// It holds a send-back past the cap of send-backs.
    SendBacks,
    /// A step failed, and the run waits for Retry step or Stop: this is how a workflow run
    /// fails (spec section 5.5), so it is told as "failed".
    StepFailed,
    Finished,
    Failed,
}

impl WorkflowNotice {
    /// What a workflow run in `status`, waiting on `waiting_on`, tells the person. `None`
    /// while it runs, once it is cancelled (the person stopped it), and for a wait that names
    /// nothing.
    pub fn of(status: WorkflowRunStatus, waiting_on: Option<WaitingOn>) -> Option<Self> {
        match (status, waiting_on) {
            (WorkflowRunStatus::Waiting, Some(WaitingOn::Check)) => Some(Self::Check),
            (WorkflowRunStatus::Waiting, Some(WaitingOn::Budget)) => Some(Self::Budget),
            (WorkflowRunStatus::Waiting, Some(WaitingOn::SendBacks)) => Some(Self::SendBacks),
            (WorkflowRunStatus::Waiting, Some(WaitingOn::StepFailed)) => Some(Self::StepFailed),
            (WorkflowRunStatus::Succeeded, _) => Some(Self::Finished),
            (WorkflowRunStatus::Failed, _) => Some(Self::Failed),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
}
```

```rust
/// notify either. A run that parks on an approval request this notifier has announced does
/// not notify a second time: one park, one notification. A workflow step's runs (members
/// included) never notify that they finished or failed: the workflow run tells what matters
/// (teams spec section 10.5); their questions, approvals and parks notify as any run's.
///
/// A workflow run notifies when it moves into a wait on the person, finishes or fails, once
/// per move: an update that repeats its status, its wait and its current step run is quiet.
/// A wait on a new step run is a new move (a second check right after an approval, a retried
/// step that fails again). A workflow run first seen waiting or ended notifies too: the core
/// publishes a run when it moves or the person acts on it, so a run first seen there moved
/// while the app was not listening (Dots restarted in the middle of a step).
#[derive(Default)]
```

**with:**

```rust
    asked: HashSet<String>,
    /// Where each workflow run seen was last, ended runs included, so that an ended run
    /// published again is not told twice. Workflow runs are few (each one waits on the
    /// person's check or budget), so they are kept until a lag clears them.
    workflows: HashMap<String, Moved>,
}

/// Where a workflow run is, as far as its notices go: its status, its wait, and the newest
/// step run of its current step.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
struct Moved {
    status: WorkflowRunStatus,
    waiting_on: Option<WaitingOn>,
    step_run: Option<String>,
}

impl Moved {
    /// Where `run` is now.
    fn of(run: &WorkflowRun) -> Self {
        let step_run = run.current_step.as_deref().and_then(|current| {
            // Step runs are oldest first.
            run.steps
                .iter()
                .rev()
                .find(|s| s.step_id == current)
                .map(|s| s.id.clone())
        });
        Moved {
            status: run.status,
            waiting_on: run.waiting_on,
            step_run,
        }
    }
}
```

```rust
/// (title, body) of a workflow run's notice, in the needs-you wording (teams spec section
/// 10.5); `team` is the name of the run's team. A test run's title says so.
fn workflow_text(run: &WorkflowRun, about: WorkflowNotice, team: &str) -> (String, String) {
    let task = first_line(Some(run.task.as_str()), "A task.");
    let step = run
        .current_step
        .as_deref()
        .and_then(|id| run.definition.step(id))
        .map(|s| s.name.as_str());
    // "<step name>: <task's first line>", or the task alone before any step.
    let at_step = match step {
        Some(name) => first_line(Some(format!("{name}: {task}").as_str()), &task),
        None => task.clone(),
    };
    let (title, body) = match about {
        WorkflowNotice::Check => (format!("{team} needs your check"), at_step),
        WorkflowNotice::Budget => (
            format!("{team} hit its budget"),
            dots_core::workflows::budget_reached(run)
                .unwrap_or_else(|| first_line(run.error.as_deref(), &task)),
        ),
        WorkflowNotice::SendBacks => (
            format!("{team} hit its send-back limit"),
            format!(
                "send-backs, {} of {}",
                run.send_backs, run.budget.max_send_backs
            ),
        ),
        // The run's error names the step: "<step name>: <what failed>".
        WorkflowNotice::StepFailed => (
            format!("{team} failed"),
            first_line(run.error.as_deref(), &at_step),
        ),
        WorkflowNotice::Finished => (format!("{team} finished"), task),
        WorkflowNotice::Failed => (format!("{team} failed"), task),
    };
    if run.test {
        (format!("{title} (test)"), body)
    } else {
        (title, body)
    }
```

```rust
    /// A workflow run of team `t` at its check step "Review" (no step run yet), with a task
    /// of two lines.
    fn workflow_run(
        id: &str,
        status: WorkflowRunStatus,
        waiting_on: Option<WaitingOn>,
    ) -> WorkflowRun {
        use dots_core::model::{ProjectSpec, WorkflowTrigger};
        WorkflowRun {
            id: id.into(),
            workflow_id: Some("wf".into()),
            team_id: "t".into(),
            definition: serde_json::from_value(json!({
                "trigger": {"kind": "manual"},
                "steps": [
                    {"id": "work", "name": "Work", "kind": "agent", "member": "d", "job": "Fix it."},
                    {"id": "review", "name": "Review", "kind": "check"},
                    {"id": "recheck", "name": "Second look", "kind": "check"},
                    {"id": "ship", "name": "Ship", "kind": "deploy", "mode": "push"}
                ]
            }))
            .unwrap(),
            project_id: Some("p".into()),
            project: ProjectSpec {
                name: "Dots".into(),
                repo_path: "C:/src/dots".into(),
                default_branch: None,
                remote: "origin".into(),
            },
            task: "\n  Fix the flaky test.\nIt fails on CI.".into(),
            trigger: WorkflowTrigger::Manual,
            origin_run_id: None,
            test: false,
            status,
            waiting_on,
            current_step: Some("review".into()),
            budget: dots_core::workflow::Budget::default(),
            send_backs: 0,
            send_back: None,
            spent_runs: 0,
            spent_tokens: 0,
            spent_cache_read: 0,
            active_ms: 0,
            error: None,
            started_at: "t".into(),
            ended_at: None,
            steps: vec![],
        }
    }

    /// Feeds `seq` as WorkflowRunUpdated events for one workflow run; what each told.
    fn workflow_told(
        n: &mut Notifier,
        id: &str,
        seq: &[(WorkflowRunStatus, Option<WaitingOn>)],
    ) -> Vec<Option<WorkflowNotice>> {
        seq.iter()
            .map(|(status, waiting_on)| {
                let ev = RuntimeEvent::WorkflowRunUpdated {
                    run: workflow_run(id, *status, *waiting_on),
                };
                n.decide(&ev).map(|notice| match notice {
                    Notice::Workflow { about, .. } => about,
                    other => panic!("not a workflow notice: {other:?}"),
                })
            })
            .collect()
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_workflow_run_notifies_once_per_wait_and_when_it_ends() {
        use WaitingOn as On;
        use WorkflowNotice as W;
        use WorkflowRunStatus::{
            Cancelled as WCancelled, Failed as WFailed, Running as WRunning,
            Succeeded as WSucceeded, Waiting,
        };
        let mut n = Notifier::default();
        // Each wait on the person notifies as the run moves into it; an update that repeats
        // the status and the wait (its spend moved) is quiet.
        assert_eq!(
            workflow_told(
                &mut n,
                "w",
                &[
                    (WRunning, None),
                    (WRunning, None),
                    (Waiting, Some(On::Check)),
                    (Waiting, Some(On::Check)),
                    (WRunning, None),
                    (Waiting, Some(On::Budget)),
                    (WRunning, None),
                    (Waiting, Some(On::SendBacks)),
                    (WRunning, None),
                    (Waiting, Some(On::StepFailed)),
                    (WRunning, None),
                    (Waiting, Some(On::Check)),
                    (WSucceeded, None),
                    (WSucceeded, None),
                ]
            ),
            [
                None,
                None,
                Some(W::Check),
                None,
                None,
                Some(W::Budget),
                None,
                Some(W::SendBacks),
                None,
                Some(W::StepFailed),
                None,
                Some(W::Check),
                Some(W::Finished),
                None,
            ]
        );
        // The person stopped it: nothing to tell.
        assert_eq!(
            workflow_told(
                &mut n,
                "c",
                &[
                    (WRunning, None),
                    (Waiting, Some(On::Check)),
                    (WCancelled, None)
                ]
            ),
            [None, Some(W::Check), None]
        );
        // A run that fails after its wait on the failed step was told then; one that fails
        // otherwise is told now.
        assert_eq!(
            workflow_told(
                &mut n,
                "f",
                &[
                    (WRunning, None),
                    (Waiting, Some(On::StepFailed)),
                    (WFailed, None)
                ]
            ),
            [None, Some(W::StepFailed), None]
        );
        assert_eq!(
            workflow_told(&mut n, "g", &[(WRunning, None), (WFailed, None)]),
            [None, Some(W::Failed)]
        );
        // First seen waiting (it moved while the app was not listening): told; a wait that
        // names nothing is not.
        assert_eq!(
            workflow_told(&mut n, "late", &[(Waiting, Some(On::Check))]),
            [Some(W::Check)]
        );
        assert_eq!(workflow_told(&mut n, "odd", &[(Waiting, None)]), [None]);
        // A dot run's events never tell about a workflow run.
        assert_eq!(
            notified(&mut n, "w", TriggerKind::Manual, &[Running]),
            [false]
        );
        // After a lag the notifier starts again, workflow runs included.
        n.forget();
        assert!(n.workflows.is_empty());
        assert_eq!(
            workflow_told(&mut n, "w", &[(WSucceeded, None)]),
            [Some(W::Finished)]
        );
    }

    /// `run` at step `step_id`, whose newest step run is `step_run_id`.
    fn at_step(run: WorkflowRun, step_id: &str, step_run_id: &str) -> WorkflowRun {
        use dots_core::model::{StepRun, StepRunStatus};
        let step_run = StepRun {
            id: step_run_id.into(),
            workflow_run_id: run.id.clone(),
            step_id: step_id.into(),
            attempt: 1,
            status: StepRunStatus::Waiting,
            handoff: None,
            decision: None,
            error: None,
            outcome: None,
            notice: None,
            send_back: None,
            deploy_result: None,
            started_at: "t".into(),
            ended_at: None,
        };
        let mut steps = run.steps.clone();
        steps.push(step_run);
        WorkflowRun {
            current_step: Some(step_id.into()),
            steps,
            ..run
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_wait_on_a_new_step_run_is_a_new_notice() {
        use WaitingOn as On;
        use WorkflowRunStatus::Waiting;
        let mut n = Notifier::default();
        let mut told = |run: WorkflowRun| {
            n.decide(&RuntimeEvent::WorkflowRunUpdated { run })
                .map(|notice| notice_text(&notice, "crew"))
        };
        // Approving the first check moves the run straight to a second one: no running
        // update between them.
        let first = at_step(workflow_run("w", Waiting, Some(On::Check)), "review", "s1");
        let second = at_step(first.clone(), "recheck", "s2");
        assert!(told(first.clone()).is_some());
        assert_eq!(told(first), None);
        assert_eq!(
            told(second.clone()),
            Some((
                "crew needs your check".into(),
                "Second look: Fix the flaky test.".into()
            ))
        );
        assert_eq!(told(second.clone()), None);
        // Retry step makes a new attempt; if it fails again, that is told again.
        let failed = WorkflowRun {
            waiting_on: Some(On::StepFailed),
            error: Some("Ship: gh is not installed".into()),
            ..at_step(second, "ship", "s3")
        };
        assert!(told(failed.clone()).is_some());
        assert_eq!(told(failed.clone()), None);
        let again = at_step(failed, "ship", "s4");
        assert_eq!(
            told(again),
            Some(("crew failed".into(), "Ship: gh is not installed".into()))
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn workflow_notices_say_what_waits_and_open_the_run_page() {
        use WaitingOn as On;
        use WorkflowRunStatus::{Failed as WFailed, Succeeded as WSucceeded, Waiting};
        let told = |run: WorkflowRun| {
            let about = WorkflowNotice::of(run.status, run.waiting_on).expect("a notice");
            Notice::Workflow {
                run: Box::new(run),
                about,
            }
        };
        let check = told(workflow_run("w1", Waiting, Some(On::Check)));
        assert_eq!(
            notice_text(&check, "crew"),
            (
                "crew needs your check".into(),
                "Review: Fix the flaky test.".into()
            )
        );
        assert_eq!(notice_route(&check, ""), "/workflow-runs/w1");
        assert_eq!(notice_route(&check, "d"), "/workflow-runs/w1");
        let budget = told(WorkflowRun {
            spent_runs: 10,
            error: Some("Budget reached: runs, 10 of 10".into()),
            ..workflow_run("w2", Waiting, Some(On::Budget))
        });
        assert_eq!(
            notice_text(&budget, "crew"),
            ("crew hit its budget".into(), "runs, 10 of 10".into())
        );
        let send_backs = told(WorkflowRun {
            send_backs: 3,
            ..workflow_run("w3", Waiting, Some(On::SendBacks))
        });
        assert_eq!(
            notice_text(&send_backs, "crew"),
            (
                "crew hit its send-back limit".into(),
                "send-backs, 3 of 3".into()
            )
        );
        let step_failed = told(WorkflowRun {
            current_step: Some("ship".into()),
            error: Some("Ship: git push was rejected\nhint: fetch first".into()),
            ..workflow_run("w4", Waiting, Some(On::StepFailed))
        });
        assert_eq!(
            notice_text(&step_failed, "crew"),
            ("crew failed".into(), "Ship: git push was rejected".into())
        );
        let cases = [
            (WSucceeded, false, "crew finished"),
            (WFailed, false, "crew failed"),
            (WSucceeded, true, "crew finished (test)"),
            (WFailed, true, "crew failed (test)"),
        ];
        for (status, test, title) in cases {
            let ended = told(WorkflowRun {
                test,
                ..workflow_run("w5", status, None)
            });
            assert_eq!(
                notice_text(&ended, "crew"),
                (title.to_string(), "Fix the flaky test.".to_string())
            );
        }
        // A test run says so on every notice; before its first step, the task alone.
        let early = told(WorkflowRun {
            test: true,
            current_step: None,
            ..workflow_run("w6", Waiting, Some(On::Check))
        });
        assert_eq!(
            notice_text(&early, "crew"),
            (
                "crew needs your check (test)".into(),
                "Fix the flaky test.".into()
            )
        );
        // Without the limit's text (the run's budget was raised since), the run's error.
        let raised = told(WorkflowRun {
            error: Some("Budget reached: tokens, 2 of 1".into()),
            ..workflow_run("w7", Waiting, Some(On::Budget))
        });
        assert_eq!(
            notice_text(&raised, "crew").1,
            "Budget reached: tokens, 2 of 1"
        );
        for notice in [check, budget, send_backs, step_failed, early, raised] {
            let (t, b) = notice_text(&notice, "crew");
            assert!(!t.contains("Inbox") && !b.contains("Inbox"), "{t} / {b}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_step_s_runs_never_notify_that_they_ended_but_their_approvals_do() {
        let mut n = Notifier::default();
        let step_run = |id: &str, status| RuntimeEvent::RunUpdated {
            run: Run {
                workflow_step_run_id: Some("s1".into()),
                ..run(id, status, TriggerKind::Workflow)
            },
        };
        let told = |n: &mut Notifier, id: &str, seq: &[RunStatus]| -> Vec<bool> {
            seq.iter()
                .map(|s| n.decide(&step_run(id, *s)).is_some())
                .collect()
        };
        assert_eq!(
            told(&mut n, "ok", &[Queued, Running, Succeeded]),
            [false, false, false]
        );
        assert_eq!(
            told(&mut n, "bad", &[Queued, Running, Failed]),
            [false, false, false]
        );
        // A chat reply made while the step is open inherits the step: quiet too, even when
        // the watchdog stopped it.
        let reply = RuntimeEvent::RunUpdated {
            run: Run {
                workflow_step_run_id: Some("s1".into()),
                quiet_stopped: true,
                ..run("reply", Failed, TriggerKind::Chat)
            },
        };
        assert!(n.decide(&step_run("reply", Running)).is_none());
        assert_eq!(n.decide(&reply), None);
        // Its approvals notify, and so does a park on a request nobody announced.
        assert!(matches!(
            n.decide(&asked("ask")),
            Some(Notice::ApprovalNeeded { .. })
        ));
        assert_eq!(
            told(&mut n, "park", &[Queued, Running, AwaitingApproval]),
            [false, false, true]
        );
        // The same run outside a workflow notifies as before.
        assert_eq!(
            notified(&mut n, "own", TriggerKind::Manual, &[Running, Succeeded]),
            [false, true]
        );
    }
}
```

```tsx
import { screen, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import type { Dot, Team } from "@/api/types";
import { backend } from "@/test/backend";
import { sample } from "@/test/fixtures";
import { renderApp } from "@/test/render";

const [crew] = sample.teams as [Team, Team];

/// A dot like the sample one, in the account and workspace given.
function dot(id: string, name: string, org_id: string | null = null, workspace_id = "default") {
  return { ...sample.dot, id, name, org_id, workspace_id } satisfies Dot;
}

/// nightly-tests and reviewer (the crew's members), designer (Personal) and ops (an org's).
const DOTS = [
  dot("01J0DOT", "nightly-tests"),
  dot("01J0DOT2", "reviewer"),
  dot("01J0DOT3", "designer"),
  dot("01J0DOT4", "ops", "acme-eng", "platform"),
];

/// The crew's page: its two members and two projects, its four workflows and no run.
function crewPage(dots: Dot[] = DOTS) {
  backend.on("team_get", () => crew);
  backend.on("workflows_list", () => sample.workflows);
  backend.on("list_dots", () => dots);
  backend.on("team_update", () => crew);
  return renderApp(`/teams/${crew.id}`);
}

const members = [
  { dot_id: "01J0DOT", role: "dev agent" },
  { dot_id: "01J0DOT2", role: "" },
];

describe("the team page", () => {
  it("shows the team's name and description, and saves them", async () => {
    const { user } = crewPage();
    expect(await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: "crew", level: 1 })).toBeVisible();
    const name = screen.getByLabelText("Name");
    expect(name).toHaveValue("crew");
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Description")).toHaveValue("Fixes and ships Dots.");
    const save = screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save" });
    expect(save).toBeDisabled();
    await user.clear(name);
    await user.type(name, "core crew ");
    await user.click(save);
    expect(backend.callsTo("team_update")).toEqual([
      {
        id: "01J0TEAM",
        spec: {
          name: "core crew",
          description: "Fixes and ships Dots.",
          members,
          org_id: null,
          workspace_id: "default",
        },
      },
    ]);
  });

  it("saves a member's role when its field is left, and removes one after asking", async () => {
    const { user } = crewPage();
    const section = within(await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Members" }));
    const role = section.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Role of nightly-tests" });
    expect(role).toHaveValue("dev agent");
    await user.clear(role);
    await user.type(role, "lead");
    // Nothing is saved while the person types.
    expect(backend.callsTo("team_update")).toEqual([]);
    await user.tab();
    expect(backend.callsTo("team_update")).toEqual([
      expect.objectContaining({
        spec: expect.objectContaining({
          members: [
            { dot_id: "01J0DOT", role: "lead" },
            { dot_id: "01J0DOT2", role: "" },
          ],
        }),
      }),
    ]);
    await user.click(section.getByRole("button", { name: "Remove reviewer" }));
    const ask = await screen.findByRole("alertdialog", { name: "Remove reviewer from the team?" });
    expect(ask).toHaveTextContent("A workflow whose steps name it cannot start");
    await user.click(within(ask).getByRole("button", { name: "Remove" }));
    expect(backend.callsTo("team_update")[1]).toMatchObject({
      spec: { members: [{ dot_id: "01J0DOT", role: "dev agent" }] },
    });
  });

  it("adds a dot of the team's account and workspace with its role", async () => {
    const { user } = crewPage();
    const section = within(await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Members" }));
    const pick = await section.findByLabelText("Dot");
    // Members and the org's dot are not offered.
    expect(within(pick).getAllByRole("option").map((o) => o.textContent)).toEqual([
      "Choose a dot",
      "designer",
    ]);
    const add = section.getByRole("button", { name: "Add member" });
    expect(add).toBeDisabled();
    await user.selectOptions(pick, "01J0DOT3");
    await user.type(section.getByLabelText("Role"), " designer ");
    await user.click(add);
    expect(backend.callsTo("team_update")[0]).toMatchObject({
      spec: { members: [...members, { dot_id: "01J0DOT3", role: "designer" }] },
    });
  });

  it("says when every dot that may join is a member", async () => {
    crewPage(DOTS.filter((d) => d.id !== "01J0DOT3"));
    expect(await screen.findByText(/Every dot of the team's account and workspace/)).toBeVisible();
  });

  it("lists the projects with their folder, remote and default branch, and opens one", async () => {
    backend.on("open_project_folder", () => null);
    const { user } = crewPage();
    const section = within(await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Projects" }));
    const dots = section.getByRole("listitem", { name: "Dots" });
    expect(dots).toHaveTextContent("C:/Users/u/src/dots");
    expect(dots).toHaveTextContent("Remote origin · default branch main");
    expect(section.getByRole("listitem", { name: "Site" })).toHaveTextContent(
      "Remote upstream · default branch found by git",
    );
    await user.click(within(dots).getByRole("button", { name: "Open the folder of Dots" }));
    expect(backend.callsTo("open_project_folder")).toEqual([
      { projectId: "01J0PRJ", workflowRunId: null, branch: null },
    ]);
  });

  it("adds a project from a picked folder, named after it, and shows a refusal", async () => {
    backend.on("dialog_open", () => "C:\\src\\tools");
    backend.on("project_add", () => {
      throw { kind: "invalid", message: "C:\\src\\tools is not a git repository" };
    });
    const { user } = crewPage();
    const section = within(await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Projects" }));
    await user.click(section.getByRole("button", { name: "Add project" }));
    const dialog = within(await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Add project" }));
    expect(dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "Add project" })).toBeDisabled();
    await user.click(dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "Choose…" }));
    expect(await dialog.findByLabelText("Folder")).toHaveValue("C:\\src\\tools");
    expect(dialog.getByLabelText("Name")).toHaveValue("tools");
    expect(dialog.getByLabelText("Remote")).toHaveValue("origin");
    await user.click(dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "Add project" }));
    expect(await dialog.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("is not a git repository");
    backend.on("project_add", () => crew.projects[0]);
    await user.click(dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "Add project" }));
    expect(backend.callsTo("project_add")).toEqual([
      {
        teamId: "01J0TEAM",
        spec: {
          name: "tools",
          repo_path: "C:\\src\\tools",
          default_branch: null,
          remote: "origin",
        },
      },
      expect.anything(),
    ]);
    expect(screen.queryByRole("dialog", { name: "Add project" })).toBeNull();
  });

  it("edits a project and deletes one after asking", async () => {
    backend.on("project_update", () => crew.projects[1]);
    backend.on("project_delete", () => null);
    const { user } = crewPage();
    const section = within(await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Projects" }));
    await user.click(section.getByRole("button", { name: "Edit Site" }));
    const dialog = within(await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Edit Site" }));
    expect(dialog.getByLabelText("Folder")).toHaveValue("C:/Users/u/src/site");
    expect(dialog.getByLabelText("Remote")).toHaveValue("upstream");
    await user.type(dialog.getByLabelText("Default branch"), "trunk");
    await user.click(dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "Save" }));
    expect(backend.callsTo("project_update")).toEqual([
      {
        id: "01J0PRJ2",
        spec: {
          name: "Site",
          repo_path: "C:/Users/u/src/site",
          default_branch: "trunk",
          remote: "upstream",
        },
      },
    ]);
    await user.click(section.getByRole("button", { name: "Delete Dots" }));
    const ask = await screen.findByRole("alertdialog", { name: "Delete Dots?" });
    expect(ask).toHaveTextContent("Its folder and git repo stay as they are");
    await user.click(within(ask).getByRole("button", { name: "Delete project" }));
    expect(backend.callsTo("project_delete")).toEqual([{ id: "01J0PRJ" }]);
  });

  it("deletes the team after saying its runs in flight are stopped, then lists teams", async () => {
    backend.on("team_delete", () => null);
    const { user, router } = crewPage();
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Delete team" }));
    const ask = await screen.findByRole("alertdialog", { name: "Delete crew?" });
    // Fix and ship has a run in flight.
    expect(ask).toHaveTextContent("Runs in flight are stopped first: 1 run now.");
    await user.click(within(ask).getByRole("button", { name: "Delete team" }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: "Teams" })).toBeVisible();
    expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe("/teams");
    expect(backend.callsTo("team_delete")).toEqual([{ id: "01J0TEAM" }]);
  });

  it("shows why a team cannot be shown", async () => {
    backend.on("team_get", () => {
      throw { kind: "not_found", message: "team nope" };
    });
    backend.on("workflows_list", () => {
      throw { kind: "not_found", message: "team nope" };
    });
    renderApp("/teams/nope");
    expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("team nope");
  });
});
```
