**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/TeamWorkflows.test.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { act, screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import type { Team, Workflow, WorkflowRun } from "@/api/types";
import { backend } from "@/test/backend";
import { sample } from "@/test/fixtures";
import { renderApp } from "@/test/render";

const [crew] = sample.teams as [Team, Team];
const [, pushIt, nightly] = sample.workflows as [Workflow, Workflow, Workflow, Workflow];
const [waiting, , , testRun, budget] = sample.workflowRuns as [
  WorkflowRun,
  WorkflowRun,
  WorkflowRun,
  WorkflowRun,
  WorkflowRun,
];

/// The crew's page with its four workflows, and the runs `runs`.
function crewPage(over: { team?: Team; runs?: WorkflowRun[] } = {}) {
  const team = over.team ?? crew;
  backend.on("team_get", () => team);
  backend.on("workflows_list", () => sample.workflows);
  backend.on("workflow_runs_list", () => over.runs ?? []);
  const view = renderApp(`/teams/${team.id}`);
  // Every path the app went to, the ones it was sent on from included.
  const visited: string[] = [];
  view.router.subscribe((state) => visited.push(state.location.pathname));
  return { ...view, visited };
}

const row = (name: string) =>
  within(screen.getByRole("region", { name: "Workflows" })).getByRole("listitem", { name });

describe("the team page's workflows", () => {
  it("lists each workflow with its trigger, switch, last run and problem", async () => {
    crewPage();
    const section = within(await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Workflows" }));
    const fix = await section.findByRole("listitem", { name: "Fix and ship" });
    expect(within(fix).getByRole("link", { name: "Fix and ship" })).toHaveAttribute(
      "href",
      "/teams/01J0TEAM/workflows/01J0WFL",
    );
    expect(fix).toHaveTextContent("Started from a chat");
    expect(fix).toHaveTextContent("1 run in flight");
    expect(within(fix).getByRole("switch", { name: "Fix and ship is on" })).toBeChecked();
    expect(fix).toHaveTextContent("Last runWaiting");
    expect(within(fix).getByRole("link", { name: "The last run of Fix and ship" })).toHaveAttribute(
      "href",
      "/workflow-runs/01J0WFR",
    );
    expect(within(fix).getByRole("button", { name: "Run Fix and ship now" })).toBeEnabled();

    const push = row("Push it");
    expect(push).toHaveTextContent("Run by hand");
    expect(push).toHaveTextContent('step "Work": 01J0GONE is not a member of the team');
    expect(within(push).getByRole("switch", { name: "Push it is on" })).not.toBeChecked();
    expect(within(push).getByText("Test")).toBeVisible();
    expect(within(push).getByRole("button", { name: "Run Push it now" })).toBeDisabled();

    expect(row("Nightly")).toHaveTextContent("On a schedule");
    expect(row("Nightly")).toHaveTextContent("Not run yet");
    expect(row("On issue")).toHaveTextContent("On a webhook");
  });

  it("turns a workflow off with the rest of it as saved", async () => {
    backend.on("workflow_update", () => ({ ...nightly, enabled: false }));
    const { user } = crewPage();
    await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Workflows" });
    await user.click(within(row("Nightly")).getByRole("switch", { name: "Nightly is on" }));
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_update")).toEqual([
      {
        id: "01J0WFL3",
        spec: { name: "Nightly", definition: nightly.definition, enabled: false },
      },
    ]);
  });

  it("makes a workflow done by the first member, off, and opens it in the builder", async () => {
    const made: Workflow = { ...pushIt, id: "01J0WFL9", name: "New workflow", problem: null };
    backend.on("workflow_create", () => made);
    const { user, visited } = crewPage();
    await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Workflows" });
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "New workflow" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(visited).toContain("/teams/01J0TEAM/workflows/01J0WFL9"));
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_create")).toEqual([
      {
        teamId: "01J0TEAM",
        spec: {
          name: "New workflow",
          enabled: false,
          definition: {
            trigger: { kind: "manual" },
            steps: [
              {
                id: "work",
                name: "Work",
                kind: "agent",
                member: "01J0DOT",
                job: "Work on the task.",
                can_send_back_to: [],
                outcomes: [],
                delegate: null,
              },
            ],
            budget: { max_runs: 10, max_tokens: 1_500_000, max_minutes: 120, max_send_backs: 3 },
          },
        },
      },
    ]);
  });

  it("asks for a member before a new workflow, and for a project before a run", async () => {
    crewPage({ team: { ...crew, members: [], projects: [] } });
    const section = within(await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Workflows" }));
    expect(section.getByRole("button", { name: "New workflow" })).toBeDisabled();
    expect(within(row("Fix and ship")).getByRole("button", { name: "Run Fix and ship now" }))
      .toBeDisabled();
  });

  it("runs a workflow with a task on a project, then opens the run's page", async () => {
    backend.on("workflow_run_start", () => waiting);
    const { user, visited } = crewPage();
    await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Workflows" });
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Run Fix and ship now" }));
    const dialog = within(await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Run Fix and ship" }));
    const start = dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "Start run" });
    expect(start).toBeDisabled();
    expect(dialog.getByLabelText("Project")).toHaveValue("01J0PRJ");
    await user.type(dialog.getByLabelText("Task"), "  Fix the tray icon.  ");
    await user.selectOptions(dialog.getByLabelText("Project"), "01J0PRJ2");
    await user.click(start);
    await waitFor(() => expect(visited).toContain("/workflow-runs/01J0WFR"));
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_run_start")).toEqual([
      { workflowId: "01J0WFL", projectId: "01J0PRJ2", task: "Fix the tray icon.", test: false },
    ]);
  });

  it("keeps the run dialog open with the core's refusal", async () => {
    backend.on("workflow_run_start", () => {
      throw { kind: "invalid", message: "C:/Users/u/src/dots is not a git repository" };
    });
    const { user } = crewPage();
    await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Workflows" });
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Run Fix and ship now" }));
    const dialog = within(await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Run Fix and ship" }));
    await user.type(dialog.getByLabelText("Task"), "Fix it.");
    await user.click(dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "Start run" }));
    expect(await dialog.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("is not a git repository");
  });
});

describe("the team page's recent runs", () => {
  it("lists the newest runs with status, task, workflow, project and spend", async () => {
    crewPage({ runs: [waiting, testRun, budget] });
    const section = within(await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Recent runs" }));
    const rows = await section.findAllByRole("row");
    // The header, then one row per run, newest first as the core answers.
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(4);
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_runs_list")).toContainEqual({
      filter: { team_id: "01J0TEAM", limit: 20 },
    });
    const first = within(rows[1]!);
    expect(rows[1]).toHaveTextContent("Needs your check");
    expect(first.getByRole("link", { name: "The tray icon is invisible on a light taskbar." }))
      .toHaveAttribute("href", "/workflow-runs/01J0WFR");
    expect(rows[1]).toHaveTextContent("Fix and ship");
    expect(rows[1]).toHaveTextContent("Dots");
    expect(rows[1]).toHaveTextContent("3 runs · 120k tokens");
    expect(rows[2]).toHaveTextContent("A step failed");
    expect(within(rows[2]!).getByText("Test")).toBeVisible();
    // Its workflow and project were deleted since; the run keeps the project's name.
    expect(rows[3]).toHaveTextContent("Hit its budget");
    expect(rows[3]).toHaveTextContent("A deleted workflow");
    expect(rows[3]).toHaveTextContent("Site");
  });

  it("says when the team has not run", async () => {
    crewPage();
    const section = within(await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Recent runs" }));
    expect(await section.findByText("No runs yet.")).toBeVisible();
  });

  it("follows a workflow run's event: the runs and the workflows are read again", async () => {
    const { listening } = crewPage({ runs: [waiting] });
    await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Recent runs" });
    await listening();
    const runs = backend.callsTo("workflow_runs_list").length;
    const workflows = backend.callsTo("workflows_list").length;
    backend.on("workflow_runs_list", () => [{ ...waiting, status: "succeeded", waiting_on: null }]);
    act(() =>
      backend.emit("runtime", {
        type: "workflow_run_updated",
        run: { ...waiting, status: "succeeded", waiting_on: null },
      }),
    );
    const section = within(screen.getByRole("region", { name: "Recent runs" }));
    expect(await section.findByText("Succeeded")).toBeVisible();
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_runs_list").length).toBe(runs + 1);
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflows_list").length).toBe(workflows + 1);
  });
});
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/TeamsScreen.test.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { screen, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import type { Team } from "@/api/types";
import { backend } from "@/test/backend";
import { sample } from "@/test/fixtures";
import { renderApp } from "@/test/render";

const [crew, platform] = sample.teams as [Team, Team];

describe("the Teams screen", () => {
  it("sits on the rail after Library, before Settings", async () => {
    renderApp("/teams");
    const nav = await screen.findByRole("navigation", { name: "Main" });
    const labels = within(nav)
      .getAllByRole("link")
      .map((link) => link.getAttribute("aria-label"));
    expect(labels).toEqual(["Home", "Inbox", "Apps", "Library", "Teams", "Settings"]);
    expect(within(nav).getByRole("link", { name: "Teams" })).toHaveAttribute("href", "/teams");
    expect(within(nav).getByRole("link", { name: "Teams" })).toHaveAttribute(
      "aria-current",
      "page",
    );
    expect(await screen.findByText(/No teams yet/)).toBeVisible();
  });

  it("lists each team with its description, members and projects, opening its page", async () => {
    backend.on("teams_list", () => [crew, platform]);
    renderApp("/teams");
    const row = await screen.findByRole("listitem", { name: "crew" });
    expect(row).toHaveTextContent("Fixes and ships Dots.");
    expect(row).toHaveTextContent("2 members · 2 projects");
    expect(within(row).getByRole("link", { name: "crew" })).toHaveAttribute(
      "href",
      "/teams/01J0TEAM",
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole("listitem", { name: "platform" })).toHaveTextContent(
      "0 members · 0 projects",
    );
    expect(screen.queryByText(/No teams yet/)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("makes a team in the account and workspace the person works in, then opens it", async () => {
    const ops: Team = {
      ...crew,
      id: "01J0TEAM9",
      name: "ops",
      description: "Runs the servers.",
      members: [],
      projects: [],
    };
    backend.on("team_create", () => ops);
    backend.on("team_get", () => ops);
    backend.on("workflows_list", () => []);
    const { user, router } = renderApp("/teams");
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "New team" }));
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "New team" });
    const create = within(dialog).getByRole("button", { name: "Create team" });
    expect(create).toBeDisabled();
    await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText("Name"), " ops ");
    await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText("Description"), "Runs the servers.");
    await user.click(create);
    expect(await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: "ops" })).toBeVisible();
    expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe("/teams/01J0TEAM9");
    expect(backend.callsTo("team_create")).toEqual([
      {
        spec: {
          name: "ops",
          description: "Runs the servers.",
          members: [],
          org_id: null,
          workspace_id: "default",
        },
      },
    ]);
  });

  it("makes a team in an org's workspace when the person works there", async () => {
    backend.on("scope_get", () => ({ account: "acme-eng", workspace: "platform" }));
    backend.on("team_create", () => platform);
    backend.on("team_get", () => platform);
    backend.on("workflows_list", () => []);
    const { user } = renderApp("/teams");
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "New team" }));
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "New team" });
    await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText("Name"), "platform");
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole("button", { name: "Create team" }));
    await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: "platform" });
    expect(backend.callsTo("team_create")[0]).toMatchObject({
      spec: { org_id: "acme-eng", workspace_id: "platform" },
    });
  });

  it("keeps the dialog open with the core's refusal", async () => {
    backend.on("team_create", () => {
      throw { kind: "conflict", message: 'a team named "crew" exists' };
    });
    const { user } = renderApp("/teams");
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "New team" }));
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "New team" });
    await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText("Name"), "crew");
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole("button", { name: "Create team" }));
    expect(await within(dialog).findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent(
      'a team named "crew" exists',
    );
    expect(within(dialog).getByRole("button", { name: "Create team" })).toBeEnabled();
  });
});
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/format.test.ts`:**

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { Step, WorkflowSpec } from "@/api/types";

import {
  formatSpend,
  newWorkflowSpec,
  taskLine,
  uniqueName,
  workflowRunStatusLabel,
} from "./format";

describe("the team page's words", () => {
  it("names a workflow run by its task's first line", () => {
    expect(taskLine("  Fix the tray icon.\r\nIt is invisible.")).toBe("Fix the tray icon.");
    expect(taskLine("One line")).toBe("One line");
    expect(taskLine("")).toBe("");
  });

  it("says what a run spent, and what a waiting run waits for when it is known", () => {
    expect(formatSpend({ spent_runs: 1, spent_tokens: 950 })).toBe("1 run · 950 tokens");
    expect(formatSpend({ spent_runs: 4, spent_tokens: 182_000 })).toBe("4 runs · 182k tokens");
    expect(workflowRunStatusLabel({ status: "waiting", waiting_on: "check" })).toBe(
      "Needs your check",
    );
    expect(workflowRunStatusLabel({ status: "waiting", waiting_on: "send_backs" })).toBe(
      "Hit its send-back limit",
    );
    expect(workflowRunStatusLabel({ status: "waiting" })).toBe("Waiting");
    expect(workflowRunStatusLabel({ status: "cancelled", waiting_on: null })).toBe("Cancelled");
  });

  it("gives a new workflow a name the team has not used, without case", () => {
    expect(uniqueName("New workflow", ["Nightly"])).toBe("New workflow");
    expect(uniqueName("New workflow", ["new workflow ", "New workflow 2"])).toBe("New workflow 3");
  });

  it("makes a new workflow the UI's own schemas read", () => {
    const spec = newWorkflowSpec("New workflow", "01J0DOT");
    expect(WorkflowSpec.parse(spec)).toEqual(spec);
    expect(Step.parse(spec.definition.steps[0])).toMatchObject({
      kind: "agent",
      member: "01J0DOT",
    });
  });
});
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/RunNowDialog.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router";

import { commands } from "@/api/commands";
import type { Project, Workflow } from "@/api/types";
import { Field } from "@/components/Field";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { NativeSelect, NativeSelectOption } from "@/components/ui/native-select";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { errorMessage } from "@/lib/notify";
import { invalidate } from "@/lib/query";

/// "Run now", or "Test run" with `test` (teams spec section 5.1): the task and the project, then
/// the run starts on the saved workflow and its page opens.
export function RunNowDialog(props: {
  workflow: Workflow;
  projects: Project[];
  test?: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [task, setTask] = useState("");
  const [projectId, setProjectId] = useState(props.projects[0]?.id ?? "");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string>();
  const test = props.test ?? false;

  async function start() {
    setBusy(true);
    setError(undefined);
    try {
      const run = await commands.workflowRunStart(props.workflow.id, projectId, task.trim(), test);
      invalidate(qc, ["workflow-runs"]);
      navigate(`/workflow-runs/${run.id}`);
    } catch (e) {
      setError(errorMessage(e));
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  const missing =
    task.trim() === ""
      ? "Say what the task is first."
      : projectId === ""
        ? "Add a project to the team first."
        : undefined;
  return (
    <Dialog open onOpenChange={(next) => !next && props.onClose()}>
      <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>
            {test ? "Test run" : "Run"} {props.workflow.name}
          </DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>
            {test
              ? "A real run of the saved workflow, except that its deploys push nothing: the branches are kept for you to look at."
              : "The saved workflow runs on the project, step by step, from the task."}
          </DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>
        <Field label="Task" htmlFor="run-task">
          <Textarea
            id="run-task"
            rows={5}
            placeholder="What should the team do?"
            value={task}
            onChange={(e) => setTask(e.target.value)}
          />
        </Field>
        <Field label="Project" htmlFor="run-project">
          <NativeSelect
            id="run-project"
            value={projectId}
            onChange={(e) => setProjectId(e.target.value)}
          >
            {props.projects.map((project) => (
              <NativeSelectOption key={project.id} value={project.id}>
                {project.name}
              </NativeSelectOption>
            ))}
          </NativeSelect>
        </Field>
        {error && (
          <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">
            {error}
          </p>
        )}
        <DialogFooter>
          <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={props.onClose}>
            Cancel
          </Button>
          <Button
            type="button"
            disabled={busy || missing !== undefined}
            title={missing}
            onClick={() => void start()}
          >
            {test ? "Start test run" : "Start run"}
          </Button>
        </DialogFooter>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/TeamMembers.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

import { commands } from "@/api/commands";
import type { MemberSpec, Team, TeamMember } from "@/api/types";
import { ConfirmButton } from "@/components/ConfirmButton";
import { ErrorNote } from "@/components/ErrorNote";
import { Field } from "@/components/Field";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { NativeSelect, NativeSelectOption } from "@/components/ui/native-select";
import { notifyError } from "@/lib/notify";
import { invalidate, keys } from "@/lib/query";
import { PERSONAL, inScope } from "@/lib/scope";

import { teamSpec } from "./format";
import { TeamSection } from "./parts";

/// The team's members (teams spec section 10.1): each dot with its role, which the person
/// edits in place, and "Remove"; then a dot of the team's account and workspace to add.
export function TeamMembers({ team }: { team: Team }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const dots = useQuery({ queryKey: keys.dots, queryFn: commands.listDots });
  const save = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (members: MemberSpec[]) =>
      commands.teamUpdate(team.id, teamSpec(team, { members })),
    onSuccess: () => invalidate(qc, keys.teams),
    onError: notifyError,
  });
  const members = teamSpec(team).members;
  // A member from elsewhere is listed in the Inbox as a problem, so none is offered.
  const scope = { account: team.org_id ?? PERSONAL, workspace: team.workspace_id };
  const candidates = (dots.data ?? []).filter(
    (dot) => inScope(dot, scope) && !members.some((m) => m.dot_id === dot.id),
  );
  return (
    <TeamSection id="team-members" title="Members">
      {team.members.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="text-muted-foreground">No members yet. Add the dots that work here.</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          {team.members.map((member) => (
            <MemberRow
              key={member.dot_id}
              member={member}
              busy={save.isPending}
              onRole={(role) =>
                save.mutate(members.map((m) => (m.dot_id === member.dot_id ? { ...m, role } : m)))
              }
              onRemove={() => save.mutate(members.filter((m) => m.dot_id !== member.dot_id))}
            />
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
      {dots.isError && <ErrorNote error={dots.error} />}
      {dots.isSuccess && (
        <AddMember
          candidates={candidates}
          busy={save.isPending}
          onAdd={(member) => save.mutate([...members, member])}
        />
      )}
    </TeamSection>
  );
}

/// A member: its name, its role (saved when the field is left or Enter is pressed) and Remove.
function MemberRow(props: {
  member: TeamMember;
  busy: boolean;
  onRole: (role: string) => void;
  onRemove: () => void;
}) {
  const { member } = props;
  const [role, setRole] = useState(member.role);
  useEffect(() => setRole(member.role), [member.role]);
  const commit = () => {
    if (role.trim() !== member.role) props.onRole(role.trim());
  };
  return (
    <li aria-label={member.dot_name} className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border p-3">
      <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate font-medium">{member.dot_name}</span>
      <Input
        aria-label={`Role of ${member.dot_name}`}
        placeholder="Role, e.g. dev agent"
        className="w-56"
        value={role}
        onChange={(e) => setRole(e.target.value)}
        onBlur={commit}
        onKeyDown={(e) => {
          if (e.key === "Enter") commit();
        }}
      />
      <ConfirmButton
        title={`Remove ${member.dot_name} from the team?`}
        description="The dot stays. A workflow whose steps name it cannot start until you change it."
        confirmLabel="Remove"
        ariaLabel={`Remove ${member.dot_name}`}
        disabled={props.busy}
        onConfirm={props.onRemove}
      >
        Remove
      </ConfirmButton>
    </li>
  );
}

/// A dot to add and its role.
function AddMember(props: {
  candidates: { id: string; name: string }[];
  busy: boolean;
  onAdd: (member: MemberSpec) => void;
}) {
  const [dotId, setDotId] = useState("");
  const [role, setRole] = useState("");
  // A dot added, or gone, is no longer offered.
  const chosen = props.candidates.some((d) => d.id === dotId) ? dotId : "";
  if (props.candidates.length === 0) {
    return (
      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
        Every dot of the team&apos;s account and workspace is a member.
      </p>
    );
  }
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-2">
      <Field label="Dot" htmlFor="member-dot">
        <NativeSelect id="member-dot" value={chosen} onChange={(e) => setDotId(e.target.value)}>
          <NativeSelectOption value="">Choose a dot</NativeSelectOption>
          {props.candidates.map((dot) => (
            <NativeSelectOption key={dot.id} value={dot.id}>
              {dot.name}
            </NativeSelectOption>
          ))}
        </NativeSelect>
      </Field>
      <Field label="Role" htmlFor="member-role">
        <Input
          id="member-role"
          className="w-56"
          placeholder="e.g. dev agent"
          value={role}
          onChange={(e) => setRole(e.target.value)}
        />
      </Field>
      <Button
        size="sm"
        disabled={chosen === "" || props.busy}
        onClick={() => {
          props.onAdd({ dot_id: chosen, role: role.trim() });
          setDotId("");
          setRole("");
        }}
      >
        Add member
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/TeamProjects.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { open } from "@tauri-apps/plugin-dialog";
import { FolderOpen, Plus } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";

import { commands } from "@/api/commands";
import type { Project, ProjectSpec, Team } from "@/api/types";
import { ConfirmButton } from "@/components/ConfirmButton";
import { Field } from "@/components/Field";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { errorMessage, notifyError } from "@/lib/notify";
import { invalidate, keys } from "@/lib/query";
import { ItemRow } from "@/screens/library/parts";

import { TeamSection } from "./parts";

/// The team's projects (teams spec section 10.1): each local git repo with its remote and
/// default branch, to open, edit or delete; and "Add project".
export function TeamProjects({ team }: { team: Team }) {
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<Project | "new" | null>(null);
  return (
    <TeamSection
      id="team-projects"
      title="Projects"
      actions={
        <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={() => setEditing("new")}>
          <Plus aria-hidden /> Add project
        </Button>
      }
    >
      {team.projects.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="text-muted-foreground">
          No projects yet. A project is a git repo on this PC that the team works in.
        </p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          {team.projects.map((project) => (
            <ProjectRow key={project.id} project={project} onEdit={() => setEditing(project)} />
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
      {editing !== null && (
        <ProjectDialog
          teamId={team.id}
          project={editing === "new" ? null : editing}
          onClose={() => setEditing(null)}
        />
      )}
    </TeamSection>
  );
}

function ProjectRow({ project, onEdit }: { project: Project; onEdit: () => void }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const name = project.name;
  const remove = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => commands.projectDelete(project.id),
    onSuccess: () => invalidate(qc, keys.teams),
    onError: notifyError,
  });
  return (
    <ItemRow
      label={name}
      actions={
        <>
          <Button
            size="sm"
            variant="ghost"
            aria-label={`Open the folder of ${name}`}
            title="Open the folder"
            onClick={() => commands.openProjectFolder(project.id).catch(notifyError)}
          >
            <FolderOpen aria-hidden />
          </Button>
          <Button size="sm" variant="outline" aria-label={`Edit ${name}`} onClick={onEdit}>
            Edit
          </Button>
          <ConfirmButton
            title={`Delete ${name}?`}
            description="Its folder and git repo stay as they are: only the team forgets it. A schedule or webhook that names it cannot start until you change it."
            confirmLabel="Delete project"
            ariaLabel={`Delete ${name}`}
            disabled={remove.isPending}
            onConfirm={() => remove.mutate()}
          >
            Delete
          </ConfirmButton>
        </>
      }
    >
      <span className="font-medium">{name}</span>
      <span className="font-mono text-xs break-all text-muted-foreground">
        {project.repo_path}
      </span>
      <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
        Remote {project.remote} ·{" "}
        {project.default_branch === null
          ? "default branch found by git"
          : `default branch ${project.default_branch}`}
      </span>
    </ItemRow>
  );
}

/// The last part of a folder's path: the name a project picked from it starts with.
function folderName(path: string): string {
  return path.replace(/[\\/]+$/, "").split(/[\\/]/).at(-1) ?? "";
}

/// "Add project" and "Edit": the name, the repo folder (picked or typed), the remote and the
/// default branch. The core refuses a folder that is not a git repo, with its reason.
function ProjectDialog(props: { teamId: string; project: Project | null; onClose: () => void }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const { project } = props;
  const [name, setName] = useState(project?.name ?? "");
  const [folder, setFolder] = useState(project?.repo_path ?? "");
  const [remote, setRemote] = useState(project?.remote ?? "origin");
  const [branch, setBranch] = useState(project?.default_branch ?? "");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string>();

  function pick() {
    open({ directory: true, title: "Choose the project's git repo" }).then((picked) => {
      if (typeof picked !== "string") return;
      setFolder(picked);
      if (name.trim() === "") setName(folderName(picked));
    }, notifyError);
  }

  async function save() {
    const spec: ProjectSpec = {
      name: name.trim(),
      repo_path: folder.trim(),
      default_branch: branch.trim() === "" ? null : branch.trim(),
      remote: remote.trim() === "" ? "origin" : remote.trim(),
    };
    setBusy(true);
    setError(undefined);
    try {
      if (project) await commands.projectUpdate(project.id, spec);
      else await commands.projectAdd(props.teamId, spec);
      invalidate(qc, keys.teams);
      props.onClose();
    } catch (e) {
      setError(errorMessage(e));
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  const missing =
    name.trim() === ""
      ? "Give the project a name first."
      : folder.trim() === ""
        ? "Choose its folder first."
        : undefined;
  return (
    <Dialog open onOpenChange={(next) => !next && props.onClose()}>
      <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>{project ? `Edit ${project.name}` : "Add project"}</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>
            A git repo on this PC. Each workflow run works in worktrees of it, on its own
            branches.
          </DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>
        <Field label="Name" htmlFor="project-name">
          <Input id="project-name" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} />
        </Field>
        <Field label="Folder" htmlFor="project-folder">
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <Input
              id="project-folder"
              className="font-mono text-xs"
              value={folder}
              onChange={(e) => setFolder(e.target.value)}
            />
            <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={pick}>
              Choose…
            </Button>
          </div>
        </Field>
        <Field
          label="Remote"
          htmlFor="project-remote"
          hint="Where push and pull request steps push."
        >
          <Input id="project-remote" value={remote} onChange={(e) => setRemote(e.target.value)} />
        </Field>
        <Field
          label="Default branch"
          htmlFor="project-branch"
          hint="Leave it empty to use the repo's own, as git finds it."
        >
          <Input
            id="project-branch"
            placeholder="main"
            value={branch}
            onChange={(e) => setBranch(e.target.value)}
          />
        </Field>
        {error && (
          <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">
            {error}
          </p>
        )}
        <DialogFooter>
          <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={props.onClose}>
            Cancel
          </Button>
          <Button
            type="button"
            disabled={busy || missing !== undefined}
            title={missing}
            onClick={() => void save()}
          >
            {project ? "Save" : "Add project"}
          </Button>
        </DialogFooter>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/TeamRuns.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Link } from "react-router";

import { commands } from "@/api/commands";
import type { Workflow } from "@/api/types";
import { ErrorNote } from "@/components/ErrorNote";
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeader,
  TableRow,
} from "@/components/ui/table";
import { localDateTime } from "@/lib/format";
import { keys } from "@/lib/query";

import { formatSpend, taskLine } from "./format";
import { TeamSection } from "./parts";
import { TestBadge, WorkflowRunBadge } from "./WorkflowRunBadge";

/// How many of the team's newest workflow runs its page lists.
export const RECENT_RUNS = 20;

/// The team's newest workflow runs (teams spec section 10.1): status, task, workflow, project
/// and spend, each opening its run page. Live: a workflow run's event marks the list stale.
export function TeamRuns({ teamId, workflows }: { teamId: string; workflows: Workflow[] }) {
  const filter = { team_id: teamId, limit: RECENT_RUNS };
  const runs = useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.workflowRuns(filter),
    queryFn: () => commands.workflowRunsList(filter),
  });
  // Blank while the workflows load.
  const workflowName = (id: string | null) =>
    id === null ? "A deleted workflow" : (workflows.find((w) => w.id === id)?.name ?? "");
  return (
    <TeamSection id="team-runs" title="Recent runs">
      {runs.isError && <ErrorNote error={runs.error} />}
      {runs.data?.length === 0 && <p className="text-muted-foreground">No runs yet.</p>}
      {(runs.data ?? []).length > 0 && (
        <Table>
          <TableHeader>
            <TableRow>
              <TableHead>Status</TableHead>
              <TableHead>Task</TableHead>
              <TableHead>Workflow</TableHead>
              <TableHead>Project</TableHead>
              <TableHead>Spent</TableHead>
              <TableHead>Started</TableHead>
            </TableRow>
          </TableHeader>
          <TableBody>
            {runs.data!.map((run) => (
              <TableRow key={run.id}>
                <TableCell>
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                    <WorkflowRunBadge status={run.status} waitingOn={run.waiting_on} />
                    {run.test && <TestBadge />}
                  </span>
                </TableCell>
                <TableCell className="max-w-sm truncate">
                  <Link to={`/workflow-runs/${run.id}`} className="hover:underline">
                    {taskLine(run.task)}
                  </Link>
                </TableCell>
                <TableCell>{workflowName(run.workflow_id)}</TableCell>
                <TableCell>{run.project.name}</TableCell>
                <TableCell className="tabular-nums">{formatSpend(run)}</TableCell>
                <TableCell>{localDateTime(run.started_at)}</TableCell>
              </TableRow>
            ))}
          </TableBody>
        </Table>
      )}
    </TeamSection>
  );
}
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/TeamScreen.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router";

import { commands } from "@/api/commands";
import type { Team } from "@/api/types";
import { ConfirmButton } from "@/components/ConfirmButton";
import { ErrorNote } from "@/components/ErrorNote";
import { Field } from "@/components/Field";
import { Page } from "@/components/Page";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { notifyError } from "@/lib/notify";
import { invalidate, keys } from "@/lib/query";
import { plural } from "@/screens/library/parts";

import { teamSpec } from "./format";
import { TeamMembers } from "./TeamMembers";
import { TeamProjects } from "./TeamProjects";
import { TeamRuns } from "./TeamRuns";
import { TeamWorkflows } from "./TeamWorkflows";

/// A team's page (teams spec section 10.1): its name and description, members, projects,
/// workflows and recent workflow runs, and "Delete team".
export function TeamScreen() {
  const { teamId = "" } = useParams();
  const team = useQuery({ queryKey: keys.team(teamId), queryFn: () => commands.teamGet(teamId) });
  const workflows = useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.workflows(teamId),
    queryFn: () => commands.workflowsList(teamId),
  });
  if (team.isPending) return <Page title="Team" />;
  if (team.isError) {
    return (
      <Page title="Team">
        <ErrorNote error={team.error} />
      </Page>
    );
  }
  const inFlight = (workflows.data ?? []).reduce((n, w) => n + w.runs_in_flight, 0);
  return (
    <Page title={team.data.name} actions={<DeleteTeam team={team.data} inFlight={inFlight} />}>
      <TeamDetails team={team.data} />
      <TeamMembers team={team.data} />
      <TeamProjects team={team.data} />
      {workflows.isError ? (
        <ErrorNote error={workflows.error} />
      ) : (
        <TeamWorkflows team={team.data} workflows={workflows.data} />
      )}
      <TeamRuns teamId={teamId} workflows={workflows.data ?? []} />
    </Page>
  );
}

/// The team's name and description, saved together.
function TeamDetails({ team }: { team: Team }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const [name, setName] = useState(team.name);
  const [description, setDescription] = useState(team.description);
  // What the team says now, when it changes elsewhere.
  useEffect(() => {
    setName(team.name);
    setDescription(team.description);
  }, [team.name, team.description]);
  const save = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () =>
      commands.teamUpdate(
        team.id,
        teamSpec(team, { name: name.trim(), description: description.trim() }),
      ),
    onSuccess: () => invalidate(qc, keys.teams),
    onError: notifyError,
  });
  const changed = name.trim() !== team.name || description.trim() !== team.description;
  return (
    <div className="flex max-w-2xl flex-col gap-3">
      <Field label="Name" htmlFor="team-name">
        <Input id="team-name" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} />
      </Field>
      <Field label="Description" htmlFor="team-description">
        <Textarea
          id="team-description"
          rows={2}
          value={description}
          onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
        />
      </Field>
      <div>
        <Button
          size="sm"
          disabled={!changed || name.trim() === "" || save.isPending}
          onClick={() => save.mutate()}
        >
          Save
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/// "Delete team": asks first, saying that its runs in flight are stopped, then goes back to the
/// list of teams.
function DeleteTeam({ team, inFlight }: { team: Team; inFlight: number }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const remove = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => commands.teamDelete(team.id),
    onSuccess: () => {
      navigate("/teams");
      invalidate(qc, keys.teams);
      invalidate(qc, ["workflow-runs"]);
      invalidate(qc, keys.inbox);
    },
    onError: notifyError,
  });
  return (
    <ConfirmButton
      title={`Delete ${team.name}?`}
      description={
        `Its projects, workflows and workflow runs are deleted with it. Runs in flight are ` +
        `stopped first${inFlight > 0 ? `: ${plural(inFlight, "run")} now` : ""}. The dots and ` +
        `the project folders stay.`
      }
      confirmLabel="Delete team"
      disabled={remove.isPending}
      onConfirm={() => remove.mutate()}
    >
      Delete team
    </ConfirmButton>
  );
}
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/TeamWorkflows.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Plus } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate } from "react-router";

import { commands } from "@/api/commands";
import type { Team, Workflow } from "@/api/types";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch";
import { relativeTime } from "@/lib/format";
import { notifyError } from "@/lib/notify";
import { invalidate, keys } from "@/lib/query";
import { ItemRow, plural } from "@/screens/library/parts";

import { newWorkflowSpec, TRIGGER_LABEL, uniqueName } from "./format";
import { TeamSection } from "./parts";
import { RunNowDialog } from "./RunNowDialog";
import { TestBadge, WorkflowRunBadge } from "./WorkflowRunBadge";

/// The builder's route for a workflow of a team.
export const builderPath = (teamId: string, workflowId: string) =>
  `/teams/${teamId}/workflows/${workflowId}`;

/// The team's workflows (teams spec section 10.1): each with its trigger, its switch, its last
/// run, why it cannot start (its `problem`) and "Run now"; and "New workflow", which makes one
/// and opens it in the builder. `workflows` is undefined while they load.
export function TeamWorkflows({ team, workflows }: { team: Team; workflows?: Workflow[] }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [running, setRunning] = useState<Workflow | null>(null);
  const first = team.members[0];
  const create = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (member: string) => {
      const name = uniqueName("New workflow", (workflows ?? []).map((w) => w.name));
      return commands.workflowCreate(team.id, newWorkflowSpec(name, member));
    },
    onSuccess: (workflow) => {
      invalidate(qc, keys.workflows(team.id));
      navigate(builderPath(team.id, workflow.id));
    },
    onError: notifyError,
  });
  return (
    <TeamSection
      id="team-workflows"
      title="Workflows"
      actions={
        <Button
          size="sm"
          variant="outline"
          disabled={first === undefined || workflows === undefined || create.isPending}
          title={
            first === undefined ? "Add a member first: a workflow's steps need one." : undefined
          }
          onClick={() => first && create.mutate(first.dot_id)}
        >
          <Plus aria-hidden /> New workflow
        </Button>
      }
    >
      {workflows?.length === 0 && (
        <p className="text-muted-foreground">
          No workflows yet. A workflow says which member does what, in which order, and where
          you check the work before it ships.
        </p>
      )}
      {(workflows ?? []).length > 0 && (
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          {workflows!.map((workflow) => (
            <WorkflowRow
              key={workflow.id}
              workflow={workflow}
              team={team}
              onRun={() => setRunning(workflow)}
            />
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
      {running && (
        <RunNowDialog
          workflow={running}
          projects={team.projects}
          onClose={() => setRunning(null)}
        />
      )}
    </TeamSection>
  );
}

/// Why "Run now" is off for `workflow` of `team`, or undefined when it may start.
function cannotRun(workflow: Workflow, team: Team): string | undefined {
  if (workflow.problem !== null) return `It cannot start: ${workflow.problem}`;
  if (!workflow.enabled) return "Turn it on first.";
  if (team.projects.length === 0) return "Add a project to the team first.";
  return undefined;
}

function WorkflowRow(props: { workflow: Workflow; team: Team; onRun: () => void }) {
  const { workflow, team } = props;
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const name = workflow.name;
  const toggle = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (enabled: boolean) =>
      commands.workflowUpdate(workflow.id, {
        name: workflow.name,
        definition: workflow.definition,
        enabled,
      }),
    onSuccess: () => {
      invalidate(qc, keys.workflows(team.id));
      invalidate(qc, keys.workflow(workflow.id));
    },
    onError: notifyError,
  });
  const blocked = cannotRun(workflow, team);
  const last = workflow.last_run;
  return (
    <ItemRow
      label={name}
      actions={
        <>
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
            <Switch
              aria-label={`${name} is on`}
              checked={workflow.enabled}
              disabled={toggle.isPending}
              onCheckedChange={(on) => toggle.mutate(on)}
            />
            On
          </label>
          <Button
            size="sm"
            variant="outline"
            aria-label={`Run ${name} now`}
            disabled={blocked !== undefined}
            title={blocked}
            onClick={props.onRun}
          >
            Run now
          </Button>
        </>
      }
    >
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <Link to={builderPath(team.id, workflow.id)} className="font-medium hover:underline">
          {name}
        </Link>
        <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          {TRIGGER_LABEL[workflow.definition.trigger.kind]}
        </span>
        {workflow.runs_in_flight > 0 && (
          <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            {plural(workflow.runs_in_flight, "run")} in flight
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
      {workflow.problem !== null && (
        <span className="text-xs text-destructive">{workflow.problem}</span>
      )}
      <span className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
        {last === null ? (
          "Not run yet"
        ) : (
          <>
            Last run
            <WorkflowRunBadge status={last.status} />
            {last.test && <TestBadge />}
            <Link
              to={`/workflow-runs/${last.id}`}
              aria-label={`The last run of ${name}`}
              className="hover:underline"
            >
              {relativeTime(last.started_at)}
            </Link>
          </>
        )}
      </span>
    </ItemRow>
  );
}
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/TeamsScreen.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Plus, UsersRound } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate } from "react-router";

import { commands } from "@/api/commands";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/EmptyState";
import { ErrorNote } from "@/components/ErrorNote";
import { Field } from "@/components/Field";
import { Page } from "@/components/Page";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { errorMessage } from "@/lib/notify";
import { invalidate, keys } from "@/lib/query";
import { PERSONAL, useCurrentScope } from "@/lib/scope";
import { ItemRow, plural } from "@/screens/library/parts";

/// The Teams screen (teams spec section 10.1): every team with its members and projects, each
/// opening its page, and "New team".
export function TeamsScreen() {
  const teams = useQuery({ queryKey: keys.teams, queryFn: commands.teamsList });
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);
  const actions = (
    <Button size="sm" onClick={() => setCreating(true)}>
      <Plus aria-hidden /> New team
    </Button>
  );
  return (
    <Page title="Teams" actions={actions}>
      {teams.isError && <ErrorNote error={teams.error} />}
      {teams.data?.length === 0 && (
        <EmptyState icon={UsersRound}>
          No teams yet. A team is dots that work on a project together, step by step, as a
          workflow says: make one with New team.
        </EmptyState>
      )}
      {(teams.data ?? []).length > 0 && (
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          {teams.data!.map((team) => (
            <ItemRow key={team.id} label={team.name}>
              <Link to={`/teams/${team.id}`} className="font-medium hover:underline">
                {team.name}
              </Link>
              {team.description && (
                <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{team.description}</span>
              )}
              <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                {plural(team.members.length, "member")} · {plural(team.projects.length, "project")}
              </span>
            </ItemRow>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
      {creating && <NewTeamDialog onClose={() => setCreating(false)} />}
    </Page>
  );
}

/// "New team": its name and description, made in the account and workspace the person works
/// in now, then opened.
function NewTeamDialog(props: { onClose: () => void }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const scope = useCurrentScope();
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [description, setDescription] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string>();

  async function create() {
    if (!scope) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setError(undefined);
    try {
      const team = await commands.teamCreate({
        name: name.trim(),
        description: description.trim(),
        members: [],
        org_id: scope.account === PERSONAL ? null : scope.account,
        workspace_id: scope.workspace,
      });
      invalidate(qc, keys.teams);
      navigate(`/teams/${team.id}`);
    } catch (e) {
      setError(errorMessage(e));
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <Dialog open onOpenChange={(next) => !next && props.onClose()}>
      <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>New team</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>
            Add its members, projects and workflows on its page next.
          </DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>
        <Field label="Name" htmlFor="team-name">
          <Input id="team-name" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} />
        </Field>
        <Field label="Description" htmlFor="team-description">
          <Textarea
            id="team-description"
            rows={3}
            value={description}
            onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
          />
        </Field>
        {error && (
          <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">
            {error}
          </p>
        )}
        <DialogFooter>
          <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={props.onClose}>
            Cancel
          </Button>
          <Button
            type="button"
            disabled={busy || !scope || name.trim() === ""}
            title={name.trim() === "" ? "Give the team a name first." : undefined}
            onClick={() => void create()}
          >
            Create team
          </Button>
        </DialogFooter>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { WaitingOn, WorkflowRunStatus } from "@/api/types";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";

import { workflowRunStatusLabel } from "./format";

const LOOK: Record<WorkflowRunStatus, string> = {
  running: "border-primary/40 bg-transparent text-primary",
  // A waiting run waits for the person: the needs colours (deep-teal spec section 4).
  waiting: "border-needs-border bg-transparent text-needs",
  succeeded: "border-success/40 bg-transparent text-success",
  failed: "border-destructive/40 bg-transparent text-destructive",
  cancelled: "bg-muted text-muted-foreground",
};

/// A workflow run's status as a badge, saying what a waiting run waits for when it is known.
export function WorkflowRunBadge(props: {
  status: WorkflowRunStatus;
  waitingOn?: WaitingOn | null;
}) {
  return (
    <Badge variant="secondary" data-status={props.status} className={LOOK[props.status]}>
      {workflowRunStatusLabel({ status: props.status, waiting_on: props.waitingOn })}
    </Badge>
  );
}

/// The "Test" label of a test run (teams spec section 5.1): its deploys pushed nothing.
export function TestBadge() {
  return (
    <Badge variant="outline" title="A test run: its deploys push nothing">
      Test
    </Badge>
  );
}
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/format.ts`:**

```ts
import type {
  Budget,
  Team,
  TeamSpec,
  Trigger,
  WaitingOn,
  WorkflowRun,
  WorkflowRunStatus,
  WorkflowSpec,
} from "@/api/types";
import { formatTokens } from "@/lib/usage-format";

/// What starts a workflow, by its trigger's kind (teams spec section 5.1).
export const TRIGGER_LABEL: Record<Trigger["kind"], string> = {
  manual: "Run by hand",
  chat: "Started from a chat",
  schedule: "On a schedule",
  webhook: "On a webhook",
};

/// What a waiting workflow run waits for, in the needs-you wording (section 10.5).
export const WAITING_LABEL: Record<WaitingOn, string> = {
  check: "Needs your check",
  budget: "Hit its budget",
  send_backs: "Hit its send-back limit",
  step_failed: "A step failed",
};

const STATUS_LABEL: Record<WorkflowRunStatus, string> = {
  running: "Running",
  waiting: "Waiting",
  succeeded: "Succeeded",
  failed: "Failed",
  cancelled: "Cancelled",
};

/// A workflow run's status as a person reads it: a waiting run says what it waits for when
/// that is known (a workflow's `last_run` does not say).
export function workflowRunStatusLabel(run: {
  status: WorkflowRunStatus;
  waiting_on?: WaitingOn | null;
}): string {
  if (run.status === "waiting" && run.waiting_on) return WAITING_LABEL[run.waiting_on];
  return STATUS_LABEL[run.status];
}

/// The first line of a task, trimmed: how lists, cards and titles name a workflow run.
export function taskLine(task: string): string {
  return (task.trim().split(/\r?\n/, 1)[0] ?? "").trim();
}

/// "3 runs · 120k tokens": what a workflow run spent so far (section 10.4).
export function formatSpend(run: Pick<WorkflowRun, "spent_runs" | "spent_tokens">): string {
  const runs = `${run.spent_runs} ${run.spent_runs === 1 ? "run" : "runs"}`;
  return `${runs} · ${formatTokens(run.spent_tokens)} tokens`;
}

/// The team as `team_update` takes it, with `change` applied: its members keep their roles,
/// and its account and workspace stay.
export function teamSpec(team: Team, change: Partial<TeamSpec> = {}): TeamSpec {
  return {
    name: team.name,
    description: team.description,
    members: team.members.map((m) => ({ dot_id: m.dot_id, role: m.role })),
    org_id: team.org_id,
    workspace_id: team.workspace_id,
    ...change,
  };
}

/// The limits a new workflow starts with (section 5.7), the core's defaults.
export const DEFAULT_BUDGET: Budget = {
  max_runs: 10,
  max_tokens: 1_500_000,
  max_minutes: 120,
  max_send_backs: 3,
};

/// A new workflow named `name`: run by hand, with one agent step that `member` does, and off
/// until the person has built it and turns it on. A save needs a step, and a step a member.
export function newWorkflowSpec(name: string, member: string): WorkflowSpec {
  return {
    name,
    enabled: false,
    definition: {
      trigger: { kind: "manual" },
      steps: [
        {
          id: "work",
          name: "Work",
          kind: "agent",
          member,
          job: "Work on the task.",
          can_send_back_to: [],
          outcomes: [],
          delegate: null,
        },
      ],
      budget: DEFAULT_BUDGET,
    },
  };
}

/// `base`, else `base 2`, `base 3`, …: the first name none of `taken` has, without case.
export function uniqueName(base: string, taken: string[]): string {
  const used = new Set(taken.map((name) => name.trim().toLowerCase()));
  if (!used.has(base.toLowerCase())) return base;
  let n = 2;
  while (used.has(`${base} ${n}`.toLowerCase())) n += 1;
  return `${base} ${n}`;
}
```

### Task 9: The workflow builder lists its steps, adds, moves and deletes them, and saves what validates

The route `/teams/:teamId/workflows/:workflowId`: a header (name, the switch, Test run, Save, Delete workflow), the trigger card, numbered step cards joined by arrows in their kind's look (a delegate dashed with its "may start" line, a check in the needs colours, a branch's paths as labelled columns), ＋ between cards and at each list's end, up and down buttons and Alt+ArrowUp/Down, delete, the Budget card, and the live check on the card it names with Save off while there is a problem. A hand-written `Sheet` holds a step's settings, here its Name only. UI only.

**Files:**
- Create: `ui/src/components/ui/sheet.tsx`, `ui/src/screens/teams/builder/{draft.ts,labels.ts,StepList.tsx,cards.tsx,WorkflowBuilderScreen.tsx}`
- Modify: `ui/src/app/routes.tsx`
- Test: `ui/src/screens/teams/builder/{draft.test.ts,WorkflowBuilderScreen.test.tsx}` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 6's `workflowGet`, `workflowUpdate`, `workflowDelete`, `workflowCheckSpec`, `keys.workflowProblem` (with `keepPreviousData`); Task 8's `RunNowDialog` (with `test`), `TRIGGER_LABEL`, `builderPath`, `DEFAULT_BUDGET`; `radix-ui`'s `Dialog` and `DropdownMenu`.
- Produces: `components/ui/sheet.tsx`: `Sheet`, `SheetTrigger`, `SheetClose`, `SheetPortal`, `SheetOverlay`, `SheetContent` (`side?: "right" | "left"`, `showCloseButton?`), `SheetHeader`, `SheetFooter`, `SheetTitle`, `SheetDescription` (a modal `role="dialog"` named by its title); `builder/draft.ts`: `StepKindName`, `STEP_KINDS`, `ListRef`, `TOP`, `allSteps`, `childLists`, `findStep`, `nextStepId(kind, steps)` (`<kind>-<n>`), `newStep(kind, steps, team)`, `editList`, `insertStep`, `updateStep`, `locateStep`, `deleteStep`, `canMove`, `moveStep` (within its own list); `builder/labels.ts`: `KIND_LABEL`, `DEPLOY_LABEL`, `NameOf`, `triggerSummary`, `delegateLine`, `deployLine`, `stepLine`; `StepList`, `StepCard`, `AddStep`, the `Builder` context (`BuilderProvider`); `TriggerCard({ trigger, nameOf })`, `BudgetCard({ budget, onChange })`, `StepSheet({ step, onChange, onClose })` (Name only); `WorkflowBuilderScreen`, `CHECK_DELAY_MS = 300`. Labels: `Workflow name`, `On`, `Test run`, `Save`, `Delete workflow`, `Settings of <name>`, `Move <name> up`, `Move <name> down`, `Delete <name>`, `Add step`, `Add step after <name>`, `Add step before <name>`, `Add step to the path <label>`, `Add step to Otherwise`, `Budget`, `Runs`, `Tokens`, `Minutes`, `Send-backs`, Save's reasons `Nothing has changed.`, `The draft is being checked.`, `Fix this first: <message>`.
