```tsx
import { act, screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import type { Step, Team, Workflow, WorkflowProblem, WorkflowSpec } from "@/api/types";
import { backend } from "@/test/backend";
import { sample } from "@/test/fixtures";
import { renderApp } from "@/test/render";

import { allSteps } from "./draft";

const [crew] = sample.teams as [Team, Team];
const [fix, pushIt, nightly, onIssue] = sample.workflows as [
  Workflow,
  Workflow,
  Workflow,
  Workflow,
];

/// The builder of `workflow`, whose draft the live check answers with `problem(spec)`, and
/// whose save answers with what it was sent.
function open(
  workflow: Workflow,
  problem: (spec: WorkflowSpec) => WorkflowProblem | null = () => null,
) {
  backend.on("team_get", () => crew);
  backend.on("workflow_get", () => workflow);
  backend.on("workflow_check_spec", (args) => problem(args.spec as WorkflowSpec));
  backend.on("workflow_update", (args) => ({ ...workflow, ...(args.spec as WorkflowSpec) }));
  return renderApp(`/teams/${crew.id}/workflows/${workflow.id}`);
}

const card = (name: string) => screen.getByRole("group", { name });
const save = () => screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save" });
/// The ids of the steps the last save sent, depth first.
const savedIds = () => {
  const spec = backend.callsTo("workflow_update").at(-1)!.spec as WorkflowSpec;
  return allSteps(spec.definition.steps).map((s: Step) => s.id);
};

describe("the workflow builder", () => {
  it("shows the header, the trigger, the numbered steps in their kind's look, and the budget", async () => {
    open(fix);
    expect(await screen.findByRole("textbox", { name: "Workflow name" })).toHaveValue(
      "Fix and ship",
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole("heading", { name: "Fix and ship" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "crew" })).toHaveAttribute("href", "/teams/01J0TEAM");
    expect(screen.getByRole("switch", { name: "On" })).toBeChecked();
    expect(screen.getByText("1 run in flight uses the previous version")).toBeVisible();
    // Nothing changed yet.
    expect(save()).toBeDisabled();

    const trigger = card("Trigger");
    expect(trigger).toHaveTextContent("When: Started from a chat");
    expect(trigger).toHaveTextContent("When you chat with nightly-tests");
    expect(trigger.className).toContain("border-primary");

    // A delegate: dashed in the accent, with what it may start, by name.
    const build = card("Step 1: Build");
    expect(build).toHaveTextContent("nightly-tests · Fix the bug and say whether it is fixed.");
    expect(build).toHaveTextContent("may start reviewer · 2 at once · 4 total");
    expect(build).toHaveTextContent("Delegate");
    expect(build.className).toContain("border-dashed");
    expect(build.className).toContain("border-primary");

    // A branch's paths are labelled columns holding cards of their own.
    const route = card("Step 2: Fixed?");
    expect(route).toHaveTextContent("Goes on by the outcome reported before it");
    const fixed = within(within(route).getByRole("region", { name: "Path fixed" }));
    const review = fixed.getByRole("group", { name: "Step 2.1: Review" });
    expect(review.className).toContain("border-needs-border");
    expect(review).toHaveTextContent("Approve, or send back to Build with a note");
    expect(fixed.getByRole("group", { name: "Step 2.2: Ship" })).toHaveTextContent(
      "Pushes, opens a pull request and merges it (squash)",
    );
    const otherwise = within(within(route).getByRole("region", { name: "Otherwise" }));
    expect(otherwise.getByRole("group", { name: "Step 2.1: Design" })).toHaveTextContent(
      "reviewer · Propose a design.",
    );

    const budget = within(screen.getByRole("region", { name: "Budget" }));
    expect(budget.getByRole("spinbutton", { name: "Runs" })).toHaveValue(10);
    expect(budget.getByRole("spinbutton", { name: "Tokens" })).toHaveValue(1_500_000);
    expect(budget.getByRole("spinbutton", { name: "Minutes" })).toHaveValue(120);
    expect(budget.getByRole("spinbutton", { name: "Send-backs" })).toHaveValue(3);
  });

  it("shows each trigger and deploy mode, and a member who left by id", async () => {
    open(onIssue);
    expect(await screen.findByRole("group", { name: "Trigger" })).toHaveTextContent(
      "When: On a webhookLook at the newest issue., unless the call says the task",
    );
    expect(card("Step 3: Ship")).toHaveTextContent("reviewer deploys: Release what was approved.");
    expect(screen.queryByText(/in flight/)).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("shows a schedule's task and project, and a branch on project", async () => {
    open(nightly);
    expect(await screen.findByRole("group", { name: "Trigger" })).toHaveTextContent(
      "Update the dependencies. · on Dots",
    );
    const branch = card("Step 3: Which project");
    expect(branch).toHaveTextContent("Goes on by the project");
    expect(
      within(within(branch).getByRole("region", { name: "Path Dots" })).getByRole("group", {
        name: "Step 3.1: Ship",
      }),
    ).toHaveTextContent("Pushes the branches and opens a pull request");
    expect(
      within(within(branch).getByRole("region", { name: "Path Site" })).getByRole("group", {
        name: "Step 3.1: Ship",
      }),
    ).toHaveTextContent("Keeps the branches: nothing is pushed");
  });

  it("adds a step of each kind between the cards and at the end, with ids it makes", async () => {
    const { user } = open(nightly);
    await screen.findByRole("group", { name: "Step 1: Work" });

    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Add step" }));
    await user.click(within(screen.getByRole("menu")).getByRole("menuitem", { name: "Check" }));
    const added = card("Step 4: Your check");
    expect(added).toHaveFocus();

    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Add step after Work" }));
    expect(
      within(screen.getByRole("menu"))
        .getAllByRole("menuitem")
        .map((item) => item.textContent),
    ).toEqual(["Agent step", "Check", "Deploy", "Branch"]);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("menuitem", { name: "Agent step" }));
    expect(card("Step 2: Work")).toHaveTextContent("nightly-tests · Work on the task.");
    expect(card("Step 3: Review")).toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Add step before Work" }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("menuitem", { name: "Branch" }));
    const branch = card("Step 1: Branch");
    expect(within(branch).getByRole("region", { name: "Path Dots" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(branch).getByRole("region", { name: "Path Site" })).toBeInTheDocument();

    const site = within(within(branch).getByRole("region", { name: "Path Site" }));
    await user.click(site.getByRole("button", { name: "Add step to the path Site" }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("menuitem", { name: "Deploy" }));
    expect(card("Step 1.1: Deploy")).toHaveTextContent("Keeps the branches: nothing is pushed");

    await waitFor(() => expect(save()).toBeEnabled());
    await user.click(save());
    expect(savedIds()).toEqual([
      "branch-1",
      "deploy-1",
      "work",
      "agent-1",
      "check",
      "by-project",
      "pr",
      "keep",
      "check-1",
    ]);
    // Saved: nothing is left to save.
    await waitFor(() => expect(save()).toBeDisabled());
  });

  it("moves a step with its buttons and Alt+Up and Alt+Down, within its own list", async () => {
    const { user } = open(fix);
    await screen.findByRole("group", { name: "Step 1: Build" });
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Move Build up" })).toBeDisabled();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Move Build down" }));
    expect(card("Step 2: Build")).toHaveFocus();
    expect(card("Step 1: Fixed?")).toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.keyboard("{Alt>}{ArrowUp}{/Alt}");
    expect(card("Step 1: Build")).toHaveFocus();

    // A card in a path moves on its path; the branch around it stays.
    const review = card("Step 2.1: Review");
    act(() => review.focus());
    await user.keyboard("{Alt>}{ArrowDown}{/Alt}");
    expect(card("Step 2.2: Review")).toHaveFocus();
    expect(card("Step 2.1: Ship")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(card("Step 2: Fixed?")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Move Review down" })).toBeDisabled();
    // At the end of its path it stays there.
    await user.keyboard("{Alt>}{ArrowDown}{/Alt}");
    expect(card("Step 2.2: Review")).toHaveFocus();

    await waitFor(() => expect(save()).toBeEnabled());
    await user.click(save());
    expect(savedIds()).toEqual(["build", "route", "ship", "review", "design"]);
  });

  it("deletes a step at once, and a branch with steps in it after asking", async () => {
    const { user } = open(nightly);
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Delete Review" }));
    expect(screen.queryByRole("group", { name: "Step 2: Review" })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(card("Step 2: Which project")).toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Delete Which project" }));
    const ask = await screen.findByRole("alertdialog", { name: "Delete Which project?" });
    expect(ask).toHaveTextContent(
      "The 2 steps inside it go with it. Nothing is saved until you press Save.",
    );
    await user.click(within(ask).getByRole("button", { name: "Delete step" }));
    expect(screen.queryByRole("group", { name: "Step 2: Which project" })).not.toBeInTheDocument();

    await waitFor(() => expect(save()).toBeEnabled());
    await user.click(save());
    expect(savedIds()).toEqual(["work"]);
  });

  it("puts the live check's problem on the card it names, else on the header, and keeps Save off", async () => {
    const noCheck: WorkflowProblem = {
      step_id: "pr",
      message: 'step "Ship": a deploy needs a check before it on every path',
    };
    const { user } = open(nightly, (spec) => {
      if (spec.definition.budget.max_runs === 0) {
        return { step_id: null, message: "every budget limit must be at least 1" };
      }
      return allSteps(spec.definition.steps).some((s) => s.kind === "check") ? null : noCheck;
    });
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Delete Review" }));
    const ship = within(screen.getByRole("region", { name: "Path Dots" })).getByRole("group", {
      name: "Step 2.1: Ship",
    });
    await waitFor(() => expect(ship).toHaveAccessibleDescription(noCheck.message));
    expect(ship).toHaveAttribute("aria-invalid", "true");
    expect(ship.className).toContain("border-destructive");
    expect(save()).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("alert")).not.toBeInTheDocument();

    // Put the check back: the problem goes, and Save comes on.
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Add step after Work" }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("menuitem", { name: "Check" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(save()).toBeEnabled());
    expect(ship).not.toHaveAttribute("aria-invalid");

    // A problem that names no step shows on the header.
    const runs = screen.getByRole("spinbutton", { name: "Runs" });
    await user.clear(runs);
    expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent(
      "every budget limit must be at least 1",
    );
    expect(save()).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Test run" })).toBeDisabled();
    // The check is asked once the draft settles, not on each key.
    const sent = backend.callsTo("workflow_check_spec").map((c) => c.spec as WorkflowSpec);
    expect(sent.at(-1)!.definition.budget.max_runs).toBe(0);
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_check_spec").at(-1)!.teamId).toBe("01J0TEAM");
    await user.type(runs, "6");
    await waitFor(() => expect(save()).toBeEnabled());
    expect(screen.queryByRole("alert")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("shows a saved workflow's problem on its card when it opens", async () => {
    const [notMember] = sample.workflowProblems as [WorkflowProblem, WorkflowProblem];
    open(pushIt, () => notMember);
    const work = await screen.findByRole("group", { name: "Step 1: Work" });
    await waitFor(() => expect(work).toHaveAccessibleDescription(notMember.message));
    // The member is shown by id: it left the team.
    expect(work).toHaveTextContent("01J0GONE · Do the task.");
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Test run" })).toBeDisabled();
  });

  it("saves the name, the switch and the budget, and shows the core's refusal on the header", async () => {
    const { user } = open(nightly);
    const name = await screen.findByRole("textbox", { name: "Workflow name" });
    await user.clear(name);
    await user.type(name, " Fix and ship ");
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("switch", { name: "On" }));
    const tokens = screen.getByRole("spinbutton", { name: "Tokens" });
    await user.clear(tokens);
    await user.type(tokens, "2000000");
    backend.on("workflow_update", () => {
      throw { kind: "conflict", message: "the team already has a workflow named Fix and ship" };
    });
    await waitFor(() => expect(save()).toBeEnabled());
    await user.click(save());
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_update")).toEqual([
      {
        id: "01J0WFL3",
        spec: {
          name: "Fix and ship",
          enabled: false,
          definition: {
            ...nightly.definition,
            budget: { ...nightly.definition.budget, max_tokens: 2_000_000 },
          },
        },
      },
    ]);
    expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent(
      "the team already has a workflow named Fix and ship",
    );
    // The draft is kept, and the next edit takes the refusal away.
    expect(name).toHaveValue(" Fix and ship ");
    await user.type(name, "2");
    expect(screen.queryByRole("alert")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("opens a step's settings in a side sheet, and renames the step in the draft", async () => {
    const { user } = open(fix);
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Settings of Ship" }));
    const sheet = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Deploy settings" });
    const field = within(sheet).getByRole("textbox", { name: "Name" });
    expect(field).toHaveValue("Ship");
    await user.clear(field);
    await user.type(field, "Merge");
    await user.keyboard("{Escape}");
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByRole("dialog")).not.toBeInTheDocument());
    expect(card("Step 2.2: Merge")).toBeInTheDocument();

    // A click on a card, or Enter on it, opens its settings too.
    await user.click(screen.getByText("reviewer · Propose a design."));
    expect(await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Agent step settings" })).toBeVisible();
    await user.keyboard("{Escape}");
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByRole("dialog")).not.toBeInTheDocument());
    act(() => card("Step 2.1: Review").focus());
    await user.keyboard("{Enter}");
    expect(await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Check settings" })).toBeVisible();
    await user.keyboard("{Escape}");

    await waitFor(() => expect(save()).toBeEnabled());
    await user.click(save());
    const spec = backend.callsTo("workflow_update")[0]!.spec as WorkflowSpec;
    expect(allSteps(spec.definition.steps).map((s) => s.name)).toEqual([
      "Build",
      "Fixed?",
      "Review",
      "Merge",
      "Design",
    ]);
  });

  it("follows the saved workflow while the draft is untouched, and keeps an edited draft", async () => {
    const { user, listening } = open(nightly);
    const name = await screen.findByRole("textbox", { name: "Workflow name" });
    await listening();
    backend.on("workflow_get", () => ({ ...nightly, name: "Nightly deps" }));
    act(() =>
      backend.emit("runtime", {
        type: "workflow_updated",
        team_id: crew.id,
        workflow_id: nightly.id,
      }),
    );
    await waitFor(() => expect(name).toHaveValue("Nightly deps"));
    expect(save()).toBeDisabled();

    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Delete Review" }));
    backend.on("workflow_get", () => ({ ...nightly, name: "Nightly again" }));
    act(() =>
      backend.emit("runtime", {
        type: "workflow_updated",
        team_id: crew.id,
        workflow_id: nightly.id,
      }),
    );
    await waitFor(() =>
      expect(screen.getByRole("heading", { name: "Nightly again" })).toBeInTheDocument(),
    );
    expect(name).toHaveValue("Nightly deps");
    expect(screen.queryByRole("group", { name: "Step 2: Review" })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("saves the draft before a test run, then asks for its task", async () => {
    const { user } = open(nightly);
    const name = await screen.findByRole("textbox", { name: "Workflow name" });
    await user.type(name, " 2");
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Test run" })).toBeEnabled());
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Test run" }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Test run Nightly 2" })).toBeVisible();
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_update")).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_update")[0]).toMatchObject({ spec: { name: "Nightly 2" } });
    await user.keyboard("{Escape}");

    // A workflow that is off cannot start, so its test run waits until it is on.
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("switch", { name: "On" }));
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Test run" })).toBeDisabled();
  });

  it("deletes the workflow after asking, then goes back to the team page", async () => {
    backend.on("workflows_list", () => sample.workflows);
    backend.on("workflow_runs_list", () => []);
    backend.on("workflow_delete", () => null);
    const { user, router } = open(fix);
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Delete workflow" }));
    const ask = await screen.findByRole("alertdialog", { name: "Delete Fix and ship?" });
    expect(ask).toHaveTextContent(
      "Its runs stay on the team page, as runs of a deleted workflow. Runs in flight go on " +
        "with the version they started with: 1 run now.",
    );
    await user.click(within(ask).getByRole("button", { name: "Delete workflow" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe("/teams/01J0TEAM"));
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_delete")).toEqual([{ id: "01J0WFL" }]);
  });

  it("shows why a workflow cannot be shown", async () => {
    backend.on("team_get", () => crew);
    backend.on("workflow_get", () => {
      throw { kind: "not_found", message: "workflow nope" };
    });
    renderApp(`/teams/${crew.id}/workflows/nope`);
    expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("workflow nope");
  });
});
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/builder/draft.test.ts`:**

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { Step, type Team, type Workflow } from "@/api/types";
import { sample } from "@/test/fixtures";

import {
  allSteps,
  canMove,
  deleteStep,
  findStep,
  insertStep,
  locateStep,
  moveStep,
  newStep,
  nextStepId,
  TOP,
  updateStep,
} from "./draft";

const [crew] = sample.teams as [Team, Team];
const [fix, , nightly] = sample.workflows as [Workflow, Workflow, Workflow, Workflow];
// Build; Fixed? (path fixed: Review, Ship; otherwise: Design).
const fixSteps = fix.definition.steps;
// Work; Review; Which project (path Dots: pr; path Site: keep).
const nightlySteps = nightly.definition.steps;
const ids = (steps: Step[]) => allSteps(steps).map((s) => s.id);

describe("the builder's draft", () => {
  it("walks the steps depth first, as the core does, and finds one at any depth", () => {
    expect(ids(fixSteps)).toEqual(["build", "route", "review", "ship", "design"]);
    expect(findStep(fixSteps, "design")?.name).toBe("Design");
    expect(findStep(fixSteps, "nope")).toBeUndefined();
    expect(locateStep(fixSteps, "ship")).toEqual({
      ref: { branch: "route", path: 0 },
      index: 1,
      length: 2,
    });
    expect(locateStep(fixSteps, "design")).toEqual({
      ref: { branch: "route", path: "otherwise" },
      index: 0,
      length: 1,
    });
    expect(locateStep(fixSteps, "route")).toEqual({ ref: TOP, index: 1, length: 2 });
  });

  it("makes a step's id from its kind, one no step of the whole tree has", () => {
    expect(nextStepId("agent", fixSteps)).toBe("agent-1");
    const taken = insertStep(fixSteps, { branch: "route", path: 0 }, 0, {
      ...newStep("check", fixSteps, crew),
    });
    expect(ids(taken)).toContain("check-1");
    expect(nextStepId("check", taken)).toBe("check-2");
    // The id fits the core's rule: 1 to 40 of a-z, 0-9, '-' and '_'.
    expect(nextStepId("deploy", taken)).toMatch(/^[a-z0-9_-]{1,40}$/);
  });

  it("gives each kind of new step defaults a save takes", () => {
    const agent = newStep("agent", nightlySteps, crew);
    expect(agent).toEqual({
      id: "agent-1",
      name: "Work",
      kind: "agent",
      member: "01J0DOT",
      job: "Work on the task.",
      can_send_back_to: [],
      outcomes: [],
      delegate: null,
    });
    expect(newStep("check", nightlySteps, crew)).toEqual({
      id: "check-1",
      name: "Your check",
      kind: "check",
      send_back_to: null,
    });
    // A new deploy pushes nothing until its mode is chosen.
    expect(newStep("deploy", nightlySteps, crew)).toEqual({
      id: "deploy-1",
      name: "Deploy",
      kind: "deploy",
      mode: "branch",
    });
    expect(newStep("branch", nightlySteps, crew)).toEqual({
      id: "branch-1",
      name: "Branch",
      kind: "branch",
      on: "project",
      paths: [
        { label: "Dots", steps: [] },
        { label: "Site", steps: [] },
      ],
      otherwise: [],
    });
    const bare = newStep("branch", [], { members: [], projects: [] });
    expect(bare.kind === "branch" && bare.paths).toEqual([{ label: "Path 1", steps: [] }]);
    for (const kind of ["agent", "check", "deploy", "branch"] as const) {
      expect(Step.safeParse(newStep(kind, nightlySteps, crew)).success, kind).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it("puts a new step into any list at its place", () => {
    const step = newStep("check", fixSteps, crew);
    expect(ids(insertStep(fixSteps, TOP, 0, step))).toEqual([
      "check-1",
      "build",
      "route",
      "review",
      "ship",
      "design",
    ]);
    expect(ids(insertStep(fixSteps, TOP, 2, step)).at(-1)).toBe("check-1");
    expect(ids(insertStep(fixSteps, { branch: "route", path: 0 }, 1, step))).toEqual([
      "build",
      "route",
      "review",
      "check-1",
      "ship",
      "design",
    ]);
    expect(ids(insertStep(fixSteps, { branch: "route", path: "otherwise" }, 1, step))).toEqual([
      "build",
      "route",
      "review",
      "ship",
      "design",
      "check-1",
    ]);
    // A list that is not there changes nothing.
    expect(insertStep(fixSteps, { branch: "route", path: 5 }, 0, step)).toEqual(fixSteps);
    expect(insertStep(fixSteps, { branch: "build", path: 0 }, 0, step)).toEqual(fixSteps);
  });

  it("changes and deletes a step by id at any depth, leaving the rest as it was", () => {
    const renamed = updateStep(fixSteps, "ship", (s) => ({ ...s, name: "Merge" }));
    expect(findStep(renamed, "ship")?.name).toBe("Merge");
    expect(allSteps(renamed).filter((s) => s.id !== "ship" && s.kind !== "branch")).toEqual(
      allSteps(fixSteps).filter((s) => s.id !== "ship" && s.kind !== "branch"),
    );
    expect(fixSteps).toEqual(fix.definition.steps);

    expect(ids(deleteStep(fixSteps, "review"))).toEqual(["build", "route", "ship", "design"]);
    expect(ids(deleteStep(fixSteps, "design"))).toEqual(["build", "route", "review", "ship"]);
    // A branch goes with the steps inside it.
    expect(ids(deleteStep(fixSteps, "route"))).toEqual(["build"]);
    expect(deleteStep(fixSteps, "nope")).toEqual(fixSteps);
  });

  it("moves a step up or down within its own list, and not past either end", () => {
    expect(ids(moveStep(nightlySteps, "work", 1))).toEqual([
      "check",
      "work",
      "by-project",
      "pr",
      "keep",
    ]);
    expect(ids(moveStep(nightlySteps, "by-project", -1)).slice(0, 3)).toEqual([
      "work",
      "by-project",
      "pr",
    ]);
    expect(canMove(nightlySteps, "work", -1)).toBe(false);
    expect(moveStep(nightlySteps, "work", -1)).toEqual(nightlySteps);
    expect(canMove(nightlySteps, "by-project", 1)).toBe(false);

    // Inside a path: the step stays on it.
    expect(ids(moveStep(fixSteps, "ship", -1))).toEqual([
      "build",
      "route",
      "ship",
      "review",
      "design",
    ]);
    expect(canMove(fixSteps, "ship", 1)).toBe(false);
    expect(canMove(fixSteps, "design", -1)).toBe(false);
    expect(canMove(fixSteps, "nope", 1)).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { TeamsScreen } from "@/screens/teams/TeamsScreen";
import { UsageScreen } from "@/screens/UsageScreen";
```

**with:**

```tsx
import { TeamsScreen } from "@/screens/teams/TeamsScreen";
import { WorkflowBuilderScreen } from "@/screens/teams/builder/WorkflowBuilderScreen";
import { UsageScreen } from "@/screens/UsageScreen";
```

**In `ui/src/app/routes.tsx`, replace:**

```tsx
      { path: "teams/:teamId", element: <TeamScreen /> },
      { path: "settings", element: <SettingsScreen /> },
```

**with:**

```tsx
      { path: "teams/:teamId", element: <TeamScreen /> },
      { path: "teams/:teamId/workflows/:workflowId", element: <WorkflowBuilderScreen /> },
      { path: "settings", element: <SettingsScreen /> },
```

**Create `ui/src/components/ui/sheet.tsx`:**

```tsx
import * as React from "react"
import { cn } from "cn"
import { Dialog as SheetPrimitive } from "radix-ui"

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { XIcon } from "lucide-react"

// A side panel on radix-ui's Dialog, written by hand in the shape of dialog.tsx (no `shadcn
// add`: no network). It is a modal dialog named by its SheetTitle.

function Sheet({ ...props }: React.ComponentProps<typeof SheetPrimitive.Root>) {
  return <SheetPrimitive.Root data-slot="sheet" {...props} />
}

function SheetTrigger({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof SheetPrimitive.Trigger>) {
  return <SheetPrimitive.Trigger data-slot="sheet-trigger" {...props} />
}

function SheetClose({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof SheetPrimitive.Close>) {
  return <SheetPrimitive.Close data-slot="sheet-close" {...props} />
}

function SheetPortal({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof SheetPrimitive.Portal>) {
  return <SheetPrimitive.Portal data-slot="sheet-portal" {...props} />
}

function SheetOverlay({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof SheetPrimitive.Overlay>) {
  return (
    <SheetPrimitive.Overlay
      data-slot="sheet-overlay"
      className={cn(
        "fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/10 duration-100 supports-backdrop-filter:backdrop-blur-xs data-open:animate-in data-open:fade-in-0 data-closed:animate-out data-closed:fade-out-0",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function SheetContent({
  className,
  children,
  side = "right",
  showCloseButton = true,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof SheetPrimitive.Content> & {
  side?: "right" | "left"
  showCloseButton?: boolean
}) {
  return (
    <SheetPortal>
      <SheetOverlay />
      <SheetPrimitive.Content
        data-slot="sheet-content"
        data-side={side}
        className={cn(
          "fixed inset-y-0 z-50 flex h-full w-3/4 flex-col gap-4 overflow-y-auto bg-popover p-4 text-sm text-popover-foreground shadow-lg ring-1 ring-foreground/10 duration-200 outline-none sm:max-w-md data-open:animate-in data-closed:animate-out",
          side === "right"
            ? "right-0 data-open:slide-in-from-right data-closed:slide-out-to-right"
            : "left-0 data-open:slide-in-from-left data-closed:slide-out-to-left",
          className
        )}
        {...props}
      >
        {children}
        {showCloseButton && (
          <SheetPrimitive.Close data-slot="sheet-close" asChild>
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              className="absolute top-2 right-2"
              size="icon-sm"
            >
              <XIcon />
              <span className="sr-only">Close</span>
            </Button>
          </SheetPrimitive.Close>
        )}
      </SheetPrimitive.Content>
    </SheetPortal>
  )
}

function SheetHeader({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="sheet-header"
      className={cn("flex flex-col gap-2 pr-8", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function SheetFooter({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="sheet-footer"
      className={cn("mt-auto flex flex-col-reverse gap-2 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function SheetTitle({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof SheetPrimitive.Title>) {
  return (
    <SheetPrimitive.Title
      data-slot="sheet-title"
      className={cn("font-heading text-base leading-none font-medium", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function SheetDescription({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof SheetPrimitive.Description>) {
  return (
    <SheetPrimitive.Description
      data-slot="sheet-description"
      className={cn("text-sm text-muted-foreground", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export {
  Sheet,
  SheetClose,
  SheetContent,
  SheetDescription,
  SheetFooter,
  SheetHeader,
  SheetOverlay,
  SheetPortal,
  SheetTitle,
  SheetTrigger,
}
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/builder/StepList.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { cn } from "cn";
import { ArrowDown, ChevronDown, ChevronUp, Plus, Settings2, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { DropdownMenu } from "radix-ui";
import { createContext, useContext, useRef, type KeyboardEvent, type MouseEvent } from "react";

import type { Step, WorkflowProblem } from "@/api/types";
import { ConfirmButton } from "@/components/ConfirmButton";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

import {
  allSteps,
  canMove,
  childLists,
  type ListRef,
  STEP_KINDS,
  type StepKindName,
} from "./draft";
import { delegateLine, KIND_LABEL, type NameOf, stepLine } from "./labels";

/// What the step cards read and do, from the builder that holds the draft.
export type Builder = {
  /// The whole draft's steps.
  steps: Step[];
  nameOf: NameOf;
  /// The live check's problem when it names a step of the draft.
  problem: WorkflowProblem | null;
  add: (list: ListRef, index: number, kind: StepKindName) => void;
  move: (id: string, by: -1 | 1) => void;
  remove: (id: string) => void;
  /// Opens the step's settings in the side sheet.
  open: (id: string) => void;
  /// Keeps each card's element, so focus can follow a card that moved.
  card: (id: string, el: HTMLElement | null) => void;
};

const BuilderContext = createContext<Builder | null>(null);
export const BuilderProvider = BuilderContext.Provider;

function useBuilder(): Builder {
  const builder = useContext(BuilderContext);
  if (!builder) throw new Error("a step card needs the builder");
  return builder;
}

const ITEM =
  "flex cursor-default items-center gap-2 rounded-sm px-2 py-1.5 outline-none select-none data-[highlighted]:bg-accent";

/// "＋ Add step": a menu of the kinds of step (section 5) that puts the new one into `list` at
/// `index`. With `text` the button says "Add step"; without, it is a small ＋ between two cards.
export function AddStep(props: { list: ListRef; index: number; label: string; text?: boolean }) {
  const { add } = useBuilder();
  // A chosen kind puts the focus on the new card, so the closing menu must not take it back.
  const chosen = useRef(false);
  return (
    <DropdownMenu.Root modal={false}>
      <DropdownMenu.Trigger asChild>
        <Button
          variant={props.text ? "outline" : "ghost"}
          size={props.text ? "xs" : "icon-xs"}
          aria-label={props.label}
          title={props.text ? undefined : props.label}
        >
          <Plus aria-hidden />
          {props.text && "Add step"}
        </Button>
      </DropdownMenu.Trigger>
      <DropdownMenu.Portal>
        <DropdownMenu.Content
          align="center"
          className="z-50 w-40 rounded-md border bg-popover p-1 text-sm text-popover-foreground shadow-md"
          onCloseAutoFocus={(e) => {
            if (chosen.current) e.preventDefault();
            chosen.current = false;
          }}
        >
          {STEP_KINDS.map((kind) => (
            <DropdownMenu.Item
              key={kind}
              className={ITEM}
              onSelect={() => {
                chosen.current = true;
                add(props.list, props.index, kind);
              }}
            >
              {KIND_LABEL[kind]}
            </DropdownMenu.Item>
          ))}
        </DropdownMenu.Content>
      </DropdownMenu.Portal>
    </DropdownMenu.Root>
  );
}

/// The arrow between two cards, with an Add step between them when `add` is given.
function Connector(props: { add?: { list: ListRef; index: number; label: string } }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center py-1 text-muted-foreground">
      <ArrowDown aria-hidden className="size-4" />
      {props.add && <AddStep {...props.add} />}
    </div>
  );
}

/// A list of step cards in run order, numbered from `prefix`, joined by arrows, with Add step
/// between the cards and at the end. `name` names the list for a screen reader and the Add
/// step buttons ("the path fixed"). The top list also gets an Add step before its first card,
/// which follows the trigger card.
export function StepList(props: {
  steps: Step[];
  list: ListRef;
  prefix: string;
  name: string;
  top?: boolean;
}) {
  const { steps, list } = props;
  const endLabel = props.top ? "Add step" : `Add step to ${props.name}`;
  return (
    <div className="flex w-full flex-col items-stretch">
      {props.top && steps.length > 0 && (
        <Connector add={{ list, index: 0, label: `Add step before ${steps[0]!.name}` }} />
      )}
      <ol aria-label={props.top ? "Steps" : `Steps of ${props.name}`} className="flex flex-col">
        {steps.map((step, i) => (
          <li key={step.id} className="flex flex-col">
            {i > 0 && (
              <Connector add={{ list, index: i, label: `Add step after ${steps[i - 1]!.name}` }} />
            )}
            <StepCard step={step} number={`${props.prefix}${i + 1}`} />
          </li>
        ))}
      </ol>
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1 pt-1">
        {steps.length > 0 && <ArrowDown aria-hidden className="size-4 text-muted-foreground" />}
        <AddStep list={list} index={steps.length} label={endLabel} text />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/// How a card of each kind looks (section 10.2): a delegate step dashed in the accent, a check
/// in the needs-you colours.
function look(step: Step): string {
  if (step.kind === "agent" && step.delegate) return "border-dashed border-primary";
  if (step.kind === "check") return "border-needs-border bg-needs-surface";
  return "";
}

/// Whether a click or key on the card is the card's own, not one of a control in it or of a
/// card inside it (a branch holds cards).
function own(e: MouseEvent | KeyboardEvent): boolean {
  const target = e.target as Element;
  if (target.closest("[data-step-id]") !== e.currentTarget) return false;
  return target === e.currentTarget || !target.closest("button, a, input, textarea, select");
}

/// One step as a card: its number, name and what it does, its kind's look, the problem the live
/// check found in it, and its buttons (settings, move up and down, delete). A click on it or
/// Enter opens its settings; Alt+ArrowUp and Alt+ArrowDown move it within its list. A branch
/// shows its paths as labelled columns of their own cards.
export function StepCard({ step, number }: { step: Step; number: string }) {
  const builder = useBuilder();
  const { nameOf, problem } = builder;
  const name = step.name.trim() || "(no name)";
  const mine = problem?.step_id === step.id ? problem : null;
  const problemId = `problem-${step.id}`;
  const delegate = step.kind === "agent" ? delegateLine(step, nameOf) : undefined;
  const badge = delegate ? "Delegate" : KIND_LABEL[step.kind];

  function onKeyDown(e: KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
    // A card inside a branch handles its own keys first: the branch does not move with them.
    if (e.defaultPrevented) return;
    if (e.altKey && (e.key === "ArrowUp" || e.key === "ArrowDown")) {
      e.preventDefault();
      builder.move(step.id, e.key === "ArrowUp" ? -1 : 1);
    } else if (e.key === "Enter" && e.target === e.currentTarget) {
      e.preventDefault();
      builder.open(step.id);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div
      ref={(el) => builder.card(step.id, el)}
      role="group"
      aria-label={`Step ${number}: ${name}`}
      aria-describedby={mine ? problemId : undefined}
      aria-invalid={mine ? true : undefined}
      tabIndex={0}
      data-step-id={step.id}
      data-kind={step.kind}
      onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
      onClick={(e) => own(e) && builder.open(step.id)}
      className={cn(
        "flex w-full flex-col gap-1 rounded-xl border bg-card p-3 text-left outline-none focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-ring",
        look(step),
        mine && "border-destructive",
      )}
    >
      <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
        <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-0.5">
          <span className={cn("font-semibold", step.kind === "check" && "text-needs")}>
            {number} · {name}
          </span>
          <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            {stepLine(step, builder.steps, nameOf)}
          </span>
          {delegate && <span className="text-xs text-primary">{delegate}</span>}
        </div>
        <Badge variant="outline">{badge}</Badge>
        <StepButtons step={step} name={name} />
      </div>
      {mine && (
        <p id={problemId} className="text-xs text-destructive">
          {mine.message}
        </p>
      )}
      {step.kind === "branch" && (
        <div className="mt-2 flex gap-3 overflow-x-auto pl-4">
          {step.paths.map((path, i) => (
            <PathColumn
              key={i}
              title={path.label.trim() || "(no label)"}
              name={`the path ${path.label.trim()}`}
              region={`Path ${path.label.trim()}`}
              steps={path.steps}
              list={{ branch: step.id, path: i }}
              prefix={`${number}.`}
            />
          ))}
          <PathColumn
            title="Otherwise"
            name="Otherwise"
            region="Otherwise"
            steps={step.otherwise}
            list={{ branch: step.id, path: "otherwise" }}
            prefix={`${number}.`}
          />
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/// One path of a branch: its label over its own list of cards.
function PathColumn(props: {
  title: string;
  name: string;
  region: string;
  steps: Step[];
  list: ListRef;
  prefix: string;
}) {
  return (
    <section
      aria-label={props.region}
      className="flex min-w-56 flex-1 flex-col gap-1 rounded-lg border border-dashed p-2"
    >
      <h3 className="text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">{props.title}</h3>
      <StepList steps={props.steps} list={props.list} prefix={props.prefix} name={props.name} />
    </section>
  );
}

/// A card's settings, move and delete buttons. Deleting a branch that holds steps asks first:
/// they go with it. Any other step goes at once; the draft is not saved until Save.
function StepButtons({ step, name }: { step: Step; name: string }) {
  const builder = useBuilder();
  const inside = childLists(step).flatMap(allSteps).length;
  return (
    <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center">
      <Button
        variant="ghost"
        size="icon-xs"
        aria-label={`Settings of ${name}`}
        title="Settings"
        onClick={() => builder.open(step.id)}
      >
        <Settings2 aria-hidden />
      </Button>
      <Button
        variant="ghost"
        size="icon-xs"
        aria-label={`Move ${name} up`}
        title="Move up (Alt+Up)"
        disabled={!canMove(builder.steps, step.id, -1)}
        onClick={() => builder.move(step.id, -1)}
      >
        <ChevronUp aria-hidden />
      </Button>
      <Button
        variant="ghost"
        size="icon-xs"
        aria-label={`Move ${name} down`}
        title="Move down (Alt+Down)"
        disabled={!canMove(builder.steps, step.id, 1)}
        onClick={() => builder.move(step.id, 1)}
      >
        <ChevronDown aria-hidden />
      </Button>
      {inside > 0 ? (
        <ConfirmButton
          variant="ghost"
          size="icon-xs"
          ariaLabel={`Delete ${name}`}
          title={`Delete ${name}?`}
          description={
            `${inside === 1 ? "The step inside it goes" : `The ${inside} steps inside it go`} ` +
            `with it. Nothing is saved until you press Save.`
          }
          confirmLabel="Delete step"
          onConfirm={() => builder.remove(step.id)}
        >
          <Trash2 aria-hidden />
        </ConfirmButton>
      ) : (
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="icon-xs"
          aria-label={`Delete ${name}`}
          title="Delete"
          onClick={() => builder.remove(step.id)}
        >
          <Trash2 aria-hidden />
        </Button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/builder/WorkflowBuilderScreen.tsx`:**

```tsx
import {
  hashKey,
  keepPreviousData,
  useMutation,
  useQuery,
  useQueryClient,
} from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router";

import { commands } from "@/api/commands";
import type { Step, Team, Workflow, WorkflowSpec } from "@/api/types";
import { ConfirmButton } from "@/components/ConfirmButton";
import { ErrorNote } from "@/components/ErrorNote";
import { Page } from "@/components/Page";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch";
import { errorMessage, notifyError } from "@/lib/notify";
import { invalidate, keys } from "@/lib/query";
import { useDebounced } from "@/lib/use-debounced";
import { plural } from "@/screens/library/parts";

import { RunNowDialog } from "../RunNowDialog";
import { BudgetCard, StepSheet, TriggerCard } from "./cards";
import {
  deleteStep,
  findStep,
  insertStep,
  type ListRef,
  moveStep,
  newStep,
  type StepKindName,
  TOP,
  updateStep,
} from "./draft";
import type { NameOf } from "./labels";
import { type Builder, BuilderProvider, StepList } from "./StepList";

/// How long the draft must stay unchanged before the live check asks the core about it.
export const CHECK_DELAY_MS = 300;

/// The builder (teams spec section 10.2, layout B) at `/teams/:teamId/workflows/:workflowId`.
export function WorkflowBuilderScreen() {
  const { teamId = "", workflowId = "" } = useParams();
  const team = useQuery({ queryKey: keys.team(teamId), queryFn: () => commands.teamGet(teamId) });
  const workflow = useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.workflow(workflowId),
    queryFn: () => commands.workflowGet(workflowId),
  });
  const failed = team.isError ? team.error : workflow.isError ? workflow.error : undefined;
  if (failed !== undefined) {
    return (
      <Page title="Workflow">
        <ErrorNote error={failed} />
      </Page>
    );
  }
  if (!team.data || !workflow.data) return <Page title="Workflow" />;
  return <WorkflowBuilder key={workflowId} team={team.data} workflow={workflow.data} />;
}

/// What a workflow saves: the builder's draft starts from it.
const specOf = (w: Workflow | WorkflowSpec): WorkflowSpec => ({
  name: w.name,
  definition: w.definition,
  enabled: w.enabled,
});

/// Whether two specs say the same, whatever the order of their fields.
const same = (a: WorkflowSpec, b: WorkflowSpec) => hashKey([a]) === hashKey([b]);

/// The draft as Save and the live check send it: its name trimmed.
const sendable = (draft: WorkflowSpec): WorkflowSpec => ({ ...draft, name: draft.name.trim() });

/// The builder edits a draft of the definition in React state. Save sends it with
/// `workflow_update`; the live check sends it (debounced) to `workflow_check_spec` and puts the
/// problem on the card whose step it names, else on the header. Save is off while the draft is
/// unchanged, has a problem, is being checked, or is being saved.
function WorkflowBuilder({ team, workflow }: { team: Team; workflow: Workflow }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const stored = specOf(workflow);
  // `base` is the saved workflow the draft was made from or has caught up with.
  const [base, setBase] = useState(stored);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState(stored);
  const [refusal, setRefusal] = useState<string>();
  const [openId, setOpenId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [testing, setTesting] = useState<Workflow | null>(null);
  const cards = useRef(new Map<string, HTMLElement>());
  const [focusId, setFocusId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // The workflow saved elsewhere (or answered by our own save): an untouched draft follows it,
  // an edited one is kept and is now compared with it.
  const storedKey = hashKey([stored]);
  useEffect(() => {
    setDraft((d) => (same(d, base) ? stored : d));
    setBase(stored);
    // Keyed by what the saved workflow says: `stored` and `base` are read as they are then.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [storedKey]);

  // Focus follows a card that moved or was added.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (focusId === null) return;
    cards.current.get(focusId)?.focus();
    setFocusId(null);
  }, [focusId]);

  const sent = useMemo(() => sendable(draft), [draft]);
  const settled = useDebounced(sent, CHECK_DELAY_MS);
  const check = useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.workflowProblem(team.id, settled),
    queryFn: () => commands.workflowCheckSpec(team.id, settled),
    // The last problem stays on its card while the next draft is checked.
    placeholderData: keepPreviousData,
  });
  const problem = check.data ?? null;
  const checking = !same(settled, sent) || check.isPending || check.isPlaceholderData;
  const onStep = problem?.step_id ? findStep(draft.definition.steps, problem.step_id) : undefined;
  const changed = !same(sent, base);
  // A check that could not be made does not hold Save back: the save checks again.
  const blocked = checking
    ? "The draft is being checked."
    : problem
      ? `Fix this first: ${problem.message}`
      : undefined;

  const save = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (spec: WorkflowSpec) => commands.workflowUpdate(workflow.id, spec),
    onSuccess: (saved, spec) => {
      qc.setQueryData(keys.workflow(saved.id), saved);
      // Edits made while it was saving stay in the draft.
      setDraft((d) => (same(sendable(d), spec) ? specOf(saved) : d));
      setRefusal(undefined);
      invalidate(qc, keys.workflows(team.id));
    },
    onError: (e) => setRefusal(errorMessage(e)),
  });

  const remove = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => commands.workflowDelete(workflow.id),
    onSuccess: () => {
      navigate(`/teams/${team.id}`);
      invalidate(qc, keys.workflows(team.id));
      invalidate(qc, ["workflow-runs"]);
    },
    onError: notifyError,
  });

  /// Every edit goes through here: a refusal of the last save is about a draft that is gone.
  function edit(change: (d: WorkflowSpec) => WorkflowSpec) {
    setDraft(change);
    setRefusal(undefined);
  }
  const editSteps = (change: (steps: Step[]) => Step[]) =>
    edit((d) => ({ ...d, definition: { ...d.definition, steps: change(d.definition.steps) } }));

  const names = new Map(team.members.map((m) => [m.dot_id, m.dot_name]));
  const nameOf: NameOf = (id) => names.get(id) ?? id;

  const builder: Builder = {
    steps: draft.definition.steps,
    nameOf,
    problem: onStep ? problem : null,
    add: (list: ListRef, index: number, kind: StepKindName) => {
      const step = newStep(kind, draft.definition.steps, team);
      editSteps((steps) => insertStep(steps, list, index, step));
      setFocusId(step.id);
    },
    move: (id, by) => {
      editSteps((steps) => moveStep(steps, id, by));
      setFocusId(id);
    },
    remove: (id) => editSteps((steps) => deleteStep(steps, id)),
    open: setOpenId,
    card: (id, el) => {
      if (el) cards.current.set(id, el);
      else cards.current.delete(id);
    },
  };

  const testBlocked =
    blocked ??
    (!draft.enabled
      ? "Turn it on first."
      : team.projects.length === 0
        ? "Add a project to the team first."
        : undefined);
  /// Test run saves the draft first (section 5.1: it runs the saved definition), then asks for
  /// the task and the project.
  async function testRun() {
    const saved = changed ? await save.mutateAsync(sent).catch(() => null) : workflow;
    if (saved) setTesting(saved);
  }

  const inFlight = workflow.runs_in_flight;
  const open = openId ? findStep(draft.definition.steps, openId) : undefined;
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-6">
      <header className="flex flex-col gap-2">
        <Link
          to={`/teams/${team.id}`}
          className="w-fit text-xs text-muted-foreground hover:underline"
        >
          {team.name}
        </Link>
        <h1 className="sr-only">{workflow.name}</h1>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <Input
            aria-label="Workflow name"
            className="h-9 max-w-sm flex-1 text-lg font-semibold"
            value={draft.name}
            onChange={(e) => {
              const name = e.target.value;
              edit((d) => ({ ...d, name }));
            }}
          />
          <span className="flex-1" />
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
            <Switch
              aria-label="On"
              checked={draft.enabled}
              onCheckedChange={(enabled) => edit((d) => ({ ...d, enabled }))}
            />
            On
          </label>
          <Button
            size="sm"
            variant="outline"
            disabled={testBlocked !== undefined || save.isPending}
            title={testBlocked}
            onClick={() => void testRun()}
          >
            Test run
          </Button>
          <Button
            size="sm"
            disabled={!changed || blocked !== undefined || save.isPending}
            title={changed ? blocked : "Nothing has changed."}
            onClick={() => save.mutate(sent)}
          >
            Save
          </Button>
          <ConfirmButton
            title={`Delete ${workflow.name}?`}
            description={
              `Its runs stay on the team page, as runs of a deleted workflow. Runs in flight go ` +
              `on with the version they started with` +
              `${inFlight > 0 ? `: ${plural(inFlight, "run")} now` : ""}.`
            }
            confirmLabel="Delete workflow"
            disabled={remove.isPending}
            onConfirm={() => remove.mutate()}
          >
            Delete workflow
          </ConfirmButton>
        </div>
        {refusal && (
          <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">
            {refusal}
          </p>
        )}
        {problem && !onStep && (
          <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">
            {problem.message}
          </p>
        )}
        {check.isError && (
          <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">
            The draft could not be checked: {errorMessage(check.error)}
          </p>
        )}
        {inFlight > 0 && (
          <p role="status" className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            {inFlight === 1
              ? "1 run in flight uses the previous version"
              : `${inFlight} runs in flight use the previous version`}
          </p>
        )}
      </header>
      <div className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-2xl flex-col items-stretch">
        <TriggerCard trigger={draft.definition.trigger} nameOf={nameOf} />
        <BuilderProvider value={builder}>
          <StepList steps={draft.definition.steps} list={TOP} prefix="" name="the workflow" top />
        </BuilderProvider>
        <div className="pt-4">
          <BudgetCard
            budget={draft.definition.budget}
            onChange={(budget) => edit((d) => ({ ...d, definition: { ...d.definition, budget } }))}
          />
        </div>
      </div>
      <StepSheet
        step={open}
        onChange={(step) => editSteps((steps) => updateStep(steps, step.id, () => step))}
        onClose={() => setOpenId(null)}
      />
      {testing && (
        <RunNowDialog
          workflow={testing}
          projects={team.projects}
          test
          onClose={() => setTesting(null)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/builder/cards.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

import type { Budget, Step, Trigger } from "@/api/types";
import { Field } from "@/components/Field";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import {
  Sheet,
  SheetContent,
  SheetDescription,
  SheetHeader,
  SheetTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/sheet";

import { KIND_LABEL, type NameOf, triggerSummary } from "./labels";

/// The trigger card, first and in the accent (section 10.2): what starts the workflow.
export function TriggerCard({ trigger, nameOf }: { trigger: Trigger; nameOf: NameOf }) {
  const [title, detail] = triggerSummary(trigger, nameOf);
  return (
    <div
      role="group"
      aria-label="Trigger"
      data-kind={trigger.kind}
      className="flex w-full flex-col gap-0.5 rounded-xl border border-primary bg-card p-3"
    >
      <span className="font-semibold">When: {title}</span>
      <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{detail}</span>
    </div>
  );
}

const LIMITS: { key: keyof Budget; label: string }[] = [
  { key: "max_runs", label: "Runs" },
  { key: "max_tokens", label: "Tokens" },
  { key: "max_minutes", label: "Minutes" },
  { key: "max_send_backs", label: "Send-backs" },
];

/// The Budget card at the bottom (section 5.7): the four limits of each run, edited in place.
export function BudgetCard(props: { budget: Budget; onChange: (budget: Budget) => void }) {
  return (
    <section
      aria-labelledby="workflow-budget"
      className="flex w-full flex-col gap-2 rounded-xl border bg-card p-3"
    >
      <h2 id="workflow-budget" className="font-semibold">
        Budget
      </h2>
      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
        What one run may spend. A run that reaches a limit waits for you: add budget or stop it.
      </p>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-4">
        {LIMITS.map(({ key, label }) => (
          <LimitField
            key={key}
            id={`budget-${key}`}
            label={label}
            value={props.budget[key]}
            onChange={(value) => props.onChange({ ...props.budget, [key]: value })}
          />
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

/// A whole number of at least 1; anything else is 0, which the live check refuses.
const limitOf = (text: string) => {
  const n = Number(text.trim());
  return Number.isInteger(n) && n > 0 ? n : 0;
};

/// One limit. Its text is kept as typed, so a field can be emptied on the way to a new number;
/// the draft gets the number it reads as.
function LimitField(props: {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  value: number;
  onChange: (value: number) => void;
}) {
  const [text, setText] = useState(String(props.value));
  // The draft's value when it changes elsewhere (a refresh, or a save's answer).
  useEffect(() => {
    setText((t) => (limitOf(t) === props.value ? t : String(props.value)));
  }, [props.value]);
  return (
    <Field label={props.label} htmlFor={props.id}>
      <Input
        id={props.id}
        type="number"
        min={1}
        step={1}
        inputMode="numeric"
        value={text}
        onChange={(e) => {
          setText(e.target.value);
          props.onChange(limitOf(e.target.value));
        }}
      />
    </Field>
  );
}

/// The side sheet of a step's settings (section 10.2), open while `step` is set. It edits the
/// draft in place: the step's name for now; the fields of each kind join it later.
export function StepSheet(props: {
  step: Step | undefined;
  onChange: (step: Step) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const { step } = props;
  return (
    <Sheet open={step !== undefined} onOpenChange={(open) => !open && props.onClose()}>
      {step && (
        <SheetContent>
          <SheetHeader>
            <SheetTitle>{KIND_LABEL[step.kind]} settings</SheetTitle>
            <SheetDescription>
              Changes go into the draft at once. Save keeps them.
            </SheetDescription>
          </SheetHeader>
          <Field label="Name" htmlFor="step-name" hint="Shown on the card and on the run page.">
            <Input
              id="step-name"
              value={step.name}
              onChange={(e) => props.onChange({ ...step, name: e.target.value })}
            />
          </Field>
        </SheetContent>
      )}
    </Sheet>
  );
}
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/builder/draft.ts`:**

```ts
import type { BranchStep, Step, Team } from "@/api/types";

/// The kinds of step the builder adds (teams spec section 5): a delegate is an agent step whose
/// settings say what it may start.
export type StepKindName = Step["kind"];
export const STEP_KINDS: StepKindName[] = ["agent", "check", "deploy", "branch"];

/// One list of steps in a definition: the top list, or one of a branch step's lists (a path by
/// its place, or `otherwise`).
export type ListRef = { branch: null } | { branch: string; path: number | "otherwise" };

/// The top list of steps.
export const TOP: ListRef = { branch: null };

/// Every step of `steps`, depth first as the core walks them: a branch, then its paths in order,
/// then `otherwise`.
export function allSteps(steps: Step[]): Step[] {
  return steps.flatMap((step) => [step, ...childLists(step).flatMap(allSteps)]);
}

/// The lists inside `step`: a branch's paths, then `otherwise`; none for the other kinds.
export function childLists(step: Step): Step[][] {
  if (step.kind !== "branch") return [];
  return [...step.paths.map((p) => p.steps), step.otherwise];
}

/// The step with the id `id`, at any depth.
export function findStep(steps: Step[], id: string): Step | undefined {
  return allSteps(steps).find((step) => step.id === id);
}

/// A new step id for `kind`: `<kind>-<n>`, the smallest n that no step of `steps` has. The
/// builder makes it once, when the step is added, and never changes it, so a step keeps its
/// id through moves, renames and saves (the core's rule: 1 to 40 of a-z, 0-9, '-' and '_').
export function nextStepId(kind: StepKindName, steps: Step[]): string {
  const taken = new Set(allSteps(steps).map((step) => step.id));
  let n = 1;
  while (taken.has(`${kind}-${n}`)) n += 1;
  return `${kind}-${n}`;
}

/// A new step of `kind` for `team`'s workflow whose steps are `steps`, with defaults that a save
/// takes where it can: an agent step done by the first member; a check that sends back to the
/// step before it; a deploy that keeps the branches and pushes nothing; a branch on the project
/// with a path per project of the team.
export function newStep(
  kind: StepKindName,
  steps: Step[],
  team: Pick<Team, "members" | "projects">,
): Step {
  const id = nextStepId(kind, steps);
  switch (kind) {
    case "agent":
      return {
        id,
        name: "Work",
        kind,
        member: team.members[0]?.dot_id ?? "",
        job: "Work on the task.",
        can_send_back_to: [],
        outcomes: [],
        delegate: null,
      };
    case "check":
      return { id, name: "Your check", kind, send_back_to: null };
    case "deploy":
      return { id, name: "Deploy", kind, mode: "branch" };
    case "branch": {
      const labels = team.projects.map((p) => p.name);
      return {
        id,
        name: "Branch",
        kind,
        on: "project",
        paths: (labels.length > 0 ? labels : ["Path 1"]).map((label) => ({ label, steps: [] })),
        otherwise: [],
      };
    }
  }
}

/// `steps` with the list `ref` replaced by `change(list)`. A ref to a branch or path that is not
/// there changes nothing.
export function editList(steps: Step[], ref: ListRef, change: (list: Step[]) => Step[]): Step[] {
  if (ref.branch === null) return change(steps);
  const { branch, path } = ref;
  return updateStep(steps, branch, (step) => {
    if (step.kind !== "branch") return step;
    if (path === "otherwise") return { ...step, otherwise: change(step.otherwise) };
    if (!step.paths[path]) return step;
    const paths = step.paths.map((p, i) => (i === path ? { ...p, steps: change(p.steps) } : p));
    return { ...step, paths } satisfies BranchStep;
  });
}

/// `steps` with `step` put into the list `ref` at `index` (its end when past it).
export function insertStep(steps: Step[], ref: ListRef, index: number, step: Step): Step[] {
  return editList(steps, ref, (list) => [...list.slice(0, index), step, ...list.slice(index)]);
}

/// `steps` with the step `id`, at any depth, replaced by `change(step)`.
export function updateStep(steps: Step[], id: string, change: (step: Step) => Step): Step[] {
  return steps.map((step) => {
    if (step.id === id) return change(step);
    if (step.kind !== "branch") return step;
    return {
      ...step,
      paths: step.paths.map((p) => ({ ...p, steps: updateStep(p.steps, id, change) })),
      otherwise: updateStep(step.otherwise, id, change),
    };
  });
}

/// Where the step `id` is: its list and its place in it; undefined when no step has the id.
export function locateStep(
  steps: Step[],
  id: string,
  ref: ListRef = TOP,
): { ref: ListRef; index: number; length: number } | undefined {
  for (const [index, step] of steps.entries()) {
    if (step.id === id) return { ref, index, length: steps.length };
    if (step.kind !== "branch") continue;
    const inner = [
      ...step.paths.map((p, path) => locateStep(p.steps, id, { branch: step.id, path })),
      locateStep(step.otherwise, id, { branch: step.id, path: "otherwise" }),
    ].find((found) => found !== undefined);
    if (inner) return inner;
  }
  return undefined;
}

/// `steps` without the step `id` (and, for a branch, the steps inside it).
export function deleteStep(steps: Step[], id: string): Step[] {
  const at = locateStep(steps, id);
  if (!at) return steps;
  return editList(steps, at.ref, (list) => list.filter((step) => step.id !== id));
}

/// Whether the step `id` can move `by` places (-1 up, 1 down) within its own list.
export function canMove(steps: Step[], id: string, by: -1 | 1): boolean {
  const at = locateStep(steps, id);
  if (!at) return false;
  const to = at.index + by;
  return to >= 0 && to < at.length;
}

/// `steps` with the step `id` moved `by` places (-1 up, 1 down) within its own list: a step
/// stays on its path. At either end of its list it stays where it is.
export function moveStep(steps: Step[], id: string, by: -1 | 1): Step[] {
  const at = locateStep(steps, id);
  if (!at || !canMove(steps, id, by)) return steps;
  return editList(steps, at.ref, (list) => {
    const next = [...list];
    const [step] = next.splice(at.index, 1);
    next.splice(at.index + by, 0, step!);
    return next;
  });
}
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/builder/labels.ts`:**

```ts
import type { AgentStep, DeployMode, DeployStep, Step, Trigger } from "@/api/types";

import { taskLine, TRIGGER_LABEL } from "../format";
import { findStep, type StepKindName } from "./draft";

/// A step's kind as the Add step menu and the cards name it.
export const KIND_LABEL: Record<StepKindName, string> = {
  agent: "Agent step",
  check: "Check",
  deploy: "Deploy",
  branch: "Branch",
};

/// What a deploy of each mode does with the approved branches (section 5.5).
export const DEPLOY_LABEL: Record<DeployMode, string> = {
  branch: "Keeps the branches: nothing is pushed",
  push: "Pushes the branches",
  pr: "Pushes the branches and opens a pull request",
  pr_merge: "Pushes, opens a pull request and merges it",
  member: "A member deploys",
};

/// A team member's name by dot id; an id that is no member shows as itself.
export type NameOf = (dotId: string) => string;

/// The trigger card's lines: what starts the workflow, then its detail.
export function triggerSummary(trigger: Trigger, nameOf: NameOf): [string, string] {
  const title = TRIGGER_LABEL[trigger.kind];
  switch (trigger.kind) {
    case "manual":
      return [title, "Run now on the team page, or Test run here"];
    case "chat":
      return [
        title,
        trigger.starters.length > 0
          ? `When you chat with ${trigger.starters.map(nameOf).join(", ")}`
          : "No member starts it yet",
      ];
    case "schedule":
      return [
        title,
        `${taskLine(trigger.task) || "No task yet"} · on ${trigger.project || "no project yet"}`,
      ];
    case "webhook":
      return [
        title,
        trigger.task
          ? `${taskLine(trigger.task)}, unless the call says the task`
          : "The call says the task",
      ];
  }
}

/// "may start Dev agent · 3 at once · 6 total": what a delegate step may start (section 10.2).
export function delegateLine(step: AgentStep, nameOf: NameOf): string | undefined {
  const d = step.delegate;
  if (!d) return undefined;
  const who = d.may_start.length > 0 ? d.may_start.map(nameOf).join(", ") : "no member yet";
  return `may start ${who} · ${d.at_once} at once · ${d.total} total`;
}

/// What a deploy does, with its mode's own field: the merge method, or the member and job.
export function deployLine(step: DeployStep, nameOf: NameOf): string {
  switch (step.mode) {
    case "pr_merge":
      return `${DEPLOY_LABEL.pr_merge} (${step.merge_method})`;
    case "member":
      return `${nameOf(step.member)} deploys: ${taskLine(step.job)}`;
    default:
      return DEPLOY_LABEL[step.mode];
  }
}

/// The card's line under the step's name, by its kind. `steps` is the whole draft, for the
/// name of the step a check sends back to.
export function stepLine(step: Step, steps: Step[], nameOf: NameOf): string {
  switch (step.kind) {
    case "agent":
      return `${nameOf(step.member)} · ${taskLine(step.job)}`;
    case "check": {
      const to = step.send_back_to ? findStep(steps, step.send_back_to) : undefined;
      return to
        ? `Approve, or send back to ${to.name} with a note`
        : "Approve, or send back with a note";
    }
    case "deploy":
      return deployLine(step, nameOf);
    case "branch":
      return step.on === "outcome"
        ? "Goes on by the outcome reported before it"
        : "Goes on by the project";
  }
}
```

### Task 10: A test run starts while its workflow is off

`Workflows::start`'s `enabled` check lets one start through: a Test run, `test: true` with the `manual` trigger. Run now, and a chat, schedule or webhook start (with or without `test`), are still refused `workflow <name> is turned off`; the order of the checks is unchanged, so a problem still refuses a Test run. The scheduler, the webhook and `startable_workflows` keep their own checks. No type changes, so the app and the UI do not change.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/dots-core/src/workflows.rs`
- Test: `crates/dots-core/tests/workflows_test.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: plan A's `Workflows::start(StartRequest { workflow_id, project_id, task, trigger, origin_run_id, test })`.
- Produces: nothing new; `start`'s doc comment says "turned off (except a Test run)".

**In `crates/dots-core/tests/workflows_test.rs`, replace:**

```rust

        .await
        .unwrap()
        .is_empty());
}

```

**with:**

```rust

        .await
        .unwrap()
        .is_empty());

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_test_run_starts_while_its_workflow_is_off_and_run_now_does_not() {
    let w = world().await;
    let flow = w.standard().await;
    let mut spec = w.store.get_workflow(&flow).await.unwrap().spec;
    spec.enabled = false;
    w.store.update_workflow(&flow, &spec).await.unwrap();
    let run_now = StartRequest {
        workflow_id: flow.clone(),
        project_id: w.project.clone(),
        task: "The tray icon is invisible in dark mode.".into(),
        trigger: WorkflowTrigger::Manual,
        origin_run_id: None,
        test: false,
    };
    // Run now (a manual start that is not a test) is still refused, with the same words.
    assert!(matches!(
        w.workflows.start(run_now.clone()).await,
        Err(Error::Conflict(m)) if m == "workflow Fix is turned off"
    ));
    // Only the manual trigger may test an off workflow: a start from a trigger `enabled` arms
    // is refused even when it asks for a test.
    for trigger in [
        WorkflowTrigger::Chat,
        WorkflowTrigger::Schedule,
        WorkflowTrigger::Webhook,
    ] {
        assert!(matches!(
            w.workflows
                .start(StartRequest { trigger, test: true, ..run_now.clone() })
                .await,
            Err(Error::Conflict(m)) if m == "workflow Fix is turned off"
        ));
    }
    assert!(w
        .store
        .list_workflow_runs(None, 10)
        .await
        .unwrap()
        .is_empty());
    // A Test run starts, is flagged `test`, and goes on to the check as any run does.
    let started = w
        .workflows
        .start(StartRequest {
            test: true,
            ..run_now.clone()
        })
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert!(started.test);
    assert_eq!(started.trigger, WorkflowTrigger::Manual);
    assert_eq!(started.status, WorkflowRunStatus::Running);
    let wr = w.until(&started.id, waits_on(WaitingOn::Check)).await;
    assert!(wr.test);
    // The workflow is still off, and Run now is still refused.
    assert!(!w.store.get_workflow(&flow).await.unwrap().spec.enabled);
    assert!(matches!(
        w.workflows.start(run_now).await,
        Err(Error::Conflict(m)) if m == "workflow Fix is turned off"
    ));
    let runs = w.store.list_workflow_runs(None, 10).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(runs.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(runs[0].id, started.id);
}

```

```rust
        // `enabled` arms the chat, schedule and webhook triggers and Run now. A Test run (the
        // person's manual start with `test`) starts while the workflow is off, so a new
        // workflow can be tried before it is armed.
        let test_run = req.test && matches!(req.trigger, WorkflowTrigger::Manual);
        if !workflow.spec.enabled && !test_run {
            return Err(Error::Conflict(format!("workflow {name} is turned off")));
```

### Task 11: Each step's settings open in a side sheet, with the trigger's schedule and webhook

The step sheet gets each kind's fields: an agent's member, job, send-back targets, outcomes and delegate; a check's target; a deploy's mode, merge method, and member and job; a branch's `on` and its paths. The trigger card opens a trigger sheet: the kind, a chat's starters, a schedule (the dot editor's `ScheduleEditor` with no None and no catch-up, its task and project), and a webhook's URL, token shown once, and fallbacks. A member in folder mode gets a warning. Test run no longer waits for the switch (Task 10). UI only.

**Files:**
- Create: `ui/src/screens/teams/builder/{StepSheet,TriggerSheet,fields}.tsx`
- Modify: `ui/src/screens/teams/builder/{cards.tsx,WorkflowBuilderScreen.tsx,draft.ts,labels.ts}`, `ui/src/screens/dot-editor/{ScheduleEditor,WebhookPanel}.tsx`
- Test: `ui/src/screens/teams/builder/{StepSheet,TriggerSheet}.test.tsx` (new), `ui/src/screens/teams/builder/draft.test.ts`, `ui/src/screens/dot-editor/ScheduleEditor.test.tsx`, `ui/src/screens/teams/builder/WorkflowBuilderScreen.test.tsx` (`saves the draft before a test run, then asks for its task` becomes `…, on or off`: Test run stays enabled while off, saves the switch, and opens `Test run Nightly 2`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 9's `StepSheet` shell (moved here out of `cards.tsx`), `TriggerCard`, `updateStep`, `KIND_LABEL`, `DEPLOY_LABEL`; Task 6's `workflowWebhookUrl`, `regenerateWorkflowToken`, `keys.workflowWebhookUrl`; `previewSchedule`, `scheduleError`, `Dot.workspace_mode` (`keys.dots`).
- Produces: `StepSheet({ step, steps, team, nameOf, onChange, onClose })`, `TriggerSheet({ open, trigger, enabled, workflow, team, nameOf, onChange, onClose })`; `builder/fields.tsx`: `Group({ legend, hint?, children })`, `MemberSelect`, `ChoiceList`, `FolderWarning({ dotIds, nameOf })`; `draft.ts`: `MAX_NAME = 80`, `MAX_JOB = 8_000`, `MAX_OUTCOME = 40`, `MAX_TASK = 8_000`, `NEW_DELEGATE`, `stepsBefore(steps, id)` (as `Definition::before`), `agentsBefore`, `outcomeSource`, `withMode(step, mode, team)`, `withTriggerKind(trigger, kind, team, schedule)`, `newPathLabel(step, suggestions)`, `toggled(list, item, on)`; `labels.ts`: `MODE_OPTIONS`, `MERGE_OPTIONS`, `ON_OPTIONS`; `TriggerCard` takes `onOpen` and gets `Settings of the trigger`; `LimitField({ id, label, value, onChange })` exported from `cards.tsx`; `CopyButton({ what, text })` exported from `WebhookPanel.tsx`; `ScheduleEditor`'s `required?: boolean`, `catchUp?: boolean`. Labels: `Trigger settings`, `What starts it`, `Started by`, `Member`, `Job`, `May send work back to`, `Outcomes`, `New outcome`, `Add outcome`, `It may start other members`, `May start`, `At once`, `Total`, `Send back to`, `The nearest agent step before it`, `Mode`, `Merge method`, `Goes by`, `Paths`, `Label of path <n>`, `Add path`, `Webhook URL`, `Token`, `Regenerate`, `Make a token`, `Task when the call has none`, `Project when the call has none`, and the folder-mode note `<name> works in the folder itself: in a workflow it changes the project's own checkout, and leaves no branch to check or deploy.`.
