**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/builder/StepSheet.test.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import type { Dot, Step, Team, Workflow, WorkflowSpec } from "@/api/types";
import { backend } from "@/test/backend";
import { sample } from "@/test/fixtures";
import { renderApp } from "@/test/render";

import { findStep } from "./draft";

const [crew] = sample.teams as [Team, Team];
const [fix, , nightly] = sample.workflows as [Workflow, Workflow, Workflow, Workflow];

/// The builder of `workflow`, with no problem in any draft, whose save answers with what it
/// was sent.
function open(workflow: Workflow) {
  backend.on("team_get", () => crew);
  backend.on("workflow_get", () => workflow);
  backend.on("workflow_check_spec", () => null);
  backend.on("workflow_update", (args) => ({ ...workflow, ...(args.spec as WorkflowSpec) }));
  return renderApp(`/teams/${crew.id}/workflows/${workflow.id}`);
}

/// Opens the settings of the step `name` and gives its sheet.
async function settings(user: ReturnType<typeof open>["user"], name: string, title: string) {
  await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: `Settings of ${name}` }));
  return within(await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: title }));
}

/// Closes the sheet, saves the draft, and gives the step `id` the save sent.
async function saved(user: ReturnType<typeof open>["user"], id: string): Promise<Step> {
  await user.keyboard("{Escape}");
  await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByRole("dialog")).not.toBeInTheDocument());
  const save = screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save" });
  await waitFor(() => expect(save).toBeEnabled());
  await user.click(save);
  const spec = backend.callsTo("workflow_update").at(-1)!.spec as WorkflowSpec;
  return findStep(spec.definition.steps, id)!;
}

const dot = (id: string, name: string, mode: Dot["workspace_mode"]): Dot => ({
  ...sample.dot,
  id,
  name,
  workspace_mode: mode,
});

describe("a step's settings sheet", () => {
  it("edits an agent step's member, job, send-backs and outcomes in the draft", async () => {
    const { user } = open(fix);
    const sheet = await settings(user, "Design", "Agent step settings");
    expect(sheet.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Name" })).toHaveAttribute("maxlength", "80");
    const member = sheet.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Member" });
    expect(member).toHaveValue("01J0DOT2");
    expect(within(member).getAllByRole("option").map((o) => o.textContent)).toEqual([
      "nightly-tests",
      "reviewer",
    ]);
    await user.selectOptions(member, "nightly-tests");
    const job = sheet.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Job" });
    expect(job).toHaveValue("Propose a design.");
    expect(job).toHaveAttribute("maxlength", "8000");
    await user.clear(job);
    await user.type(job, "Draw it.");

    // Only the agent step before it on its path: Build, not the steps of the path beside it.
    const back = within(sheet.getByRole("group", { name: "May send work back to" }));
    expect(back.getAllByRole("checkbox").map((b) => b.getAttribute("aria-label"))).toEqual([
      "Build",
    ]);
    expect(back.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Build" })).toBeChecked();
    await user.click(back.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Build" }));

    const outcomes = within(sheet.getByRole("group", { name: "Outcomes" }));
    const next = outcomes.getByRole("textbox", { name: "New outcome" });
    expect(next).toHaveAttribute("maxlength", "40");
    await user.type(next, " done {Enter}");
    await user.type(next, "later");
    await user.click(outcomes.getByRole("button", { name: "Add outcome" }));
    // The same label twice is not taken.
    await user.type(next, "done");
    expect(outcomes.getByRole("button", { name: "Add outcome" })).toBeDisabled();
    expect(outcomes.getByText("It is listed already.")).toBeVisible();
    await user.clear(next);
    await user.click(outcomes.getByRole("button", { name: "Remove the outcome later" }));
    expect(outcomes.getByRole("list", { name: "Outcomes" })).toHaveTextContent("done");
    expect(outcomes.getByRole("list", { name: "Outcomes" })).not.toHaveTextContent("later");

    // The card follows the draft.
    expect(screen.getByRole("group", { name: "Step 2.1: Design", hidden: true })).toHaveTextContent(
      "nightly-tests · Draw it.",
    );
    expect(await saved(user, "design")).toEqual({
      id: "design",
      name: "Design",
      kind: "agent",
      member: "01J0DOT",
      job: "Draw it.",
      can_send_back_to: [],
      outcomes: ["done"],
      delegate: null,
    });
  });

  it("makes an agent step a delegate with what it may start, and back", async () => {
    const { user } = open(fix);
    let sheet = await settings(user, "Build", "Agent step settings");
    const delegate = sheet.getByRole("switch", { name: "It may start other members" });
    expect(delegate).toBeChecked();
    const may = within(sheet.getByRole("group", { name: "May start" }));
    expect(may.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "reviewer" })).toBeChecked();
    expect(may.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "nightly-tests" })).not.toBeChecked();
    await user.click(may.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "nightly-tests" }));
    expect(sheet.getByRole("spinbutton", { name: "At once" })).toHaveValue(2);
    const total = sheet.getByRole("spinbutton", { name: "Total" });
    expect(total).toHaveValue(4);
    await user.clear(total);
    await user.type(total, "5");
    expect(screen.getByRole("group", { name: "Step 1: Build", hidden: true })).toHaveTextContent(
      "may start reviewer, nightly-tests · 2 at once · 5 total",
    );
    expect(await saved(user, "build")).toMatchObject({
      delegate: { may_start: ["01J0DOT2", "01J0DOT"], at_once: 2, total: 5 },
    });

    // Off, then on again: the section 5.3 defaults, with no member yet.
    sheet = await settings(user, "Build", "Agent step settings");
    await user.click(sheet.getByRole("switch", { name: "It may start other members" }));
    expect(sheet.queryByRole("group", { name: "May start" })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(await saved(user, "build")).toMatchObject({ delegate: null });
    sheet = await settings(user, "Build", "Agent step settings");
    await user.click(sheet.getByRole("switch", { name: "It may start other members" }));
    expect(sheet.getByRole("spinbutton", { name: "At once" })).toHaveValue(3);
    expect(sheet.getByRole("spinbutton", { name: "Total" })).toHaveValue(6);
    expect(await saved(user, "build")).toMatchObject({
      delegate: { may_start: [], at_once: 3, total: 6 },
    });
  });

  it("warns about a member that works in the folder itself", async () => {
    backend.on("list_dots", () => [
      dot("01J0DOT", "nightly-tests", "worktree"),
      dot("01J0DOT2", "reviewer", "folder"),
    ]);
    const { user } = open(fix);
    const warning =
      "reviewer works in the folder itself: in a workflow it changes the project's own " +
      "checkout, and leaves no branch to check or deploy.";
    let sheet = await settings(user, "Design", "Agent step settings");
    expect(await sheet.findByRole("note")).toHaveTextContent(warning);
    await user.selectOptions(sheet.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Member" }), "nightly-tests");
    expect(sheet.queryByRole("note")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.keyboard("{Escape}");
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByRole("dialog")).not.toBeInTheDocument());

    // A member a delegate may start works in the project too.
    sheet = await settings(user, "Build", "Agent step settings");
    expect(await sheet.findByRole("note")).toHaveTextContent(warning);
  });

  it("picks the agent step a check sends back to, or the nearest one", async () => {
    const { user } = open(fix);
    const sheet = await settings(user, "Review", "Check settings");
    const to = sheet.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Send back to" });
    expect(to).toHaveValue("build");
    expect(within(to).getAllByRole("option").map((o) => o.textContent)).toEqual([
      "The nearest agent step before it",
      "Build",
    ]);
    await user.selectOptions(to, "The nearest agent step before it");
    expect(screen.getByRole("group", { name: "Step 2.1: Review", hidden: true })).toHaveTextContent(
      "Approve, or send back with a note",
    );
    expect(await saved(user, "review")).toEqual({
      id: "review",
      name: "Review",
      kind: "check",
      send_back_to: null,
    });
  });

  it("changes a deploy's mode, its merge method, and the member who deploys", async () => {
    const { user } = open(fix);
    let sheet = await settings(user, "Ship", "Deploy settings");
    const mode = sheet.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Mode" });
    expect(mode).toHaveValue("pr_merge");
    expect(within(mode).getAllByRole("option").map((o) => o.textContent)).toEqual([
      "Keep the branches",
      "Push",
      "Push and open a pull request",
      "Push, open a pull request and merge it",
      "A member deploys",
    ]);
    const method = sheet.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Merge method" });
    expect(method).toHaveValue("squash");
    await user.selectOptions(method, "Rebase");
    expect(await saved(user, "ship")).toMatchObject({ mode: "pr_merge", merge_method: "rebase" });

    sheet = await settings(user, "Ship", "Deploy settings");
    await user.selectOptions(sheet.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Mode" }), "A member deploys");
    expect(sheet.queryByRole("combobox", { name: "Merge method" })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(sheet.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Member" })).toHaveValue("01J0DOT");
    await user.selectOptions(sheet.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Member" }), "reviewer");
    const job = sheet.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Job" });
    await user.clear(job);
    await user.type(job, "Tag a release.");
    expect(await saved(user, "ship")).toEqual({
      id: "ship",
      name: "Ship",
      kind: "deploy",
      mode: "member",
      member: "01J0DOT2",
      job: "Tag a release.",
    });

    sheet = await settings(user, "Ship", "Deploy settings");
    await user.selectOptions(sheet.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Mode" }), "Push");
    expect(sheet.queryByRole("combobox", { name: "Member" })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(sheet.getByText("Pushes the branches")).toBeVisible();
    expect(await saved(user, "ship")).toEqual({
      id: "ship",
      name: "Ship",
      kind: "deploy",
      mode: "push",
    });
  });

  it("adds, renames and removes a branch's paths, and says what Otherwise holds", async () => {
    const { user } = open(fix);
    const sheet = await settings(user, "Fixed?", "Branch settings");
    const on = sheet.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Goes by" });
    expect(on).toHaveValue("outcome");
    expect(
      sheet.getByText("A path's label is an outcome of Build: fixed, needs_design."),
    ).toBeVisible();
    expect(
      sheet.getByText("Otherwise holds 1 step, run when no path's label matches."),
    ).toBeVisible();
    expect(sheet.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Label of path 1" })).toHaveValue("fixed");

    // A new path takes the outcome no path has yet; its steps are added on its cards.
    await user.click(sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Add path" }));
    const second = sheet.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Label of path 2" });
    expect(second).toHaveValue("needs_design");
    expect(second).toHaveAttribute("maxlength", "40");
    expect(
      screen.getByRole("region", { name: "Path needs_design", hidden: true }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.clear(second);
    await user.type(second, "later");
    expect(screen.getByRole("region", { name: "Path later", hidden: true })).toBeInTheDocument();

    // A path with steps on it asks before it goes; an empty one goes at once.
    await user.click(sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Remove the path fixed" }));
    const ask = await screen.findByRole("alertdialog", { name: "Remove the path fixed?" });
    expect(ask).toHaveTextContent(
      "The 2 steps on it go with it. Nothing is saved until you press Save.",
    );
    await user.click(within(ask).getByRole("button", { name: "Remove path" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByRole("alertdialog")).not.toBeInTheDocument());
    expect(sheet.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Label of path 1" })).toHaveValue("later");

    await user.selectOptions(sheet.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Goes by" }), "The project");
    expect(sheet.getByText("A path's label is a project's name: Dots, Site.")).toBeVisible();
    await user.click(sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Remove the path later" }));
    expect(sheet.queryByRole("textbox", { name: "Label of path 1" })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Add path" }));
    expect(sheet.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Label of path 1" })).toHaveValue("Dots");

    expect(await saved(user, "route")).toMatchObject({
      on: "project",
      paths: [{ label: "Dots", steps: [] }],
      otherwise: [findStep(fix.definition.steps, "design")],
    });
  });

  it("says what a branch on outcome can go by when the step before declares none", async () => {
    const { user } = open(nightly);
    const sheet = await settings(user, "Which project", "Branch settings");
    expect(
      sheet.getByText(
        "Otherwise is empty: when no path's label matches, the workflow goes on after the branch.",
      ),
    ).toBeVisible();
    expect(sheet.getByText("A path's label is a project's name: Dots, Site.")).toBeVisible();
    await user.selectOptions(
      sheet.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Goes by" }),
      "The outcome reported before it",
    );
    // Work, the agent step before it, declares no outcome.
    expect(sheet.getByText("A path's label is an outcome of Work: it has none yet.")).toBeVisible();
  });
});
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/builder/TriggerSheet.test.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { act, screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import type { Team, Trigger, Workflow, WorkflowSpec } from "@/api/types";
import { backend } from "@/test/backend";
import { sample } from "@/test/fixtures";
import { renderApp } from "@/test/render";

const [crew] = sample.teams as [Team, Team];
const [fix, pushIt, nightly, onIssue] = sample.workflows as [
  Workflow,
  Workflow,
  Workflow,
  Workflow,
];

/// The builder of `workflow`, with no problem in any draft, whose save answers with what it
/// was sent.
function open(workflow: Workflow) {
  backend.on("team_get", () => crew);
  backend.on("workflow_get", () => workflow);
  backend.on("workflow_check_spec", () => null);
  backend.on("workflow_update", (args) => ({ ...workflow, ...(args.spec as WorkflowSpec) }));
  return renderApp(`/teams/${crew.id}/workflows/${workflow.id}`);
}

type User = ReturnType<typeof open>["user"];

/// Opens the trigger's settings from its card and gives the sheet.
async function settings(user: User) {
  await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Settings of the trigger" }));
  return within(await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Trigger settings" }));
}

/// Closes the sheet, saves the draft, and gives the trigger the save sent.
async function saved(user: User): Promise<Trigger> {
  await user.keyboard("{Escape}");
  await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByRole("dialog")).not.toBeInTheDocument());
  const save = screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save" });
  await waitFor(() => expect(save).toBeEnabled());
  await user.click(save);
  return (backend.callsTo("workflow_update").at(-1)!.spec as WorkflowSpec).definition.trigger;
}

const trigger = () => screen.getByRole("group", { name: "Trigger", hidden: true });

describe("the trigger's settings sheet", () => {
  it("opens from the trigger card and picks the members who start it from a chat", async () => {
    const { user } = open(fix);
    // A click on the card, or Enter on it, opens the settings too.
    await user.click(await screen.findByText("When you chat with nightly-tests"));
    expect(await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Trigger settings" })).toBeVisible();
    await user.keyboard("{Escape}");
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByRole("dialog")).not.toBeInTheDocument());
    act(() => trigger().focus());
    await user.keyboard("{Enter}");
    const sheet = within(await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Trigger settings" }));

    const kind = sheet.getByRole("combobox", { name: "What starts it" });
    expect(kind).toHaveValue("chat");
    expect(within(kind).getAllByRole("option").map((o) => o.textContent)).toEqual([
      "Run by hand",
      "Started from a chat",
      "On a schedule",
      "On a webhook",
    ]);
    const by = within(sheet.getByRole("group", { name: "Started by" }));
    expect(by.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "nightly-tests" })).toBeChecked();
    expect(by.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "reviewer" })).not.toBeChecked();
    await user.click(by.getByRole("checkbox", { name: "reviewer" }));
    expect(trigger()).toHaveTextContent("When you chat with nightly-tests, reviewer");
    // It is on: no note that the trigger starts nothing.
    expect(sheet.queryByRole("note")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(await saved(user)).toEqual({ kind: "chat", starters: ["01J0DOT", "01J0DOT2"] });
  });

  it("puts a workflow on a schedule with the schedule editor, a task and a project", async () => {
    const { user } = open(pushIt);
    const sheet = await settings(user);
    // Push it is off: its trigger starts nothing until it is on, and a Test run still works.
    expect(sheet.getByRole("note")).toHaveTextContent(
      "The workflow is off: this trigger starts nothing until you turn it on and save. A Test " +
        "run works while it is off.",
    );
    const kinds = sheet.getByRole("combobox", { name: "What starts it" });
    await user.selectOptions(kinds, "On a schedule");
    // Always a schedule, and never a catch-up (section 5.1).
    const kind = sheet.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Kind" });
    expect(kind).toHaveValue("daily");
    expect(within(kind).queryByRole("option", { name: "None" })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(sheet.queryByLabelText("Catch up a missed run")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(await sheet.findByRole("list", { name: "Next runs" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    const time = sheet.getByLabelText("Time");
    await user.clear(time);
    await user.type(time, "07:30");

    const task = sheet.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Task" });
    expect(task).toHaveAttribute("maxlength", "8000");
    await user.type(task, "Update the dependencies.");
    const project = sheet.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Project" });
    expect(project).toHaveValue("Dots");
    await user.selectOptions(project, "Site");
    expect(trigger()).toHaveTextContent("Update the dependencies. · on Site");
    expect(await saved(user)).toEqual({
      kind: "schedule",
      schedule: { rule: { kind: "daily", time: "07:30" }, catch_up: false, until: null },
      task: "Update the dependencies.",
      project: "Site",
    });
    expect(backend.callsTo("preview_schedule").at(-1)).toMatchObject({ count: 3 });
  });

  it("shows a webhook's URL, makes a new token shown once, and sets its fallbacks", async () => {
    const { user } = open(onIssue);
    const sheet = await settings(user);
    expect(await sheet.findByRole("textbox", { name: "Webhook URL" })).toHaveValue(
      "http://127.0.0.1:47321/workflows/01J0WFL4/trigger",
    );
    expect(sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Copy the webhook URL" })).toBeVisible();
    expect(
      sheet.getByText("This workflow has a token. It was shown once, when it was made."),
    ).toBeVisible();
    // Saved with this trigger: calls reach it.
    expect(sheet.queryByRole("note")).not.toBeInTheDocument();

    backend.on("regenerate_workflow_token", () => "wf-token-2");
    await user.click(sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Regenerate" }));
    const ask = await screen.findByRole("alertdialog", { name: "Make a new token?" });
    await user.click(within(ask).getByRole("button", { name: "Make a new token" }));
    expect(await sheet.findByRole("textbox", { name: "Token" })).toHaveValue("wf-token-2");
    expect(sheet.getByText("Copy it now: you won't see this again.")).toBeVisible();
    expect(backend.callsTo("regenerate_workflow_token")).toEqual([{ id: "01J0WFL4" }]);

    const task = sheet.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Task when the call has none" });
    expect(task).toHaveValue("Look at the newest issue.");
    await user.clear(task);
    const project = sheet.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Project when the call has none" });
    expect(project).toHaveValue("");
    expect(within(project).getAllByRole("option").map((o) => o.textContent)).toEqual([
      "None: the call names it",
      "Dots",
      "Site",
    ]);
    await user.selectOptions(project, "Site");
    expect(trigger()).toHaveTextContent("The call says the task");
    expect(await saved(user)).toEqual({ kind: "webhook", task: null, project: "Site" });
    // The token is shown once: the sheet opened again does not show it.
    const again = await settings(user);
    expect(again.queryByRole("textbox", { name: "Token" })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      again.getByText("This workflow has a token. It was shown once, when it was made."),
    ).toBeVisible();
  });

  it("says a webhook not saved yet refuses calls, and why its URL is missing", async () => {
    backend.on("workflow_webhook_url", () => {
      throw { kind: "conflict", message: "the local server is not running" };
    });
    const { user } = open(nightly);
    const sheet = await settings(user);
    const kinds = sheet.getByRole("combobox", { name: "What starts it" });
    await user.selectOptions(kinds, "On a webhook");
    expect(sheet.getByRole("note")).toHaveTextContent(
      "Calls are refused until the workflow is saved with this trigger.",
    );
    expect(await sheet.findByText("the local server is not running")).toBeVisible();
    expect(
      sheet.getByText("This workflow has no token yet, so its webhook refuses every call."),
    ).toBeVisible();
    backend.on("regenerate_workflow_token", () => "wf-token-1");
    await user.click(sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Make a token" }));
    expect(await sheet.findByRole("textbox", { name: "Token" })).toHaveValue("wf-token-1");
    // The schedule's task and project carry over as the fallbacks.
    expect(sheet.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Task when the call has none" })).toHaveValue(
      "Update the dependencies.",
    );
    expect(sheet.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Project when the call has none" })).toHaveValue(
      "Dots",
    );
    expect(await saved(user)).toEqual({
      kind: "webhook",
      task: "Update the dependencies.",
      project: "Dots",
    });
  });
});
```

```ts
  it("finds the steps before a step on its path, as the core's before does", () => {
    const names = (steps: Step[]) => steps.map((s) => s.id);
    // Inside a path: the steps before the branch, the branch, then the path's earlier steps.
    expect(names(stepsBefore(fixSteps, "ship"))).toEqual(["build", "route", "review"]);
    // Otherwise does not see the paths beside it.

    expect(agentsBefore(fixSteps, "build")).toEqual([]);
    expect(outcomeSource(fixSteps, "route")?.outcomes).toEqual(["fixed", "needs_design"]);
    expect(outcomeSource(fixSteps, "build")).toBeUndefined();

  it("gives a deploy of a new mode and a trigger of a new kind defaults a save takes", () => {
    const ship = findStep(fixSteps, "ship") as DeployStep;
    expect(withMode(ship, "pr_merge", crew)).toBe(ship);
    expect(withMode(ship, "push", crew)).toEqual({
      id: "ship",
      name: "Ship",
      kind: "deploy",
      mode: "push",
    });
    const member = withMode(ship, "member", crew);
    expect(member).toEqual({
      id: "ship",
      name: "Ship",
      kind: "deploy",
      mode: "member",
      member: "01J0DOT",
      job: "Deploy what was approved.",
    });
    expect(withMode(member, "pr_merge", crew)).toMatchObject({ merge_method: "squash" });
    for (const mode of ["branch", "push", "pr", "pr_merge", "member"] as const) {
      expect(Step.safeParse(withMode(ship, mode, crew)).success).toBe(true);
    }

    const daily = (nightly.definition.trigger as Extract<Trigger, { kind: "schedule" }>).schedule;
    const manual: Trigger = { kind: "manual" };
    expect(withTriggerKind(manual, "chat", crew, daily)).toEqual({
      kind: "chat",
      starters: ["01J0DOT"],
    });
    expect(withTriggerKind(manual, "schedule", crew, daily)).toEqual({
      kind: "schedule",
      schedule: daily,
      task: "",
      project: "Dots",
    });
    expect(withTriggerKind(manual, "webhook", crew, daily)).toEqual({
      kind: "webhook",
      task: null,
      project: null,
    });
    // A schedule's task and project become a webhook's fallbacks, and come back.
    const hook = withTriggerKind(nightly.definition.trigger, "webhook", crew, daily);
    expect(hook).toEqual({ kind: "webhook", task: "Update the dependencies.", project: "Dots" });
    expect(withTriggerKind(hook, "schedule", crew, daily)).toEqual(nightly.definition.trigger);
    expect(withTriggerKind(hook, "manual", crew, daily)).toEqual(manual);
    expect(withTriggerKind(hook, "webhook", crew, daily)).toBe(hook);
  });

  it("names a new path after a free outcome or project, else Path n, and ticks a choice once", () => {
    const route = findStep(fixSteps, "route") as BranchStep;
    expect(newPathLabel(route, ["fixed", "needs_design"])).toBe("needs_design");
    // Labels are compared without case, as the core compares them.
    expect(newPathLabel(route, ["FIXED"])).toBe("Path 1");
    const taken = { ...route, paths: [...route.paths, { label: "path 1", steps: [] }] };
    expect(newPathLabel(taken, [])).toBe("Path 2");

    expect(toggled(["a"], "b", true)).toEqual(["a", "b"]);
    expect(toggled(["a", "b"], "a", true)).toEqual(["a", "b"]);
    expect(toggled(["a", "b"], "a", false)).toEqual(["b"]);
  });
});
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/builder/StepSheet.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { Plus, X } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";

import type { AgentStep, BranchStep, CheckStep, DeployStep, Step, Team } from "@/api/types";
import { ConfirmButton } from "@/components/ConfirmButton";
import { Field } from "@/components/Field";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { NativeSelect, NativeSelectOption } from "@/components/ui/native-select";
import {
  Sheet,
  SheetContent,
  SheetDescription,
  SheetHeader,
  SheetTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/sheet";
import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { plural } from "@/screens/library/parts";

import { LimitField } from "./cards";
import {
  agentsBefore,
  findStep,
  MAX_JOB,
  MAX_NAME,
  MAX_OUTCOME,
  NEW_DELEGATE,
  newPathLabel,
  outcomeSource,
  toggled,
  withMode,
} from "./draft";
import { ChoiceList, FolderWarning, Group, MemberSelect } from "./fields";
import {
  DEPLOY_LABEL,
  KIND_LABEL,
  MERGE_OPTIONS,
  MODE_OPTIONS,
  type NameOf,
  ON_OPTIONS,
} from "./labels";

/// What each kind's fields read: the step, the whole draft (for the steps before it), the team
/// and its members' names; `onChange` takes the whole changed step.
type KindProps<S extends Step> = {
  step: S;
  steps: Step[];
  team: Team;
  nameOf: NameOf;
  onChange: (step: Step) => void;
};

/// A step's name in a list of steps to choose from.
const nameIn = (steps: Step[], id: string) => findStep(steps, id)?.name.trim() || id;

/// The side sheet of a step's settings (section 10.2), open while `step` is set: its name, then
/// the fields of its kind. It edits the draft in place, so the live check sees each change; a
/// branch's paths hold steps of their own, which are edited on their cards.
export function StepSheet(props: {
  step: Step | undefined;
  steps: Step[];
  team: Team;
  nameOf: NameOf;
  onChange: (step: Step) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const { step } = props;
  return (
    <Sheet open={step !== undefined} onOpenChange={(open) => !open && props.onClose()}>
      {step && (
        <SheetContent>
          <SheetHeader>
            <SheetTitle>{KIND_LABEL[step.kind]} settings</SheetTitle>
            <SheetDescription>
              Changes go into the draft at once. Save keeps them.
            </SheetDescription>
          </SheetHeader>
          <Field label="Name" htmlFor="step-name" hint="Shown on the card and on the run page.">
            <Input
              id="step-name"
              maxLength={MAX_NAME}
              value={step.name}
              onChange={(e) => props.onChange({ ...step, name: e.target.value })}
            />
          </Field>
          <KindFields {...props} step={step} />
        </SheetContent>
      )}
    </Sheet>
  );
}

function KindFields(props: KindProps<Step>) {
  const { step } = props;
  switch (step.kind) {
    case "agent":
      return <AgentFields {...props} step={step} />;
    case "check":
      return <CheckFields {...props} step={step} />;
    case "deploy":
      return <DeployFields {...props} step={step} />;
    case "branch":
      return <BranchFields {...props} step={step} />;
  }
}

/// The job of a step's member: a textarea within the core's limit.
function JobField(props: { value: string; hint: string; onChange: (job: string) => void }) {
  return (
    <Field label="Job" htmlFor="step-job" hint={props.hint}>
      <Textarea
        id="step-job"
        maxLength={MAX_JOB}
        value={props.value}
        onChange={(e) => props.onChange(e.target.value)}
      />
    </Field>
  );
}

/// An agent step (sections 5.2 and 5.3): its member and job, the earlier agent steps it may
/// send work back to, the outcomes it may report, and whether it is a delegate.
function AgentFields({ step, steps, team, nameOf, onChange }: KindProps<AgentStep>) {
  const earlier = agentsBefore(steps, step.id);
  const d = step.delegate;
  return (
    <>
      <MemberSelect
        id="step-member"
        label="Member"
        hint="The dot of the team that does this step."
        members={team.members}
        value={step.member}
        nameOf={nameOf}
        onChange={(member) => onChange({ ...step, member })}
      />
      <FolderWarning dotIds={[step.member]} nameOf={nameOf} />
      <JobField
        value={step.job}
        hint="What this step does. It is added to the member's own instructions."
        onChange={(job) => onChange({ ...step, job })}
      />
      <ChoiceList
        legend="May send work back to"
        hint="Earlier agent steps this member may send the work back to, with a note."
        options={earlier.map((s) => ({ value: s.id, label: s.name.trim() || "(no name)" }))}
        chosen={step.can_send_back_to}
        stale={(id) => nameIn(steps, id)}
        empty="No agent step comes before it."
        onChange={(id, on) =>
          onChange({ ...step, can_send_back_to: toggled(step.can_send_back_to, id, on) })
        }
      />
      <OutcomeList
        outcomes={step.outcomes}
        onChange={(outcomes) => onChange({ ...step, outcomes })}
      />
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Switch
            id="step-delegate"
            checked={d !== null}
            onCheckedChange={(on) => onChange({ ...step, delegate: on ? NEW_DELEGATE : null })}
          />
          <Label htmlFor="step-delegate">It may start other members</Label>
        </div>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          A delegate step: its member starts members of the team on parts of the task, and the
          step ends when every one of them has ended.
        </p>
      </div>
      {d && (
        <>
          <ChoiceList
            legend="May start"
            options={team.members.map((m) => ({ value: m.dot_id, label: m.dot_name }))}
            chosen={d.may_start}
            stale={nameOf}
            empty="The team has no member yet."
            onChange={(id, on) =>
              onChange({ ...step, delegate: { ...d, may_start: toggled(d.may_start, id, on) } })
            }
          />
          <FolderWarning dotIds={d.may_start} nameOf={nameOf} />
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <LimitField
              id="step-at-once"
              label="At once"
              value={d.at_once}
              onChange={(at_once) => onChange({ ...step, delegate: { ...d, at_once } })}
            />
            <LimitField
              id="step-total"
              label="Total"
              value={d.total}
              onChange={(total) => onChange({ ...step, delegate: { ...d, total } })}
            />
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            At once is a ceiling: the limit on runs at once in Settings still applies.
          </p>
        </>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

/// The labels an agent step may report: each with a remove button, and a field to add one.
function OutcomeList(props: { outcomes: string[]; onChange: (outcomes: string[]) => void }) {
  const [text, setText] = useState("");
  const label = text.trim();
  const twice = props.outcomes.some((o) => o.trim() === label);
  function add() {
    if (label === "" || twice) return;
    props.onChange([...props.outcomes, label]);
    setText("");
  }
  return (
    <Group
      legend="Outcomes"
      hint="Labels the member may report, for a branch on outcome after this step."
    >
      {props.outcomes.length > 0 && (
        <ul aria-label="Outcomes" className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
          {props.outcomes.map((o, i) => (
            <li
              key={i}
              className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md border bg-card py-0.5 pr-0.5 pl-2 text-xs"
            >
              {o}
              <Button
                variant="ghost"
                size="icon-xs"
                aria-label={`Remove the outcome ${o}`}
                title="Remove"
                onClick={() => props.onChange(props.outcomes.filter((_, j) => j !== i))}
              >
                <X aria-hidden />
              </Button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <Input
          aria-label="New outcome"
          placeholder="fixed"
          maxLength={MAX_OUTCOME}
          value={text}
          onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
          onKeyDown={(e) => {
            if (e.key !== "Enter") return;
            e.preventDefault();
            add();
          }}
        />
        <Button variant="outline" size="sm" disabled={label === "" || twice} onClick={add}>
          <Plus aria-hidden /> Add outcome
        </Button>
      </div>
      {label !== "" && twice && <p className="text-xs text-destructive">It is listed already.</p>}
    </Group>
  );
}

/// A check (section 5.4): the agent step a send-back goes to.
function CheckFields({ step, steps, onChange }: KindProps<CheckStep>) {
  const earlier = agentsBefore(steps, step.id);
  const to = step.send_back_to;
  // A target that is no longer before it stays shown until another is picked.
  const stale = to !== null && !earlier.some((s) => s.id === to) ? to : null;
  return (
    <Field
      label="Send back to"
      htmlFor="step-send-back-to"
      hint="Where Send back takes the work, with your note."
    >
      <NativeSelect
        id="step-send-back-to"
        className="w-full"
        value={to ?? ""}
        onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...step, send_back_to: e.target.value || null })}
      >
        <NativeSelectOption value="">The nearest agent step before it</NativeSelectOption>
        {stale !== null && (
          <NativeSelectOption value={stale}>
            {nameIn(steps, stale)} (not before it)
          </NativeSelectOption>
        )}
        {earlier.map((s) => (
          <NativeSelectOption key={s.id} value={s.id}>
            {s.name.trim() || "(no name)"}
          </NativeSelectOption>
        ))}
      </NativeSelect>
    </Field>
  );
}

/// A deploy (section 5.5): its mode, and the mode's own fields.
function DeployFields({ step, team, nameOf, onChange }: KindProps<DeployStep>) {
  return (
    <>
      <Field label="Mode" htmlFor="step-mode" hint={DEPLOY_LABEL[step.mode]}>
        <NativeSelect
          id="step-mode"
          className="w-full"
          value={step.mode}
          onChange={(e) => {
            const mode = MODE_OPTIONS.find(([m]) => m === e.target.value)?.[0];
            if (mode) onChange(withMode(step, mode, team));
          }}
        >
          {MODE_OPTIONS.map(([mode, label]) => (
            <NativeSelectOption key={mode} value={mode}>
              {label}
            </NativeSelectOption>
          ))}
        </NativeSelect>
      </Field>
      {step.mode === "pr_merge" && (
        <Field label="Merge method" htmlFor="step-merge-method">
          <NativeSelect
            id="step-merge-method"
            className="w-full"
            value={step.merge_method}
            onChange={(e) => {
              const method = MERGE_OPTIONS.find(([m]) => m === e.target.value)?.[0];
              if (method) onChange({ ...step, merge_method: method });
            }}
          >
            {MERGE_OPTIONS.map(([method, label]) => (
              <NativeSelectOption key={method} value={method}>
                {label}
              </NativeSelectOption>
            ))}
          </NativeSelect>
        </Field>
      )}
      {step.mode === "member" && (
        <>
          <MemberSelect
            id="step-member"
            label="Member"
            hint="The dot of the team that deploys what the check approved."
            members={team.members}
            value={step.member}
            nameOf={nameOf}
            onChange={(member) => onChange({ ...step, member })}
          />
          <FolderWarning dotIds={[step.member]} nameOf={nameOf} />
          <JobField
            value={step.job}
            hint="How it deploys, for example: tag a release."
            onChange={(job) => onChange({ ...step, job })}
          />
        </>
      )}
      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
        Git and gh run as you, with your own credentials. A Test run pushes nothing: each deploy
        keeps the branches.
      </p>
    </>
  );
}

/// A branch (section 5.6): what it goes by, its paths' labels, and what Otherwise holds.
function BranchFields({ step, steps, team, onChange }: KindProps<BranchStep>) {
  const source = step.on === "outcome" ? outcomeSource(steps, step.id) : undefined;
  const labels =
    step.on === "outcome" ? (source?.outcomes ?? []) : team.projects.map((p) => p.name);
  const listed = labels.join(", ");
  const hint =
    step.on === "project"
      ? `A path's label is a project's name: ${listed || "the team has no project yet"}.`
      : source
        ? `A path's label is an outcome of ${source.name.trim() || "(no name)"}: ` +
          `${listed || "it has none yet"}.`
        : "No agent step comes before it to report an outcome.";
  const setPaths = (paths: BranchStep["paths"]) => onChange({ ...step, paths });
  const inOtherwise = step.otherwise.length;
  return (
    <>
      <Field label="Goes by" htmlFor="step-on" hint={hint}>
        <NativeSelect
          id="step-on"
          className="w-full"
          value={step.on}
          onChange={(e) => {
            const on = ON_OPTIONS.find(([o]) => o === e.target.value)?.[0];
            if (on) onChange({ ...step, on });
          }}
        >
          {ON_OPTIONS.map(([on, label]) => (
            <NativeSelectOption key={on} value={on}>
              {label}
            </NativeSelectOption>
          ))}
        </NativeSelect>
      </Field>
      <Group legend="Paths" hint="Each path's steps are its cards under the branch.">
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
          {step.paths.map((path, i) => (
            <li key={i} className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Input
                aria-label={`Label of path ${i + 1}`}
                maxLength={step.on === "outcome" ? MAX_OUTCOME : undefined}
                value={path.label}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  const label = e.target.value;
                  setPaths(step.paths.map((p, j) => (j === i ? { ...p, label } : p)));
                }}
              />
              <RemovePath
                label={path.label.trim() || `path ${i + 1}`}
                inside={path.steps.length}
                onRemove={() => setPaths(step.paths.filter((_, j) => j !== i))}
              />
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
        <Button
          variant="outline"
          size="sm"
          className="w-fit"
          onClick={() =>
            setPaths([...step.paths, { label: newPathLabel(step, labels), steps: [] }])
          }
        >
          <Plus aria-hidden /> Add path
        </Button>
      </Group>
      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
        {inOtherwise === 0
          ? "Otherwise is empty: when no path's label matches, the workflow goes on after the " +
            "branch."
          : `Otherwise holds ${plural(inOtherwise, "step")}, run when no path's label matches.`}
      </p>
    </>
  );
}

/// A path's remove button. A path that holds steps asks first: they go with it.
function RemovePath(props: { label: string; inside: number; onRemove: () => void }) {
  const name = `Remove the path ${props.label}`;
  if (props.inside === 0) {
    return (
      <Button
        variant="ghost"
        size="icon-xs"
        aria-label={name}
        title="Remove"
        onClick={props.onRemove}
      >
        <X aria-hidden />
      </Button>
    );
  }
  return (
    <ConfirmButton
      variant="ghost"
      size="icon-xs"
      ariaLabel={name}
      title={`${name}?`}
      description={
        `${props.inside === 1 ? "The step on it goes" : `The ${props.inside} steps on it go`} ` +
        `with it. Nothing is saved until you press Save.`
      }
      confirmLabel="Remove path"
      onConfirm={props.onRemove}
    >
      <X aria-hidden />
    </ConfirmButton>
  );
}
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/builder/TriggerSheet.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useState } from "react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { commands } from "@/api/commands";
import type { Schedule, Team, Trigger, Workflow } from "@/api/types";
import { ConfirmButton } from "@/components/ConfirmButton";
import { Field } from "@/components/Field";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { NativeSelect, NativeSelectOption } from "@/components/ui/native-select";
import {
  Sheet,
  SheetContent,
  SheetDescription,
  SheetHeader,
  SheetTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/sheet";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { scheduleError, scheduleOf, withKind } from "@/lib/dot-rules";
import { errorMessage, notifyError } from "@/lib/notify";
import { invalidate, keys } from "@/lib/query";
import { useDebounced } from "@/lib/use-debounced";
import { ScheduleEditor } from "@/screens/dot-editor/ScheduleEditor";
import { CopyButton } from "@/screens/dot-editor/WebhookPanel";

import { TRIGGER_LABEL } from "../format";
import { MAX_TASK, toggled, withTriggerKind } from "./draft";
import { ChoiceList } from "./fields";
import type { NameOf } from "./labels";

const KINDS: Trigger["kind"][] = ["manual", "chat", "schedule", "webhook"];

/// What each kind of trigger does (section 5.1), under the kind's field.
const KIND_HINT: Record<Trigger["kind"], string> = {
  manual: "Run now on the team page, or Test run here: you type the task and pick the project.",
  chat: "A member starts it with start_workflow while you chat with it.",
  schedule:
    "It runs at each time of the schedule. A time that comes while a run is still in flight is skipped.",
  webhook: "Another program starts it with a POST request to its URL.",
};

/// The side sheet of the trigger's settings (section 10.2), open while `open`: its kind and the
/// kind's fields. It edits the draft in place. `workflow` is the saved workflow: its webhook
/// URL and token are the saved workflow's, and they take calls once the draft is saved.
export function TriggerSheet(props: {
  open: boolean;
  trigger: Trigger;
  enabled: boolean;
  workflow: Workflow;
  team: Team;
  nameOf: NameOf;
  onChange: (trigger: Trigger) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const { trigger, team, onChange } = props;
  return (
    <Sheet open={props.open} onOpenChange={(open) => !open && props.onClose()}>
      {props.open && (
        <SheetContent>
          <SheetHeader>
            <SheetTitle>Trigger settings</SheetTitle>
            <SheetDescription>
              Changes go into the draft at once. Save keeps them.
            </SheetDescription>
          </SheetHeader>
          <Field label="What starts it" htmlFor="trigger-kind" hint={KIND_HINT[trigger.kind]}>
            <NativeSelect
              id="trigger-kind"
              className="w-full"
              value={trigger.kind}
              onChange={(e) => {
                const kind = KINDS.find((k) => k === e.target.value);
                // A new schedule starts daily at 09:00, as the dot editor's does, with no
                // catch-up: a workflow's schedule never catches up.
                const daily = { ...(withKind(null, "daily", null) as Schedule), catch_up: false };
                if (kind) onChange(withTriggerKind(trigger, kind, team, daily));
              }}
            >
              {KINDS.map((kind) => (
                <NativeSelectOption key={kind} value={kind}>
                  {TRIGGER_LABEL[kind]}
                </NativeSelectOption>
              ))}
            </NativeSelect>
          </Field>
          {!props.enabled && (
            <p role="note" className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              The workflow is off: this trigger starts nothing until you turn it on and save. A
              Test run works while it is off.
            </p>
          )}
          {trigger.kind === "chat" && (
            <ChoiceList
              legend="Started by"
              hint="The members who may start it from a chat with you."
              options={team.members.map((m) => ({ value: m.dot_id, label: m.dot_name }))}
              chosen={trigger.starters}
              stale={props.nameOf}
              empty="The team has no member yet."
              onChange={(id, on) =>
                onChange({ ...trigger, starters: toggled(trigger.starters, id, on) })
              }
            />
          )}
          {trigger.kind === "schedule" && (
            <ScheduleFields
              trigger={trigger}
              stored={props.workflow.definition.trigger}
              team={team}
              onChange={onChange}
            />
          )}
          {trigger.kind === "webhook" && (
            <>
              <WebhookFields
                workflow={props.workflow}
                saved={props.workflow.definition.trigger.kind === "webhook"}
              />
              <Field
                label="Task when the call has none"
                htmlFor="trigger-task"
                hint="Used when the body has no task. Leave it empty to make every call say one."
              >
                <Textarea
                  id="trigger-task"
                  maxLength={MAX_TASK}
                  value={trigger.task ?? ""}
                  onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...trigger, task: e.target.value || null })}
                />
              </Field>
              <ProjectSelect
                label="Project when the call has none"
                team={team}
                value={trigger.project ?? ""}
                none="None: the call names it"
                onChange={(project) => onChange({ ...trigger, project: project || null })}
              />
            </>
          )}
        </SheetContent>
      )}
    </Sheet>
  );
}

/// A project of the team, picked and kept by name (a trigger names its project). `none`, when
/// given, is the choice of no project; else none picked yet asks for one. A name the team no
/// longer has stays shown until another is picked.
function ProjectSelect(props: {
  label: string;
  team: Team;
  value: string;
  none?: string;
  onChange: (name: string) => void;
}) {
  const { value } = props;
  const known = props.team.projects.some((p) => p.name === value);
  return (
    <Field label={props.label} htmlFor="trigger-project">
      <NativeSelect
        id="trigger-project"
        className="w-full"
        value={value}
        onChange={(e) => props.onChange(e.target.value)}
      >
        {props.none !== undefined ? (
          <NativeSelectOption value="">{props.none}</NativeSelectOption>
        ) : (
          value === "" && (
            <NativeSelectOption value="" disabled>
              Pick a project
            </NativeSelectOption>
          )
        )}
        {!known && value !== "" && (
          <NativeSelectOption value={value}>{value} (not a project of the team)</NativeSelectOption>
        )}
        {props.team.projects.map((p) => (
          <NativeSelectOption key={p.id} value={p.name}>
            {p.name}
          </NativeSelectOption>
        ))}
      </NativeSelect>
    </Field>
  );
}

type ScheduleTrigger = Extract<Trigger, { kind: "schedule" }>;

/// A schedule trigger: the dot editor's schedule fields (always a schedule, never a catch-up),
/// with the core's next runs, then the fixed task and project of each run.
function ScheduleFields(props: {
  trigger: ScheduleTrigger;
  stored: Trigger;
  team: Team;
  onChange: (trigger: Trigger) => void;
}) {
  const { trigger, onChange } = props;
  // As the dot editor does: the core previews the schedule once it settles; a field the core
  // could not even be sent (an emptied number) is held back with the UI's own message.
  const schedule = scheduleOf(useDebounced(trigger.schedule, 300));
  const typed = schedule ? scheduleError(schedule) : undefined;
  const preview = useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.schedulePreview(schedule),
    queryFn: () => (schedule ? commands.previewSchedule(schedule, 3) : []),
    enabled: typed === undefined,
  });
  const error =
    scheduleError(trigger.schedule) ??
    (typed === undefined && preview.isError ? errorMessage(preview.error) : undefined);
  return (
    <>
      <ScheduleEditor
        schedule={trigger.schedule}
        stored={props.stored.kind === "schedule" ? props.stored.schedule : null}
        onChange={(next) => next && onChange({ ...trigger, schedule: next })}
        error={error}
        preview={{
          times: typed === undefined && preview.data ? preview.data : [],
          pending: preview.isFetching,
        }}
        required
        catchUp={false}
      />
      <Field label="Task" htmlFor="trigger-task" hint="What each run of the schedule works on.">
        <Textarea
          id="trigger-task"
          maxLength={MAX_TASK}
          value={trigger.task}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...trigger, task: e.target.value })}
        />
      </Field>
      <ProjectSelect
        label="Project"
        team={props.team}
        value={trigger.project}
        onChange={(project) => onChange({ ...trigger, project })}
      />
    </>
  );
}

/// How another program starts the saved workflow: its URL and bearer token, as a dot's
/// `WebhookPanel` shows them. Dots keeps only the token's hash, so a token is shown once, when
/// it is made. `saved`: the saved workflow has a webhook trigger, so calls reach it.
function WebhookFields({ workflow, saved }: { workflow: Workflow; saved: boolean }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const [shown, setShown] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const url = useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.workflowWebhookUrl(workflow.id),
    queryFn: () => commands.workflowWebhookUrl(workflow.id),
  });
  const regenerate = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => commands.regenerateWorkflowToken(workflow.id),
    onSuccess: (token) => {
      setShown(token);
      qc.setQueryData<Workflow>(keys.workflow(workflow.id), (w) =>
        w ? { ...w, has_webhook_token: true } : w,
      );
      invalidate(qc, keys.workflows(workflow.team_id));
      toast.success(
        workflow.has_webhook_token ? "New token. The old one no longer works." : "Token made.",
      );
    },
    onError: notifyError,
  });
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
        Send a POST request to the URL with the header{" "}
        <code>Authorization: Bearer &lt;token&gt;</code>. The body's <code>task</code> and{" "}
        <code>project</code> (by name) are used; the fallbacks below fill in what it leaves out.
      </p>
      {!saved && (
        <p role="note" className="text-xs text-needs">
          Calls are refused until the workflow is saved with this trigger.
        </p>
      )}
      <Field
        label="Webhook URL"
        htmlFor="workflow-webhook-url"
        error={url.isError ? errorMessage(url.error) : undefined}
      >
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <Input
            id="workflow-webhook-url"
            readOnly
            className="font-mono"
            value={url.data ?? ""}
          />
          {url.data && <CopyButton what="webhook URL" text={url.data} />}
        </div>
      </Field>
      {shown !== null ? (
        <Field
          label="Token"
          htmlFor="workflow-webhook-token"
          hint="Copy it now: you won't see this again."
        >
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <Input id="workflow-webhook-token" readOnly className="font-mono" value={shown} />
            <CopyButton what="token" text={shown} />
          </div>
        </Field>
      ) : (
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          {workflow.has_webhook_token
            ? "This workflow has a token. It was shown once, when it was made."
            : "This workflow has no token yet, so its webhook refuses every call."}
        </p>
      )}
      <div>
        {workflow.has_webhook_token ? (
          <ConfirmButton
            title="Make a new token?"
            description="Anything that still sends the old token will be refused."
            confirmLabel="Make a new token"
            onConfirm={() => regenerate.mutate()}
            disabled={regenerate.isPending}
          >
            Regenerate
          </ConfirmButton>
        ) : (
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="outline"
            size="sm"
            onClick={() => regenerate.mutate()}
            disabled={regenerate.isPending}
          >
            Make a token
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
/// The side sheet of a step's settings (section 10.2), open while `step` is set. It edits the
/// draft in place: the step's name for now; the fields of each kind join it later.
export function StepSheet(props: {
  step: Step | undefined;
  onChange: (step: Step) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const { step } = props;
  return (
    <Sheet open={step !== undefined} onOpenChange={(open) => !open && props.onClose()}>
      {step && (
        <SheetContent>
          <SheetHeader>
            <SheetTitle>{KIND_LABEL[step.kind]} settings</SheetTitle>
            <SheetDescription>
              Changes go into the draft at once. Save keeps them.
            </SheetDescription>
          </SheetHeader>
          <Field label="Name" htmlFor="step-name" hint="Shown on the card and on the run page.">
            <Input
              id="step-name"
              value={step.name}
              onChange={(e) => props.onChange({ ...step, name: e.target.value })}
            />
          </Field>
        </SheetContent>
      )}
    </Sheet>
  );
}
```

```ts
import type {
  AgentStep,
  BranchStep,
  DeployMode,
  DeployStep,
  Delegate,
  Schedule,
  Step,
  Team,
  Trigger,
} from "@/api/types";

/// The core's limits on what a step holds (`workflow.rs`), in characters: a step's name, an
/// agent's or a deploying member's job, an outcome label, and a trigger's task.
export const MAX_NAME = 80;
export const MAX_JOB = 8_000;
export const MAX_OUTCOME = 40;
export const MAX_TASK = 8_000;

/// What a step becomes a delegate with (section 5.3): no member yet, 3 at once, 6 in all.
export const NEW_DELEGATE: Delegate = { may_start: [], at_once: 3, total: 6 };
```

```ts
/// The way down to the step `id`: for each level from the top, the list it is in and its place
/// there. The last entry is the step itself, the ones before it its enclosing branches.
function trail(steps: Step[], id: string): [Step[], number][] | undefined {
  for (const [i, step] of steps.entries()) {
    if (step.id === id) return [[steps, i]];
    for (const list of childLists(step)) {
      const inner = trail(list, id);
      if (inner) return [[steps, i], ...inner];
    }
  }
  return undefined;
}

/// The steps that run before the step `id` on its path, in run order, as the core's
/// `Definition::before` finds them: the earlier steps of each enclosing list and the enclosing
/// branches themselves. Empty when no step has the id.
export function stepsBefore(steps: Step[], id: string): Step[] {
  const way = trail(steps, id);
  if (!way) return [];
  const last = way.length - 1;
  return way.flatMap(([list, i], level) => list.slice(0, level < last ? i + 1 : i));
}

/// The agent steps before the step `id` on its path: what it may send work back to.
export function agentsBefore(steps: Step[], id: string): AgentStep[] {
  return stepsBefore(steps, id).filter((step): step is AgentStep => step.kind === "agent");
}

/// The agent step whose outcome a branch on outcome `id` goes by: the nearest one before it.
export function outcomeSource(steps: Step[], id: string): AgentStep | undefined {
  return agentsBefore(steps, id).at(-1);
}

/// `step` in deploy mode `mode`, keeping its id and name. A deploy holds only its mode's
/// fields, so a new mode starts from its defaults: squash (section 5.5's default), or the
/// team's first member with a plain job.
export function withMode(
  step: DeployStep,
  mode: DeployMode,
  team: Pick<Team, "members">,
): DeployStep {
  if (step.mode === mode) return step;
  const base = { id: step.id, name: step.name, kind: step.kind };
  switch (mode) {
    case "pr_merge":
      return { ...base, mode, merge_method: "squash" };
    case "member":
      return {
        ...base,
        mode,
        member: team.members[0]?.dot_id ?? "",
        job: "Deploy what was approved.",
      };
    default:
      return { ...base, mode };
  }
}

/// `trigger` as a trigger of `kind`. A task and project typed for a schedule carry over to a
/// webhook's fallbacks and back; a new chat trigger is started by the team's first member; a
/// new schedule is `schedule` (daily) on the team's first project.
export function withTriggerKind(
  trigger: Trigger,
  kind: Trigger["kind"],
  team: Pick<Team, "members" | "projects">,
  schedule: Schedule,
): Trigger {
  if (trigger.kind === kind) return trigger;
  const task =
    trigger.kind === "schedule" ? trigger.task : trigger.kind === "webhook" ? trigger.task : null;
  const project =
    trigger.kind === "schedule"
      ? trigger.project
      : trigger.kind === "webhook"
        ? trigger.project
        : null;
  switch (kind) {
    case "manual":
      return { kind };
    case "chat":
      return { kind, starters: team.members.slice(0, 1).map((m) => m.dot_id) };
    case "schedule":
      return {
        kind,
        schedule,
        task: task ?? "",
        project: project || (team.projects[0]?.name ?? ""),
      };
    case "webhook":
      return { kind, task: task?.trim() ? task : null, project: project || null };
  }
}

/// The label of a new path of `step`: the first of `suggestions` (the outcomes or projects it
/// can go by) that no path has, else "Path <n>" with the smallest free n. Labels are compared
/// without case, as the core compares them.
export function newPathLabel(step: BranchStep, suggestions: string[]): string {
  const taken = new Set(step.paths.map((p) => p.label.trim().toLowerCase()));
  const free = suggestions.find((s) => s.trim() !== "" && !taken.has(s.trim().toLowerCase()));
  if (free) return free.trim();
  let n = 1;
  while (taken.has(`path ${n}`)) n += 1;
  return `Path ${n}`;
}

/// `list` with `item` added at its end when `on` (once), or taken out when not.
export function toggled(list: string[], item: string, on: boolean): string[] {
  if (!on) return list.filter((x) => x !== item);
  return list.includes(item) ? list : [...list, item];
}
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/builder/fields.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";

import { commands } from "@/api/commands";
import type { TeamMember } from "@/api/types";
import { Field } from "@/components/Field";
import { NativeSelect, NativeSelectOption } from "@/components/ui/native-select";
import { keys } from "@/lib/query";

import type { NameOf } from "./labels";

/// A fieldset of the settings sheet, its legend styled like a field's label, with a hint under
/// its controls.
export function Group(props: { legend: string; hint?: ReactNode; children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <fieldset className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
      <legend className="mb-1.5 text-sm leading-none font-medium">{props.legend}</legend>
      {props.children}
      {props.hint && <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{props.hint}</p>}
    </fieldset>
  );
}

/// A member of the team, picked by name and kept by dot id. A dot that is not a member (it
/// left the team) stays shown by its id until another is picked; none picked yet asks for one.
export function MemberSelect(props: {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  hint?: ReactNode;
  members: TeamMember[];
  value: string;
  nameOf: NameOf;
  onChange: (dotId: string) => void;
}) {
  const known = props.members.some((m) => m.dot_id === props.value);
  return (
    <Field label={props.label} htmlFor={props.id} hint={props.hint}>
      <NativeSelect
        id={props.id}
        className="w-full"
        value={props.value}
        onChange={(e) => props.onChange(e.target.value)}
      >
        {props.value === "" && (
          <NativeSelectOption value="" disabled>
            Pick a member
          </NativeSelectOption>
        )}
        {!known && props.value !== "" && (
          <NativeSelectOption value={props.value}>
            {props.nameOf(props.value)} (not a member)
          </NativeSelectOption>
        )}
        {props.members.map((m) => (
          <NativeSelectOption key={m.dot_id} value={m.dot_id}>
            {m.dot_name}
          </NativeSelectOption>
        ))}
      </NativeSelect>
    </Field>
  );
}

/// One checkbox per option, named by its label; `chosen` holds the values ticked. A chosen
/// value no option has (a step that moved, a member who left) stays as a ticked box marked
/// `stale`, so it can be taken out. `empty` shows when there is nothing to choose.
export function ChoiceList(props: {
  legend: string;
  hint?: ReactNode;
  options: { value: string; label: string }[];
  chosen: string[];
  stale: (value: string) => string;
  empty: string;
  onChange: (value: string, on: boolean) => void;
}) {
  const gone = props.chosen.filter((v) => !props.options.some((o) => o.value === v));
  const box = (value: string, label: string, note?: string) => (
    <li key={value}>
      <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
        <input
          type="checkbox"
          aria-label={label}
          checked={props.chosen.includes(value)}
          onChange={(e) => props.onChange(value, e.target.checked)}
        />
        {label}
        {note && <span className="text-xs text-destructive">{note}</span>}
      </label>
    </li>
  );
  return (
    <Group legend={props.legend} hint={props.hint}>
      {props.options.length === 0 && gone.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{props.empty}</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          {props.options.map((o) => box(o.value, o.label))}
          {gone.map((v) => box(v, props.stale(v), "Not allowed here"))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </Group>
  );
}

/// A warning for each of `dotIds` that works in folder mode (teams spec section 8): in a
/// workflow it works in the project's own checkout, not in a worktree of its own. Nothing while
/// the dots are not known.
export function FolderWarning({ dotIds, nameOf }: { dotIds: string[]; nameOf: NameOf }) {
  const dots = useQuery({ queryKey: keys.dots, queryFn: commands.listDots });
  const folder = [...new Set(dotIds)].filter(
    (id) => dots.data?.find((d) => d.id === id)?.workspace_mode === "folder",
  );
  if (folder.length === 0) return null;
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
      {folder.map((id) => (
        <p
          key={id}
          role="note"
          className="rounded-md border border-needs-border bg-needs-surface p-2 text-xs text-needs"
        >
          {nameOf(id)} works in the folder itself: in a workflow it changes the project's own
          checkout, and leaves no branch to check or deploy.
        </p>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Task 12: The workflow run page shows its steps live, with the check, the limits, retries and the deploy's result

The route `/workflow-runs/:runId`: a header (team, trigger, Open the chat, the workflow linked to the builder, the task's first line, the project, the status and Test badges, Stop), the budget meters, the definition's steps with each step's newest step run (status, attempt, notice, error, outcome, decision, members, dot-run links, a branch's path taken), the waiting check (`CheckPanel`: summary, View diff per branch, note, Send back, Approve with the check's tips), the limit waits (`LimitPanel`: Add budget, Allow one more send-back), Retry step on a failed step, and a deploy's result. Dot-run links read the newest 100 dot runs (`keys.recentRuns`) until Task 15. UI only.

**Files:**
- Create: `ui/src/screens/teams/run/{WorkflowRunScreen,RunMeters,RunSteps,CheckPanel,LimitPanel,DeployResult,actions}.tsx`, `ui/src/screens/teams/run/steps.ts`
- Modify: `ui/src/app/routes.tsx`, `ui/src/lib/query.ts` (`keys.recentRuns`)
- Test: `ui/src/screens/teams/run/{steps.test.ts,WorkflowRunScreen.test.tsx,CheckPanel.test.tsx,LimitPanel.test.tsx}` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 6's `workflowRunGet`, `workflowCheck`, `workflowBranchDiff`, `workflowApprove`, `workflowSendBack`, `workflowAddBudget`, `workflowAllowSendBack`, `workflowRetryStep`, `workflowStop`, `openBranchFolder` and their keys; Tasks 8, 9 and 11's `WorkflowRunBadge`, `TestBadge`, `TRIGGER_LABEL`, `builderPath`, `DEFAULT_BUDGET`, `stepLine`, `delegateLine`, `CopyButton`; `DiffView`, `Markdown`, `StatusBadge`, `ConfirmButton`, `notifyError`.
- Produces: `CheckPanel({ run, stop? = true })` and `LimitPanel({ run, stop? = true })` (each a `<section aria-label>`: `Your check`, `Budget reached`, `Send-back limit reached`; `null` outside its wait); `actions.tsx`: `settle(qc, next: WorkflowRun)` (writes `keys.workflowRun`, marks `["workflow-runs"]`, `keys.workflows(team)` and `keys.inbox` stale), `inFlight(run) -> boolean`, `StopRun({ run })`, `RetryStep({ run })`, `ViewDiffButton({ runId, branch, commit })`; `DeployResult({ run, result })`; `RunMeters({ run })`; `RunSteps({ steps, prefix, name, view })`, type `RunView`; `steps.ts`: `latestStepRuns(run) -> Map<string, StepRun>`, `STEP_STATUS_LABEL`, `pathTaken(stepRun)`, `sendBackTarget(steps, checkId)`, `stepName(run, id)`, type `Limit`, `limits(run)`, `budgetReached(run)`, `joinAnd(items)`, type `DotRunLink`, `stepRunLinks(stepRun, runs: Run[], nameOf)`; `WorkflowRunScreen`, `RECENT_DOT_RUNS = 100`; `keys.recentRuns = ["runs", "recent"]`. Labels: `Running`, `Waiting`, `Waiting for you`, `Done`, `Failed`, `Stopped`, `Skipped`, `Not reached`, `· attempt <n>`, `Outcome: <label>`, `Taken`, `Not taken`, `Branches to check`, `Note`, `Approve`, `Send back to <step>`, `Write a note first: it says what to change.`, `It hit its budget`, `Add budget`, `It hit its send-back limit`, `Allow one more send-back`, `Deployed branches`, `Ready`, `Pushed`, `Not pushed`, `Pull request`, `merges once its checks pass`, `Diff of <branch>`, `Retry step`, `Stop this workflow run?`, `Stop run`.

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/run/CheckPanel.test.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { render, screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import type { WorkflowRun } from "@/api/types";
import { createQueryClient, keys } from "@/lib/query";
import { backend } from "@/test/backend";
import { sample } from "@/test/fixtures";

import { CheckPanel } from "./CheckPanel";

const [waiting, done] = sample.workflowRuns as [WorkflowRun, WorkflowRun];
const LEAD = "dots/nightly-tests/01J0RUN5";
const MEMBER = "dots/reviewer/01J0RUN6";
const TIP = "2222222222222222222222222222222222222222";
const CHANGED = `The branches changed since the check was shown: ${LEAD}. Look at the check again.`;

/// The check's actions on their own, as a conversation's workflow card shows them.
function show(run: WorkflowRun, stop?: boolean) {
  backend.on("workflow_check", () => sample.check);
  const queryClient = createQueryClient();
  const view = render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      <CheckPanel run={run} stop={stop} />
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
  return { ...view, queryClient, user: userEvent.setup() };
}

describe("the check's actions", () => {
  it("show the hand-off's summary, each branch it shows, and Stop unless told not to", async () => {
    show(waiting);
    const check = within(screen.getByRole("region", { name: "Your check" }));
    expect(await check.findByText("Made the icon dark on a light taskbar.")).toBeVisible();
    const branches = within(check.getByRole("list", { name: "Branches to check" }));
    expect(branches.getByRole("button", { name: `View diff of ${LEAD}` })).toBeEnabled();
    const gone = within(branches.getByRole("listitem", { name: MEMBER }));
    expect(gone.getByText("Gone")).toBeVisible();
    expect(gone.getByRole("button", { name: `View diff of ${MEMBER}` })).toBeDisabled();
    expect(gone.getByRole("button", { name: `View diff of ${MEMBER}` })).toHaveAttribute(
      "title",
      "The branch is gone.",
    );
    expect(check.getByRole("button", { name: "Stop" })).toBeVisible();
  });

  it("show nothing while the run does not wait on a check, and leave Stop out when asked", async () => {
    const { container } = show(done);
    expect(container).toBeEmptyDOMElement();
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_check")).toEqual([]);
    show(waiting, false);
    await screen.findByRole("listitem", { name: LEAD });
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "Stop" })).toBeNull();
  });

  it("send the work back to the check's target only with a note", async () => {
    backend.on("workflow_send_back", () => ({ ...waiting, status: "running", waiting_on: null }));
    const { user, queryClient } = show(waiting);
    const back = screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Send back to Build" });
    expect(back).toBeDisabled();
    expect(back).toHaveAttribute("title", "Write a note first: it says what to change.");
    await user.type(screen.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Note" }), "  Keep the old icon.  ");
    await user.click(back);
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_send_back")).toEqual([
      { id: "01J0WFR", note: "Keep the old icon." },
    ]);
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Note" })).toHaveValue(""));
    expect(queryClient.getQueryData(keys.workflowRun("01J0WFR"))).toMatchObject({
      status: "running",
    });
  });

  it("approve with the note and the branches and commits the check showed", async () => {
    backend.on("workflow_approve", () => ({ ...done, id: waiting.id }));
    const { user } = show(waiting);
    await screen.findByRole("listitem", { name: LEAD });
    await user.type(screen.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Note" }), "Looks right.");
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Approve" }));
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_approve")).toEqual([
      {
        id: "01J0WFR",
        note: "Looks right.",
        tips: [
          { branch: LEAD, commit: TIP },
          { branch: MEMBER, commit: null },
        ],
      },
    ]);
  });

  it("show the core's refusal when a branch moved, read the check again, and approve what it shows now", async () => {
    let answers = 0;
    backend.on("workflow_approve", () => {
      answers += 1;
      if (answers === 1) throw { kind: "conflict", message: CHANGED };
      return { ...done, id: waiting.id };
    });
    const { user } = show(waiting);
    await screen.findByRole("listitem", { name: LEAD });
    const moved = "4444444444444444444444444444444444444444";
    backend.on("workflow_check", () => ({
      ...sample.check,
      branches: [{ ...sample.check.branches[0]!, commit: moved }],
    }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Approve" }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent(CHANGED);
    await waitFor(() => expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_check")).toHaveLength(2));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByRole("listitem", { name: MEMBER })).toBeNull());
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Approve" }));
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_approve")[1]).toEqual({
      id: "01J0WFR",
      note: null,
      tips: [{ branch: LEAD, commit: moved }],
    });
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByRole("alert")).toBeNull());
  });

  it("show a branch's diff from the default branch in a dialog", async () => {
    backend.on("workflow_branch_diff", () => sample.diff);
    const { user } = show(waiting);
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: `View diff of ${LEAD}` }));
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: `Diff of ${LEAD}` });
    expect(dialog).toHaveTextContent(
      "What its commits change, from the project's default branch, at 2222222.",
    );
    expect(await within(dialog).findByText("src/lib.rs")).toBeVisible();
    expect(within(dialog).getByText("logo.png")).toBeVisible();
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_branch_diff")).toEqual([
      { id: "01J0WFR", branch: LEAD, commit: TIP },
    ]);
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { render, screen, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import type { WorkflowRun } from "@/api/types";
import { createQueryClient, keys } from "@/lib/query";
import { backend } from "@/test/backend";
import { sample } from "@/test/fixtures";

import { LimitPanel } from "./LimitPanel";

const [waiting, , held, , budget] = sample.workflowRuns as [
  WorkflowRun,
  WorkflowRun,
  WorkflowRun,
  WorkflowRun,
  WorkflowRun,
];

/// The limit's actions on their own, as a conversation's workflow card shows them.
function show(run: WorkflowRun, stop?: boolean) {
  const queryClient = createQueryClient();
  const view = render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      <LimitPanel run={run} stop={stop} />
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
  return { ...view, queryClient, user: userEvent.setup() };
}

describe("the limit's actions", () => {
  it("say every limit the run reached and what Add budget gives, then add it", async () => {
    const spent: WorkflowRun = { ...budget, spent_tokens: 1_600_000, active_ms: 121 * 60_000 };
    backend.on("workflow_add_budget", () => ({ ...spent, status: "running", waiting_on: null }));
    const { user, queryClient } = show(spent);
    const wait = within(screen.getByRole("region", { name: "Budget reached" }));
    expect(
      wait.getByText(
        "It reached its limits: runs 3 / 3, tokens 1.6M / 1.5M and minutes 121 / 120. Add budget gives it 10 runs, 1.5M tokens and 120 minutes more, once.",
      ),
    ).toBeVisible();
    expect(wait.getByRole("button", { name: "Stop" })).toBeVisible();
    await user.click(wait.getByRole("button", { name: "Add budget" }));
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_add_budget")).toEqual([{ id: "01J0WFR5" }]);
    expect(queryClient.getQueryData(keys.workflowRun("01J0WFR5"))).toMatchObject({
      status: "running",
    });
  });

  it("say what the person's own held send-back was, and allow one more", async () => {
    const mine: WorkflowRun = {
      ...held,
      send_back: { from: "review", to: "build", note: "Smaller.", by: "person", held: true },
    };
    backend.on("workflow_allow_send_back", () => ({
      ...mine,
      status: "running",
      waiting_on: null,
    }));
    const { user } = show(mine, false);
    const wait = within(screen.getByRole("region", { name: "Send-back limit reached" }));
    expect(wait.getByText("You sent it back from Review to Build: “Smaller.”")).toBeVisible();
    expect(wait.queryByRole("button", { name: "Stop" })).toBeNull();
    await user.click(wait.getByRole("button", { name: "Allow one more send-back" }));
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_allow_send_back")).toEqual([{ id: "01J0WFR3" }]);
  });

  it("show nothing for a run that waits on something else or is not waiting", () => {
    expect(show(waiting).container).toBeEmptyDOMElement();
    const going: WorkflowRun = { ...budget, status: "running", waiting_on: null };
    expect(show(going).container).toBeEmptyDOMElement();
  });

  it("stop the run after asking", async () => {
    backend.on("workflow_stop", () => ({ ...budget, status: "cancelled", waiting_on: null }));
    const { user } = show(budget);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Stop" }));
    await user.click(
      within(await screen.findByRole("alertdialog")).getByRole("button", { name: "Stop run" }),
    );
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_stop")).toEqual([{ id: "01J0WFR5" }]);
  });
});
```
