**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/run/WorkflowRunScreen.test.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { act, screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import type { Run, Team, Workflow, WorkflowRun } from "@/api/types";
import { backend } from "@/test/backend";
import { sample } from "@/test/fixtures";
import { renderApp } from "@/test/render";

const [crew] = sample.teams as [Team, Team];
const [fixAndShip] = sample.workflows as [Workflow, Workflow, Workflow, Workflow];
const [waiting, done, held, failed, budget] = sample.workflowRuns as [
  WorkflowRun,
  WorkflowRun,
  WorkflowRun,
  WorkflowRun,
  WorkflowRun,
];
const LEAD = "dots/nightly-tests/01J0RUN5";
const MEMBER = "dots/reviewer/01J0RUN6";

/// The page of `run`, of crew's Fix and ship; the newest dot runs are `runs`.
function runPage(run: WorkflowRun, runs: Run[] = []) {
  backend.on("workflow_run_get", () => run);
  backend.on("team_get", () => crew);
  backend.on("workflow_get", () => fixAndShip);
  backend.on("workflow_check", () => sample.check);
  backend.on("list_runs", () => runs);
  return renderApp(`/workflow-runs/${run.id}`);
}

const step = (name: string) => screen.getByRole("group", { name });
/// The run's status badge in the header.
const status = (text: string) => screen.findByText(text, { selector: "[data-status]" });

describe("the workflow run page", () => {
  it("shows the workflow, the task, the project, the status and the budget spent", async () => {
    runPage(waiting);
    const title = await screen.findByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: "Fix and ship" });
    expect(within(title).getByRole("link", { name: "Fix and ship" })).toHaveAttribute(
      "href",
      "/teams/01J0TEAM/workflows/01J0WFL",
    );
    expect(screen.getByText("The tray icon is invisible on a light taskbar.")).toBeVisible();
    expect(screen.getByText("On Dots")).toBeVisible();
    expect(screen.getByText("Needs your check")).toBeVisible();
    expect(await screen.findByRole("link", { name: "crew" })).toHaveAttribute(
      "href",
      "/teams/01J0TEAM",
    );
    expect(screen.getByText("· Started from a chat")).toBeVisible();
    expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "· Open the chat" })).toHaveAttribute(
      "href",
      "/runs/01J0RUN",
    );
    expect(screen.queryByText("Test")).toBeNull();

    const budget = within(screen.getByRole("list", { name: "Budget" }));
    expect(budget.getByRole("listitem", { name: "Runs: 3 of 10" })).toHaveTextContent("3 / 10");
    expect(
      budget.getByRole("listitem", {
        name: "Tokens: 120k of 1.5M, and 900k read from the cache",
      }),
    ).toHaveTextContent("Tokens· 900k cache reads120k / 1.5M");
    expect(budget.getByRole("listitem", { name: "Minutes: 5 of 120" })).toBeVisible();
    expect(budget.getByRole("listitem", { name: "Send-backs: 0 of 3" })).toBeVisible();
  });

  it("lays out the steps with their state, the path taken, the members and their dot runs", async () => {
    const lead: Run = {
      ...sample.run,
      id: "01J0RUN5",
      root_run_id: "01J0RUN5",
      status: "succeeded",
      workflow_step_run_id: "01J0STP1",
    };
    runPage(waiting, [lead]);
    const build = within(await screen.findByRole("group", { name: "Step 1: Build" }));
    expect(build.getByText("Done")).toBeVisible();
    expect(build.getByText("Delegate")).toBeVisible();
    expect(await build.findByText("may start reviewer · 2 at once · 4 total")).toBeVisible();
    expect(build.getByText("fixed")).toBeVisible();
    expect(build.getByRole("link", { name: "Run of nightly-tests" })).toHaveAttribute(
      "href",
      "/runs/01J0RUN5",
    );
    const members = within(build.getByRole("list", { name: "Members Build started" }));
    const windows = within(
      members.getByRole("listitem", { name: "Check the icon on Windows 10." }),
    );
    expect(windows.getByText("reviewer")).toBeVisible();
    expect(windows.getByText("Succeeded")).toBeVisible();
    expect(windows.getByText(MEMBER)).toBeVisible();
    expect(windows.getByRole("link", { name: "Open run" })).toHaveAttribute(
      "href",
      "/runs/01J0RUN6",
    );
    const contrast = within(
      members.getByRole("listitem", { name: "Check the high-contrast theme." }),
    );
    expect(contrast.getByText("Failed")).toBeVisible();
    expect(contrast.getByText("No branch")).toBeVisible();
    expect(contrast.queryByRole("link")).toBeNull();

    const route = within(step("Step 2: Fixed?"));
    expect(route.getByText("Done")).toBeVisible();
    expect(route.getByRole("region", { name: "Path fixed" })).toHaveTextContent("fixed · Taken");
    expect(route.getByRole("region", { name: "Otherwise" })).toHaveTextContent(
      "Otherwise · Not taken",
    );
    expect(within(step("Step 2.1: Review")).getByText("Waiting for you")).toBeVisible();
    expect(step("Step 2.1: Review")).toHaveAttribute("data-status", "waiting");
    expect(within(step("Step 2.2: Ship")).getByText("Not reached")).toBeVisible();
    expect(within(step("Step 2.1: Design")).getByText("Not reached")).toBeVisible();
    // The check is acted on where it waits; the page has one Stop, in its header.
    const check = within(step("Step 2.1: Review")).getByRole("region", { name: "Your check" });
    expect(await within(check).findByText("Made the icon dark on a light taskbar.")).toBeVisible();
    expect(within(check).queryByRole("button", { name: "Stop" })).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.getAllByRole("button", { name: "Stop" })).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(backend.callsTo("list_runs")).toContainEqual({ dotId: null, limit: 100 });
  });

  it("approves the check on its card, then shows the run going on", async () => {
    const after: WorkflowRun = { ...done, id: waiting.id };
    backend.on("workflow_approve", () => after);
    const { user } = runPage(waiting);
    const check = within(await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Your check" }));
    await check.findByRole("listitem", { name: LEAD });
    await user.click(check.getByRole("button", { name: "Approve" }));
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_approve")).toEqual([
      {
        id: "01J0WFR",
        note: null,
        tips: [
          { branch: LEAD, commit: "2222222222222222222222222222222222222222" },
          { branch: MEMBER, commit: null },
        ],
      },
    ]);
    expect(await status("Succeeded")).toBeVisible();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("region", { name: "Your check" })).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "Stop" })).toBeNull();
  });

  it("stops the run after asking", async () => {
    backend.on("workflow_stop", () => ({ ...waiting, status: "cancelled", waiting_on: null }));
    const { user } = runPage(waiting);
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Stop" }));
    const ask = await screen.findByRole("alertdialog", { name: "Stop this workflow run?" });
    expect(ask).toHaveTextContent("Its branches stay, and nothing already pushed is undone.");
    await user.click(within(ask).getByRole("button", { name: "Stop run" }));
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_stop")).toEqual([{ id: "01J0WFR" }]);
    expect(await status("Cancelled")).toBeVisible();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "Stop" })).toBeNull();
  });

  it("asks for more budget on a run whose workflow was deleted, and opens its ready branch", async () => {
    backend.on("workflow_add_budget", () => ({ ...budget, status: "running", waiting_on: null }));
    backend.on("open_project_folder", () => null);
    backend.on("workflow_branch_diff", () => ({ exists: true, truncated: false, files: [] }));
    const { user } = runPage(budget);
    expect(
      await screen.findByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: "A deleted workflow" }),
    ).toBeVisible();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("link", { name: "A deleted workflow" })).toBeNull();
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_get")).toEqual([]);
    expect(screen.getByText("On Site")).toBeVisible();
    expect(screen.getByRole("listitem", { name: "Runs: 3 of 3, reached" })).toHaveAttribute(
      "data-reached",
      "true",
    );

    const wait = within(screen.getByRole("region", { name: "Budget reached" }));
    expect(wait.getByText("It hit its budget")).toBeVisible();
    expect(
      wait.getByText("It reached its limit: runs 3 / 3. Add budget gives it 10 runs more, once."),
    ).toBeVisible();
    expect(wait.queryByRole("button", { name: "Stop" })).toBeNull();

    const site = within(screen.getByRole("region", { name: "Path Site" }));
    const ready = within(
      within(site.getByRole("group", { name: "Step 3.1: Ship" })).getByRole("listitem", {
        name: LEAD,
      }),
    );
    expect(ready.getByText("Ready")).toBeVisible();
    await user.click(ready.getByRole("button", { name: `Open folder of ${LEAD}` }));
    expect(backend.callsTo("open_project_folder")).toEqual([
      { projectId: null, workflowRunId: "01J0WFR5", branch: LEAD },
    ]);
    await user.click(ready.getByRole("button", { name: `View diff of ${LEAD}` }));
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: `Diff of ${LEAD}` });
    expect(await within(dialog).findByText("No changes.")).toBeVisible();
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_branch_diff")).toEqual([
      { id: "01J0WFR5", branch: LEAD, commit: "2222222222222222222222222222222222222222" },
    ]);
    await user.keyboard("{Escape}");

    await user.click(wait.getByRole("button", { name: "Add budget" }));
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_add_budget")).toEqual([{ id: "01J0WFR5" }]);
    await waitFor(() =>
      expect(screen.queryByRole("region", { name: "Budget reached" })).toBeNull(),
    );
    expect(await status("Running")).toBeVisible();
  });

  it("holds a send-back at its limit until one more is allowed", async () => {
    backend.on("workflow_allow_send_back", () => ({
      ...held,
      status: "running",
      waiting_on: null,
    }));
    const { user } = runPage(held);
    const wait = within(await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Send-back limit reached" }));
    expect(wait.getByText("The work was sent back 3 times, and its limit is 3.")).toBeVisible();
    expect(
      wait.getByText("Design asks to send it back to Build: “The icon needs a new shape first.”"),
    ).toBeVisible();
    expect(within(step("Step 2.1: Design")).getByText("Waiting")).toBeVisible();
    expect(
      within(step("Step 2.1: Design")).getByText(
        "Sends the work back to Build: “The icon needs a new shape first.”",
      ),
    ).toBeVisible();
    await user.click(wait.getByRole("button", { name: "Allow one more send-back" }));
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_allow_send_back")).toEqual([{ id: "01J0WFR3" }]);
    await waitFor(() =>
      expect(screen.queryByRole("region", { name: "Send-back limit reached" })).toBeNull(),
    );
  });

  it("shows a failed deploy's error once, what it pushed, and retries the step", async () => {
    backend.on("workflow_retry_step", () => ({ ...failed, status: "running", waiting_on: null }));
    const { user } = runPage(failed);
    const ship = within(await screen.findByRole("group", { name: "Step 2.2: Ship" }));
    expect(screen.getByText("Test")).toBeVisible();
    expect(ship.getByText("Failed")).toBeVisible();
    expect(ship.getAllByText("gh: could not create the pull request")).toHaveLength(1);
    // The run's own error names the step, which its card already says.
    expect(screen.queryByText("Ship: gh: could not create the pull request")).toBeNull();
    expect(within(ship.getByRole("listitem", { name: LEAD })).getByText("Pushed")).toBeVisible();
    expect(
      within(ship.getByRole("listitem", { name: MEMBER })).getByText("Not pushed"),
    ).toBeVisible();
    expect(ship.queryByRole("button", { name: `Open folder of ${LEAD}` })).toBeNull();
    await user.click(ship.getByRole("button", { name: "Retry step" }));
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_retry_step")).toEqual([{ id: "01J0WFR4" }]);
    await waitFor(() => expect(ship.queryByRole("button", { name: "Retry step" })).toBeNull());
  });

  it("shows a finished run's attempts, the check's decision and the pull request it opened", async () => {
    runPage(done);
    const build = within(await screen.findByRole("group", { name: "Step 1: Build" }));
    expect(build.getByText("· attempt 2")).toBeVisible();
    expect(build.getByText("Its earlier chat could not go on, so it started fresh.")).toBeVisible();
    expect(within(step("Step 2.1: Review")).getByText("Approved")).toBeVisible();
    const ship = within(within(step("Step 2.2: Ship")).getByRole("listitem", { name: LEAD }));
    expect(ship.getByRole("link", { name: "Pull request" })).toHaveAttribute(
      "href",
      "https://github.com/Aakshar-D/dots/pull/40",
    );
    expect(ship.getByRole("link", { name: "Pull request" })).toHaveAttribute("target", "_blank");
    expect(ship.getByText("merges once its checks pass")).toBeVisible();
    expect(ship.getByRole("button", { name: `Copy the branch ${LEAD}` })).toBeVisible();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "Stop" })).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("region", { name: "Your check" })).toBeNull();
  });

  it("follows the run's events: what the core says now is shown", async () => {
    const { listening } = runPage(waiting);
    await screen.findByText("Needs your check");
    await listening();
    const moved: WorkflowRun = { ...waiting, status: "running", waiting_on: null };
    backend.on("workflow_run_get", () => moved);
    act(() => backend.emit("runtime", { type: "workflow_run_updated", run: moved }));
    expect(await status("Running")).toBeVisible();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("region", { name: "Your check" })).toBeNull();
  });

  it("says why a run cannot be shown", async () => {
    backend.on("workflow_run_get", () => {
      throw { kind: "not_found", message: "workflow run 01J0NOPE" };
    });
    renderApp("/workflow-runs/01J0NOPE");
    expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("workflow run 01J0NOPE");
    expect(screen.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: "Workflow run" })).toBeVisible();
  });
});
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/run/steps.test.ts`:**

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import type { Run, StepRun, WorkflowRun } from "@/api/types";
import { sample } from "@/test/fixtures";

import {
  budgetReached,
  joinAnd,
  latestStepRuns,
  limits,
  pathTaken,
  sendBackTarget,
  stepName,
  stepRunLinks,
} from "./steps";

const [waiting, done, , , budget] = sample.workflowRuns as [
  WorkflowRun,
  WorkflowRun,
  WorkflowRun,
  WorkflowRun,
  WorkflowRun,
];
const names: Record<string, string> = { "01J0DOT": "nightly-tests", "01J0DOT2": "reviewer" };
const nameOf = (id: string) => names[id] ?? id;
const run = (id: string, over: Partial<Run> = {}): Run => ({
  ...sample.run,
  id,
  root_run_id: id,
  ...over,
});

describe("the run page's steps", () => {
  it("shows each step's newest step run, a later attempt over an earlier one", () => {
    const latest = latestStepRuns(done);
    expect(latest.get("build")?.id).toBe("01J0STP9");
    expect(latest.get("build")?.attempt).toBe(2);
    expect(latest.get("review")?.id).toBe("01J0STP4");
    expect(latest.get("ship")?.id).toBe("01J0STP5");
    expect(latest.has("design")).toBe(false);
  });

  it("reads the path a branch took, Otherwise as null, and nothing before it decided", () => {
    const route = latestStepRuns(waiting).get("route");
    expect(pathTaken(route)).toBe("fixed");
    expect(pathTaken({ ...route!, decision: { path: null } })).toBeNull();
    expect(pathTaken(latestStepRuns(waiting).get("review"))).toBeUndefined();
    expect(pathTaken(undefined)).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("sends a check back to its own target, else to the nearest agent step before it", () => {
    expect(sendBackTarget(waiting.definition.steps, "review")?.name).toBe("Build");
    expect(sendBackTarget(budget.definition.steps, "check")?.name).toBe("Work");
    expect(sendBackTarget(waiting.definition.steps, "nope")).toBeUndefined();
    expect(stepName(waiting, "design")).toBe("Design");
    expect(stepName(waiting, "gone")).toBe("gone");
  });

  it("says what each limit spent of its limit, which are reached, and what Add budget adds", () => {
    expect(limits(waiting).map((l) => [l.label, l.text, l.reached])).toEqual([
      ["Runs", "3 / 10", false],
      ["Tokens", "120k / 1.5M", false],
      ["Minutes", "5 / 120", false],
      ["Send-backs", "0 / 3", false],
    ]);
    expect(budgetReached(budget).map((l) => [l.text, l.more])).toEqual([["3 / 3", "10 runs"]]);
    const spent: WorkflowRun = {
      ...budget,
      spent_tokens: 1_500_000,
      active_ms: 120 * 60_000,
      send_backs: 3,
    };
    expect(budgetReached(spent).map((l) => l.more)).toEqual([
      "10 runs",
      "1.5M tokens",
      "120 minutes",
    ]);
    expect(joinAnd(["10 runs", "1.5M tokens", "120 minutes"])).toBe(
      "10 runs, 1.5M tokens and 120 minutes",
    );
    expect(joinAnd(["10 runs"])).toBe("10 runs");
  });

  it("links a step to its dot runs by thread, and each member to the run of its branch", () => {
    const build = latestStepRuns(waiting).get("build") as StepRun;
    const runs = [
      run("01J0RUN7", { dot_id: "01J0DOT", root_run_id: "01J0RUN5", status: "succeeded" }),
      run("01J0RUN5", { dot_id: "01J0DOT", workflow_step_run_id: build.id }),
      run("01J0RUN6", { dot_id: "01J0DOT2", workflow_step_run_id: build.id }),
      // Another step run's.
      run("01J0RUN8", { workflow_step_run_id: "01J0STP2" }),
    ];
    const links = stepRunLinks(build, runs, nameOf);
    // The lead's thread is its root, once; the member's run is linked from the member.
    expect(links.step).toEqual([{ id: "01J0RUN5", label: "nightly-tests", status: "succeeded" }]);
    expect([...links.members]).toEqual([["dots/reviewer/01J0RUN6", "01J0RUN6"]]);

    // Runs that fell out of the newest still link from the hand-off, named by their branch.
    const old = stepRunLinks(build, [], nameOf);
    expect(old.step).toEqual([
      { id: "01J0RUN5", label: "dots/nightly-tests/01J0RUN5", status: null },
    ]);
    expect(stepRunLinks(latestStepRuns(waiting).get("review")!, runs, nameOf).step).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/run/CheckPanel.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useState } from "react";

import { CommandError, commands } from "@/api/commands";
import type { WorkflowRun } from "@/api/types";
import { ErrorNote } from "@/components/ErrorNote";
import { Field } from "@/components/Field";
import { Markdown } from "@/components/Markdown";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { errorMessage } from "@/lib/notify";
import { invalidate, keys } from "@/lib/query";

import { settle, StopRun, ViewDiffButton } from "./actions";
import { sendBackTarget } from "./steps";

/// The check a workflow run waits on (section 5.4), with what the person does about it: its
/// hand-off summary, View diff for each branch it shows, a note, Send back (to the step it sends
/// back to; needs the note), Approve and, unless `stop` is false, Stop. Approve sends the
/// branches and commits it showed: when git moved since, the core refuses, the refusal is shown
/// and the check is read again. Shown only while the run waits on a check.
export function CheckPanel({ run, stop = true }: { run: WorkflowRun; stop?: boolean }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const waiting = run.status === "waiting" && run.waiting_on === "check";
  const check = useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.workflowCheck(run.id),
    queryFn: () => commands.workflowCheck(run.id),
    enabled: waiting,
  });
  const [note, setNote] = useState("");
  const [refusal, setRefusal] = useState<string>();
  const onError = (e: unknown) => {
    setRefusal(errorMessage(e));
    // The check moved under the page (a branch changed, the run went on): read it again.
    if (e instanceof CommandError && e.kind === "conflict") {
      invalidate(qc, keys.workflowCheck(run.id));
    }
  };
  const done = (next: WorkflowRun) => {
    setNote("");
    setRefusal(undefined);
    settle(qc, next);
  };
  const approve = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => {
      const tips = (check.data?.branches ?? []).map(({ branch, commit }) => ({ branch, commit }));
      return commands.workflowApprove(run.id, note.trim() || null, tips);
    },
    onSuccess: done,
    onError,
  });
  const sendBack = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => commands.workflowSendBack(run.id, note.trim()),
    onSuccess: done,
    onError,
  });
  if (!waiting) return null;

  const stepId = check.data?.step_id ?? run.current_step ?? "";
  const target = sendBackTarget(run.definition.steps, stepId);
  const busy = approve.isPending || sendBack.isPending;
  const noteId = `check-note-${run.id}`;
  return (
    <section
      aria-label="Your check"
      className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-lg border border-needs-border bg-needs-surface p-3"
    >
      {check.isError ? (
        <ErrorNote error={check.error} />
      ) : !check.data ? (
        <p className="text-muted-foreground">Reading the check…</p>
      ) : (
        <>
          {check.data.summary.trim() ? (
            <div className="text-sm">
              <Markdown text={check.data.summary} />
            </div>
          ) : (
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">The step before it said nothing.</p>
          )}
          {check.data.branches.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">It shows no branch: nothing changed.</p>
          ) : (
            <ul aria-label="Branches to check" className="flex flex-col gap-1">
              {check.data.branches.map((b) => (
                <li
                  key={b.branch}
                  aria-label={b.branch}
                  className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-sm"
                >
                  <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 font-mono text-xs break-all">{b.branch}</span>
                  {!b.commit && <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Gone</span>}
                  <ViewDiffButton runId={run.id} branch={b.branch} commit={b.commit} />
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
        </>
      )}
      <Field
        label="Note"
        htmlFor={noteId}
        hint="Send back needs one: it tells the step what to change. Approve keeps it with the approval."
      >
        <Textarea
          id={noteId}
          rows={3}
          value={note}
          placeholder="What should change?"
          onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)}
        />
      </Field>
      {refusal && (
        <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-destructive">
          {refusal}
        </p>
      )}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <Button size="sm" disabled={busy || !check.data} onClick={() => approve.mutate()}>
          Approve
        </Button>
        <Button
          size="sm"
          variant="outline"
          disabled={busy || note.trim() === ""}
          title={note.trim() === "" ? "Write a note first: it says what to change." : undefined}
          onClick={() => sendBack.mutate()}
        >
          {target ? `Send back to ${target.name.trim() || target.id}` : "Send back"}
        </Button>
        {stop && <StopRun run={run} />}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/run/DeployResult.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { FolderOpen } from "lucide-react";

import { commands } from "@/api/commands";
import type { DeployResult as Result, WorkflowRun } from "@/api/types";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { notifyError } from "@/lib/notify";
import { CopyButton } from "@/screens/dot-editor/WebhookPanel";

import { ViewDiffButton } from "./actions";

/// What a deploy did (section 5.5): each branch pushed, with its pull request; or the branches
/// kept, ready, with Open folder and View diff; then the error of a deploy that failed part way.
/// A pull request opens in the browser, as the app's other links to GitHub do; only an https
/// address is a link.
export function DeployResult({ run, result }: { run: WorkflowRun; result: Result }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm">
      {result.branches.length === 0 ? (
        !result.error && <p className="text-muted-foreground">No branch was deployed.</p>
      ) : (
        <ul aria-label="Deployed branches" className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          {result.branches.map((b) => (
            <li
              key={b.branch}
              aria-label={b.branch}
              className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 rounded-md border bg-background p-2"
            >
              <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 font-mono text-xs break-all">{b.branch}</span>
              <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                {result.ready ? "Ready" : b.pushed ? "Pushed" : "Not pushed"}
              </span>
              {b.pr_url?.startsWith("https://") && (
                <a href={b.pr_url} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" className="underline">
                  Pull request
                </a>
              )}
              {b.pr_url && b.auto_merge && (
                <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">merges once its checks pass</span>
              )}
              <CopyButton what={`branch ${b.branch}`} text={b.branch} />
              {result.ready && (
                <>
                  <Button
                    variant="outline"
                    size="sm"
                    aria-label={`Open folder of ${b.branch}`}
                    onClick={() => commands.openBranchFolder(run.id, b.branch).catch(notifyError)}
                  >
                    <FolderOpen aria-hidden /> Open folder
                  </Button>
                  <ViewDiffButton runId={run.id} branch={b.branch} commit={b.commit} />
                </>
              )}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
      {result.error && <p className="text-destructive">{result.error}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/run/LimitPanel.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";

import { commands } from "@/api/commands";
import type { WorkflowRun } from "@/api/types";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { notifyError } from "@/lib/notify";

import { settle, StopRun } from "./actions";
import { budgetReached, joinAnd, stepName } from "./steps";

/// What a workflow run held at a limit waits for (section 9), with what the person does about
/// it: at the budget, what it spent of each limit it reached and **Add budget**, which raises
/// each of them once by its default amount (the core's choice: it takes no amounts); at the
/// send-back cap, the send-back it holds and **Allow one more send-back**. Then, unless `stop` is
/// false, Stop. Shown only while the run waits on one of the two.
export function LimitPanel({ run, stop = true }: { run: WorkflowRun; stop?: boolean }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const add = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () =>
      run.waiting_on === "budget"
        ? commands.workflowAddBudget(run.id)
        : commands.workflowAllowSendBack(run.id),
    onSuccess: (next) => settle(qc, next),
    onError: notifyError,
  });
  if (run.status !== "waiting") return null;
  if (run.waiting_on !== "budget" && run.waiting_on !== "send_backs") return null;

  const atBudget = run.waiting_on === "budget";
  const reached = budgetReached(run);
  const held = run.send_back?.held ? run.send_back : null;
  return (
    <section
      aria-label={atBudget ? "Budget reached" : "Send-back limit reached"}
      className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-lg border border-needs-border bg-needs-surface p-3 text-sm"
    >
      <p className="font-semibold text-needs">
        {atBudget ? "It hit its budget" : "It hit its send-back limit"}
      </p>
      {atBudget ? (
        reached.length > 0 ? (
          <p>
            It reached its {reached.length === 1 ? "limit" : "limits"}:{" "}
            {joinAnd(reached.map((l) => `${l.label.toLowerCase()} ${l.text}`))}. Add budget gives
            it {joinAnd(reached.map((l) => l.more))} more, once.
          </p>
        ) : (
          <p>It spent what its budget allows. Add budget raises the limits it reached.</p>
        )
      ) : (
        <>
          <p>
            The work was sent back {run.send_backs}{" "}
            {run.send_backs === 1 ? "time" : "times"}, and its limit is {run.budget.max_send_backs}.
          </p>
          {held && (
            <p>
              {held.by === "person"
                ? `You sent it back from ${stepName(run, held.from)} to ${stepName(run, held.to)}`
                : `${stepName(run, held.from)} asks to send it back to ${stepName(run, held.to)}`}
              : “{held.note}”
            </p>
          )}
        </>
      )}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <Button size="sm" disabled={add.isPending} onClick={() => add.mutate()}>
          {atBudget ? "Add budget" : "Allow one more send-back"}
        </Button>
        {stop && <StopRun run={run} />}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/run/RunMeters.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { cn } from "cn";

import type { WorkflowRun } from "@/api/types";
import { formatTokens } from "@/lib/usage-format";

import { limits } from "./steps";

/// The run's budget across the top (section 10.3): runs, tokens, minutes and send-backs, each
/// "spent / limit" over a bar, amber once reached. Cache reads are shown beside the tokens: they
/// are not counted against the limit.
export function RunMeters({ run }: { run: WorkflowRun }) {
  return (
    <ul aria-label="Budget" className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-4">
      {limits(run).map((l) => {
        const cache = l.key === "max_tokens" && run.spent_cache_read > 0;
        const name =
          `${l.label}: ${l.text.replace(" / ", " of ")}` +
          (cache ? `, and ${formatTokens(run.spent_cache_read)} read from the cache` : "") +
          (l.reached ? ", reached" : "");
        const share = l.limit > 0 ? Math.min(100, Math.round((l.spent / l.limit) * 100)) : 100;
        return (
          <li
            key={l.key}
            aria-label={name}
            data-reached={l.reached || undefined}
            className="flex flex-col gap-1 rounded-lg border bg-card p-2"
          >
            <span className="flex items-baseline gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              {l.label}
              {cache && <span>· {formatTokens(run.spent_cache_read)} cache reads</span>}
            </span>
            <span className={cn("text-sm font-semibold", l.reached && "text-needs")}>
              {l.text}
            </span>
            <span aria-hidden className="h-1.5 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-muted">
              <span
                className={cn("block h-full rounded-full", l.reached ? "bg-needs" : "bg-primary")}
                style={{ width: `${share}%` }}
              />
            </span>
          </li>
        );
      })}
    </ul>
  );
}
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/run/RunSteps.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { cn } from "cn";
import { ArrowDown } from "lucide-react";
import { Link } from "react-router";

import type { Run, Step, StepRun, WorkflowRun } from "@/api/types";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/StatusBadge";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";

import { delegateLine, KIND_LABEL, type NameOf, stepLine } from "../builder/labels";
import { taskLine } from "../format";
import { RetryStep } from "./actions";
import { CheckPanel } from "./CheckPanel";
import { DeployResult } from "./DeployResult";
import { pathTaken, STEP_STATUS_LABEL, stepName, stepRunLinks } from "./steps";

/// What every card of the run page reads: the run, the newest step run of each step, the team's
/// names and the newest dot runs (to link each step to its dot runs).
export type RunView = {
  run: WorkflowRun;
  latest: Map<string, StepRun>;
  nameOf: NameOf;
  dotRuns: Run[];
};

const MARK: Record<keyof typeof STEP_STATUS_LABEL, string> = {
  running: "animate-pulse bg-primary motion-reduce:animate-none",
  waiting: "bg-needs",
  succeeded: "bg-success",
  failed: "bg-destructive",
  cancelled: "bg-muted-foreground",
  skipped: "border border-muted-foreground bg-transparent",
  not_reached: "border border-dashed border-muted-foreground bg-transparent",
};

/// A step's state as a coloured dot, running with the pulse of a running dot.
function StepMark({ status }: { status: keyof typeof STEP_STATUS_LABEL }) {
  return (
    <span
      aria-hidden
      data-status={status}
      className={cn("inline-block size-2.5 shrink-0 rounded-full", MARK[status])}
    />
  );
}

/// The run's steps as the builder lays them out (section 10.3), numbered, joined by arrows, a
/// branch's paths as labelled columns; each card shows its newest step run.
export function RunSteps(props: { steps: Step[]; prefix: string; name: string; view: RunView }) {
  return (
    <ol aria-label={props.name} className="flex flex-col">
      {props.steps.map((step, i) => (
        <li key={step.id} className="flex flex-col">
          {i > 0 && (
            <div className="flex justify-center py-1 text-muted-foreground">
              <ArrowDown aria-hidden className="size-4" />
            </div>
          )}
          <StepRunCard step={step} number={`${props.prefix}${i + 1}`} view={props.view} />
        </li>
      ))}
    </ol>
  );
}

/// How a card looks: a waiting check in the needs colours, a delegate dashed in the accent, a
/// running step in the accent, a failed one in red.
function look(step: Step, stepRun: StepRun | undefined): string {
  if (step.kind === "check" && stepRun?.status === "waiting") {
    return "border-needs-border bg-needs-surface";
  }
  if (stepRun?.status === "failed") return "border-destructive";
  if (step.kind === "agent" && step.delegate) return "border-dashed border-primary";
  if (stepRun?.status === "running") return "border-primary";
  return "";
}

/// One step and its newest step run: its state and attempt, what the run page says about the
/// attempt, its error, outcome or decision, a delegate's members, a deploy's result, links to
/// its dot runs, and what the person can do here: the waiting check, or Retry step.
function StepRunCard({ step, number, view }: { step: Step; number: string; view: RunView }) {
  const { run, nameOf } = view;
  const stepRun = view.latest.get(step.id);
  const status = stepRun?.status ?? "not_reached";
  const name = step.name.trim() || "(no name)";
  const delegate = step.kind === "agent" ? delegateLine(step, nameOf) : undefined;
  const current = run.status === "waiting" && run.current_step === step.id;
  const links = stepRun ? stepRunLinks(stepRun, view.dotRuns, nameOf) : null;
  const decision = stepRun?.decision;
  const deployError = stepRun?.deploy_result?.error;
  const label =
    step.kind === "check" && status === "waiting" ? "Waiting for you" : STEP_STATUS_LABEL[status];
  return (
    <div
      role="group"
      aria-label={`Step ${number}: ${name}`}
      data-step-id={step.id}
      data-status={status}
      className={cn(
        "flex w-full flex-col gap-1.5 rounded-xl border bg-card p-3",
        look(step, stepRun),
      )}
    >
      <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
        <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-0.5">
          <span className={cn("font-semibold", step.kind === "check" && "text-needs")}>
            {number} · {name}
          </span>
          <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            {stepLine(step, run.definition.steps, nameOf)}
          </span>
          {delegate && <span className="text-xs text-primary">{delegate}</span>}
        </div>
        <Badge variant="outline">{delegate ? "Delegate" : KIND_LABEL[step.kind]}</Badge>
        <span className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 text-xs">
          <StepMark status={status} />
          {label}
          {stepRun && stepRun.attempt > 1 && (
            <span className="text-muted-foreground">· attempt {stepRun.attempt}</span>
          )}
        </span>
      </div>
      {stepRun?.notice && <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{stepRun.notice}</p>}
      {stepRun?.error && stepRun.error !== deployError && (
        <p className="text-sm text-destructive">{stepRun.error}</p>
      )}
      {stepRun?.outcome && (
        <p className="text-xs">
          Outcome: <span className="font-medium">{stepRun.outcome}</span>
        </p>
      )}
      {decision && "decision" in decision && (
        <p className="text-xs">
          {decision.decision === "approved" ? "Approved" : "Sent back"}
          {decision.note ? `: ${decision.note}` : ""}
        </p>
      )}
      {stepRun?.send_back && (
        <p className="text-xs">
          Sends the work back to {stepName(run, stepRun.send_back.to)}: “{stepRun.send_back.note}”
        </p>
      )}
      {stepRun?.handoff && stepRun.handoff.members.length > 0 && (
        <ul aria-label={`Members ${name} started`} className="mt-1 flex flex-col gap-1 pl-4">
          {stepRun.handoff.members.map((member, i) => {
            const runId = member.branch ? links?.members.get(member.branch) : undefined;
            return (
              <li
                key={i}
                aria-label={taskLine(member.task)}
                className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 rounded-md border bg-background p-2 text-xs"
              >
                <span className="font-medium">{nameOf(member.dot_id)}</span>
                <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">{taskLine(member.task)}</span>
                <StatusBadge status={member.status} />
                <span className="font-mono break-all text-muted-foreground">
                  {member.branch ?? "No branch"}
                </span>
                {runId && (
                  <Link to={`/runs/${runId}`} className="underline">
                    Open run
                  </Link>
                )}
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ul>
      )}
      {stepRun?.deploy_result && <DeployResult run={run} result={stepRun.deploy_result} />}
      {links && links.step.length > 0 && (
        <ul aria-label={`Dot runs of ${name}`} className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-xs">
          {links.step.map((link) => (
            <li key={link.id} className="flex items-center gap-1">
              <Link to={`/runs/${link.id}`} className="underline">
                Run of {link.label}
              </Link>
              {link.status && <StatusBadge status={link.status} />}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
      {step.kind === "check" && current && <CheckPanel run={run} stop={false} />}
      {current && run.waiting_on === "step_failed" && stepRun?.status === "failed" && (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <RetryStep run={run} />
          <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            A new attempt of the step, counted against the budget.
          </span>
        </div>
      )}
      {step.kind === "branch" && (
        <div className="mt-2 flex gap-3 overflow-x-auto pl-4">
          {[
            ...step.paths.map((path) => ({ label: path.label, steps: path.steps })),
            { label: null, steps: step.otherwise },
          ].map((path) => {
            const taken = pathTaken(stepRun);
            const title = path.label === null ? "Otherwise" : path.label.trim() || "(no label)";
            const region = path.label === null ? "Otherwise" : `Path ${path.label.trim()}`;
            const state = taken === undefined ? null : taken === path.label ? "Taken" : "Not taken";
            return (
              <section
                key={region}
                aria-label={region}
                data-taken={state === null ? undefined : state === "Taken"}
                className={cn(
                  "flex min-w-56 flex-1 flex-col gap-1 rounded-lg border border-dashed p-2",
                  state === "Taken" && "border-primary",
                )}
              >
                <h3 className="text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
                  {title}
                  {state && ` · ${state}`}
                </h3>
                {path.steps.length === 0 ? (
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">No steps: it goes on after.</p>
                ) : (
                  <RunSteps
                    steps={path.steps}
                    prefix={`${number}.`}
                    name={
                      path.label === null
                        ? "Steps of Otherwise"
                        : `Steps of the path ${path.label.trim()}`
                    }
                    view={view}
                  />
                )}
              </section>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/run/WorkflowRunScreen.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Link, useParams } from "react-router";

import { commands } from "@/api/commands";
import type { WorkflowRun } from "@/api/types";
import { ErrorNote } from "@/components/ErrorNote";
import { Page } from "@/components/Page";
import { keys } from "@/lib/query";

import { builderPath } from "../TeamWorkflows";
import { taskLine, TRIGGER_LABEL } from "../format";
import { TestBadge, WorkflowRunBadge } from "../WorkflowRunBadge";
import { inFlight, StopRun } from "./actions";
import { LimitPanel } from "./LimitPanel";
import { RunMeters } from "./RunMeters";
import { RunSteps, type RunView } from "./RunSteps";
import { latestStepRuns } from "./steps";

/// How many of the newest dot runs the page reads to link its steps to their dot runs. A run in
/// flight is among them; an older one's steps still link to the runs their hand-offs name.
export const RECENT_DOT_RUNS = 100;

/// A workflow run's page (teams spec section 10.3, option A): live from the run's events.
export function WorkflowRunScreen() {
  const { runId = "" } = useParams();
  const run = useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.workflowRun(runId),
    queryFn: () => commands.workflowRunGet(runId),
  });
  if (run.isPending) return <Page title="Workflow run" />;
  if (run.isError) {
    return (
      <Page title="Workflow run">
        <ErrorNote error={run.error} />
      </Page>
    );
  }
  return <RunPage run={run.data} />;
}

/// The header (the workflow, the task's first line, the team and project, Test, the status,
/// Stop), the budget's meters, a wait on a limit, then the definition's steps with their step
/// runs.
function RunPage({ run }: { run: WorkflowRun }) {
  const team = useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.team(run.team_id),
    queryFn: () => commands.teamGet(run.team_id),
  });
  const workflowId = run.workflow_id;
  const workflow = useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.workflow(workflowId ?? ""),
    queryFn: () => commands.workflowGet(workflowId!),
    enabled: workflowId !== null,
  });
  const dotRuns = useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.recentRuns,
    queryFn: () => commands.listRuns(null, RECENT_DOT_RUNS),
  });
  const names = new Map((team.data?.members ?? []).map((m) => [m.dot_id, m.dot_name]));
  const view: RunView = {
    run,
    latest: latestStepRuns(run),
    nameOf: (id) => names.get(id) ?? id,
    dotRuns: dotRuns.data ?? [],
  };
  // The workflow is gone once its id is null, or when it was deleted under the page.
  const gone = workflowId === null || workflow.isError;
  const title = gone ? "A deleted workflow" : (workflow.data?.name ?? "Workflow run");
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-6">
      <header className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          <Link to={`/teams/${run.team_id}`} className="hover:underline">
            {team.data?.name ?? "Team"}
          </Link>
          <span>· {TRIGGER_LABEL[run.trigger]}</span>
          {run.origin_run_id && (
            <Link to={`/runs/${run.origin_run_id}`} className="hover:underline">
              · Open the chat
            </Link>
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <h1 className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-lg font-semibold">
            {workflow.data && !gone ? (
              <Link to={builderPath(run.team_id, workflow.data.id)} className="hover:underline">
                {title}
              </Link>
            ) : (
              title
            )}
          </h1>
          {inFlight(run) && <StopRun run={run} />}
        </div>
        <p className="text-sm">{taskLine(run.task) || "No task."}</p>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          <span>On {run.project.name}</span>
          <WorkflowRunBadge status={run.status} waitingOn={run.waiting_on} />
          {run.test && <TestBadge />}
        </div>
      </header>
      <RunMeters run={run} />
      {run.error && run.waiting_on !== "step_failed" && (
        <p role="alert" className="rounded-md border border-destructive/50 p-3 text-destructive">
          {run.error}
        </p>
      )}
      <LimitPanel run={run} stop={false} />
      <RunSteps steps={run.definition.steps} prefix="" name="Steps" view={view} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/run/actions.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient, type QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useState } from "react";

import { commands } from "@/api/commands";
import type { WorkflowRun } from "@/api/types";
import { ConfirmButton } from "@/components/ConfirmButton";
import { DiffView } from "@/components/DiffView";
import { ErrorNote } from "@/components/ErrorNote";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { notifyError } from "@/lib/notify";
import { invalidate, keys } from "@/lib/query";

/// After the core answered an action on a workflow run with the run as it is now: the run page
/// shows it at once, and the lists of runs (the team page's, a conversation's cards) and what
/// needs the person are read again. The run's own event follows and changes nothing more.
export function settle(qc: QueryClient, next: WorkflowRun): void {
  qc.setQueryData(keys.workflowRun(next.id), next);
  for (const queryKey of [["workflow-runs"], keys.workflows(next.team_id), keys.inbox]) {
    invalidate(qc, queryKey);
  }
}

/// Whether a workflow run can still be acted on: it is running or waiting.
export function inFlight(run: Pick<WorkflowRun, "status">): boolean {
  return run.status === "running" || run.status === "waiting";
}

/// "Stop" (section 5.4): asks first, then cancels the step at work and every step after it.
export function StopRun({ run }: { run: WorkflowRun }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const stop = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => commands.workflowStop(run.id),
    onSuccess: (next) => settle(qc, next),
    onError: notifyError,
  });
  return (
    <ConfirmButton
      title="Stop this workflow run?"
      description="The step at work stops and no step after it runs. Its branches stay, and nothing already pushed is undone."
      confirmLabel="Stop run"
      disabled={stop.isPending || !inFlight(run)}
      onConfirm={() => stop.mutate()}
    >
      Stop
    </ConfirmButton>
  );
}

/// "Retry step" on a failed step (section 10.3): a new attempt, counted against the budget.
export function RetryStep({ run }: { run: WorkflowRun }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const retry = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => commands.workflowRetryStep(run.id),
    onSuccess: (next) => settle(qc, next),
    onError: notifyError,
  });
  return (
    <Button size="sm" disabled={retry.isPending} onClick={() => retry.mutate()}>
      Retry step
    </Button>
  );
}

/// "View diff" of a branch a check shows or a deploy names, at `commit`, in a dialog: what its
/// commits change from the project's default branch. A branch that is gone (no commit) has none.
export function ViewDiffButton(props: { runId: string; branch: string; commit: string | null }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  return (
    <>
      <Button
        variant="outline"
        size="sm"
        aria-label={`View diff of ${props.branch}`}
        title={props.commit ? undefined : "The branch is gone."}
        disabled={!props.commit}
        onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
      >
        View diff
      </Button>
      {open && props.commit && (
        <BranchDiffDialog
          runId={props.runId}
          branch={props.branch}
          commit={props.commit}
          onClose={() => setOpen(false)}
        />
      )}
    </>
  );
}

function BranchDiffDialog(props: {
  runId: string;
  branch: string;
  commit: string;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const diff = useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.workflowBranchDiff(props.runId, props.branch, props.commit),
    queryFn: () => commands.workflowBranchDiff(props.runId, props.branch, props.commit),
  });
  return (
    <Dialog open onOpenChange={(next) => !next && props.onClose()}>
      <DialogContent className="max-h-[85vh] overflow-y-auto sm:max-w-3xl">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle className="break-all">Diff of {props.branch}</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>
            What its commits change, from the project's default branch, at{" "}
            <span className="font-mono">{props.commit.slice(0, 7)}</span>.
          </DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>
        {diff.isError ? (
          <ErrorNote error={diff.error} />
        ) : diff.data ? (
          <DiffView diff={diff.data} />
        ) : (
          <p className="text-muted-foreground">Reading the diff…</p>
        )}
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/run/steps.ts`:**

```ts
import type {
  AgentStep,
  Budget,
  Run,
  RunStatus,
  Step,
  StepRun,
  StepRunStatus,
  WorkflowRun,
} from "@/api/types";
import { formatTokens } from "@/lib/usage-format";

import { agentsBefore, findStep } from "../builder/draft";
import { DEFAULT_BUDGET } from "../format";

/// The newest step run of each step, by step id: what the run page shows on the step's card. A
/// run's step runs come oldest first, so a later attempt replaces an earlier one.
export function latestStepRuns(run: Pick<WorkflowRun, "steps">): Map<string, StepRun> {
  const latest = new Map<string, StepRun>();
  for (const stepRun of run.steps) latest.set(stepRun.step_id, stepRun);
  return latest;
}

/// A step's state as its card says it (section 10.3); a step with no step run is not reached.
export const STEP_STATUS_LABEL: Record<StepRunStatus | "not_reached", string> = {
  running: "Running",
  waiting: "Waiting",
  succeeded: "Done",
  failed: "Failed",
  cancelled: "Stopped",
  skipped: "Skipped",
  not_reached: "Not reached",
};

/// The path a branch step took, from its step run's decision: the label, null for Otherwise,
/// undefined while it has not decided.
export function pathTaken(stepRun: StepRun | undefined): string | null | undefined {
  const decision = stepRun?.decision;
  if (!decision || !("path" in decision)) return undefined;
  return decision.path;
}

/// The agent step a check sends the work back to: its own `send_back_to`, else the nearest agent
/// step before it on its path, as the core picks it.
export function sendBackTarget(steps: Step[], checkId: string): AgentStep | undefined {
  const check = findStep(steps, checkId);
  if (check?.kind === "check" && check.send_back_to) {
    const to = findStep(steps, check.send_back_to);
    return to?.kind === "agent" ? to : undefined;
  }
  return agentsBefore(steps, checkId).at(-1);
}

/// The name of the step `id` in the run's definition, or the id when it has none.
export function stepName(run: Pick<WorkflowRun, "definition">, id: string): string {
  return findStep(run.definition.steps, id)?.name.trim() || id;
}

/// One of the four limits of a workflow run (section 5.7): what it spent and its limit, and
/// what Add budget adds to it once it is reached (the core's `raised`: the default amount).
export type Limit = {
  key: keyof Budget;
  label: string;
  spent: number;
  limit: number;
  reached: boolean;
  /// "3 / 10", "120k / 1.5M".
  text: string;
  /// "10 runs", "1.5M tokens": what Add budget adds to it.
  more: string;
};

/// The run's four limits in the order the meters show them. Minutes are the run's active
/// minutes, whole ones; send-backs have no Add budget (Allow one more send-back raises them).
export function limits(run: WorkflowRun): Limit[] {
  const minutes = Math.floor(run.active_ms / 60_000);
  const b = run.budget;
  return [
    {
      key: "max_runs",
      label: "Runs",
      spent: run.spent_runs,
      limit: b.max_runs,
      reached: run.spent_runs >= b.max_runs,
      text: `${run.spent_runs} / ${b.max_runs}`,
      more: `${DEFAULT_BUDGET.max_runs} runs`,
    },
    {
      key: "max_tokens",
      label: "Tokens",
      spent: run.spent_tokens,
      limit: b.max_tokens,
      reached: run.spent_tokens >= b.max_tokens,
      text: `${formatTokens(run.spent_tokens)} / ${formatTokens(b.max_tokens)}`,
      more: `${formatTokens(DEFAULT_BUDGET.max_tokens)} tokens`,
    },
    {
      key: "max_minutes",
      label: "Minutes",
      spent: minutes,
      limit: b.max_minutes,
      // As the core counts it: the active time in milliseconds against the limit's.
      reached: run.active_ms >= b.max_minutes * 60_000,
      text: `${minutes} / ${b.max_minutes}`,
      more: `${DEFAULT_BUDGET.max_minutes} minutes`,
    },
    {
      key: "max_send_backs",
      label: "Send-backs",
      spent: run.send_backs,
      limit: b.max_send_backs,
      reached: run.send_backs >= b.max_send_backs,
      text: `${run.send_backs} / ${b.max_send_backs}`,
      more: "1 send-back",
    },
  ];
}

/// The limits Add budget raises: the reached ones among runs, tokens and minutes.
export function budgetReached(run: WorkflowRun): Limit[] {
  return limits(run).filter((l) => l.key !== "max_send_backs" && l.reached);
}

/// "a, b and c".
export function joinAnd(items: string[]): string {
  if (items.length <= 1) return items.join("");
  return `${items.slice(0, -1).join(", ")} and ${items.at(-1)}`;
}

/// A link from a step's card to one of its dot runs' run screens.
export type DotRunLink = { id: string; label: string; status: RunStatus | null };

/// The dot runs of a step run and the run each delegate member's branch is from. `runs` are the
/// newest dot runs (newest first): those of the step run are grouped by thread, one link per
/// thread, to its root. A hand-off branch whose run is not among them still gets its link, to
/// the run the branch names. Members' runs are linked from the members, not again from the step.
export function stepRunLinks(
  stepRun: StepRun,
  runs: Run[],
  nameOf: (dotId: string) => string,
): { step: DotRunLink[]; members: Map<string, string> } {
  const byId = new Map(runs.map((r) => [r.id, r]));
  const threads = new Map<string, DotRunLink>();
  for (const r of runs) {
    if (r.workflow_step_run_id !== stepRun.id || threads.has(r.root_run_id)) continue;
    threads.set(r.root_run_id, { id: r.root_run_id, label: nameOf(r.dot_id), status: r.status });
  }
  const linkOf = (runId: string) => byId.get(runId)?.root_run_id ?? runId;
  const handoff = stepRun.handoff;
  for (const ref of handoff?.branches ?? []) {
    const id = linkOf(ref.run_id);
    const known = byId.get(ref.run_id);
    if (threads.has(id)) continue;
    threads.set(id, {
      id,
      label: known ? nameOf(known.dot_id) : ref.branch,
      status: known?.status ?? null,
    });
  }
  const members = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const member of handoff?.members ?? []) {
    const ref = member.branch ? handoff?.branches.find((b) => b.branch === member.branch) : null;
    if (ref) members.set(member.branch!, linkOf(ref.run_id));
  }
  const memberRuns = new Set(members.values());
  return { step: [...threads.values()].filter((l) => !memberRuns.has(l.id)), members };
}
```

### Task 13: A chat that starts a workflow shows its live card after that turn

`Timeline` reads, in one `workflow_runs_for_origins` query, the workflow runs that every run it shows started; `TopicRuns` passes them to `Transcript`'s new `after` slot, so each card sits in the run's section after its check-ins and before the next turn. The `WorkflowCard` shows the team, the workflow, the badges, the spend, the task's first line, one line of step marks along the path taken, an error line, the run page's `CheckPanel` and `LimitPanel`, and Open run, Retry step and one Stop. Its actions never wake the chat's dot. UI only.

**Files:**
- Create: `ui/src/screens/teams/run/WorkflowCard.tsx`
- Modify: `ui/src/components/Transcript.tsx`, `ui/src/screens/home/{Timeline,TopicRuns}.tsx`, `ui/src/screens/teams/run/steps.ts`
- Test: `ui/src/screens/teams/run/WorkflowCard.test.tsx`, `ui/src/screens/home/WorkflowCards.test.tsx` (new), `ui/src/screens/teams/run/steps.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 12's `CheckPanel`, `LimitPanel`, `StopRun`, `RetryStep`, `STEP_STATUS_LABEL`; Task 9's `allSteps`; Task 8's `WorkflowRunBadge`, `TestBadge`, `formatSpend`, `taskLine`; Task 6's `workflowRunsForOrigins`, `keys.workflowRunsForOrigins` (marked stale by `workflow_run_updated`).
- Produces: `useStartedWorkflows(runIds: string[]) -> { byRun: ReadonlyMap<string, WorkflowRun[]>, error: Error | null }` and `WorkflowCard({ run })` (an `<article>` named `Workflow run: <task line>`, `data-status`); `steps.ts`: type `CardStep { id, name, status: StepRunStatus | "not_reached", label }`, `cardSteps(run) -> CardStep[]`; `Transcript`'s prop `after?: (run: Run) => ReactNode`; `TopicRuns`'s prop `workflows?: ReadonlyMap<string, WorkflowRun[]>`. Labels: `Workflow run: <task line>`, `Team`, `· <workflow>`, `· A deleted workflow`, the list `Steps` with items `<step>: <state>`, `Open run`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

**Create `ui/src/screens/home/WorkflowCards.test.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { act, screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import type { Team, Workflow, WorkflowRun } from "@/api/types";
import { backend } from "@/test/backend";
import { sample } from "@/test/fixtures";
import { chat, dot, home, reply, said } from "@/test/home";
import { renderApp } from "@/test/render";

const [crew] = sample.teams as [Team, Team];
const [fixAndShip] = sample.workflows as [Workflow, Workflow, Workflow, Workflow];
const [waiting] = sample.workflowRuns as [WorkflowRun];
const CARD = "Workflow run: The tray icon is invisible on a light taskbar.";

/// A chat topic of two turns, the first of which started crew's Fix and ship, waiting on the
/// check; `started` holds what `workflow_runs_for_origins` answers.
function topic() {
  const first = chat("current", "10:00");
  const second = reply("current2", first, "10:05");
  home(
    [first, second],
    [said("current", 1, "I handed it to the team."), said("current2", 1, "It waits on you.")],
  );
  const started: { runs: WorkflowRun[] } = { runs: [{ ...waiting, origin_run_id: "current" }] };
  backend.on("workflow_runs_for_origins", () => started.runs);
  backend.on("team_get", () => crew);
  backend.on("workflow_get", () => fixAndShip);
  backend.on("workflow_check", () => sample.check);
  return { started, view: renderApp(`/home/${dot.id}`) };
}

const conversation = () => screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Conversation" });
const follows = (a: Node, b: Node) =>
  (a.compareDocumentPosition(b) & Node.DOCUMENT_POSITION_FOLLOWING) !== 0;

describe("a workflow started from a chat", () => {
  it("shows its card after the turn whose run started it, from one query for the topic", async () => {
    const { view } = topic();
    const shown = within(await conversation());
    const card = await shown.findByRole("article", { name: CARD });
    expect(await within(card).findByText("· Fix and ship")).toBeVisible();
    // After the first turn's words, before the second turn.
    expect(follows(shown.getByText("I handed it to the team."), card)).toBe(true);
    expect(follows(card, shown.getByText("reply current2"))).toBe(true);
    expect(shown.getAllByRole("article", { name: CARD })).toHaveLength(1);
    await view.listening();
    const calls = backend.callsTo("workflow_runs_for_origins");
    expect(calls.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    for (const call of calls) expect(call).toEqual({ runIds: ["current", "current2"] });
  });

  it("acts on the workflow only: approving does not wake the chat's dot", async () => {
    const { started, view } = topic();
    const card = within(await within(await conversation()).findByRole("article", { name: CARD }));
    await view.listening();
    const moved: WorkflowRun = { ...started.runs[0]!, status: "running", waiting_on: null };
    backend.on("workflow_approve", () => moved);
    started.runs = [moved];
    await view.user.click(await card.findByRole("button", { name: "Approve" }));
    expect(await card.findByText("Running")).toHaveAttribute("data-status", "running");
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_approve")).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(backend.callsTo("chat")).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("follows the workflow run's events", async () => {
    const { started, view } = topic();
    const card = within(await within(await conversation()).findByRole("article", { name: CARD }));
    expect(await card.findByRole("region", { name: "Your check" })).toBeVisible();
    await view.listening();
    const finished: WorkflowRun = {
      ...started.runs[0]!,
      status: "succeeded",
      waiting_on: null,
      current_step: null,
    };
    started.runs = [finished];
    act(() => backend.emit("runtime", { type: "workflow_run_updated", run: finished }));
    expect(await card.findByText("Succeeded")).toHaveAttribute("data-status", "succeeded");
    await waitFor(() => expect(card.queryByRole("region", { name: "Your check" })).toBeNull());
    expect(card.queryByRole("button", { name: "Stop" })).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/run/WorkflowCard.test.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { render, screen, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { MemoryRouter } from "react-router";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import type { Team, Workflow, WorkflowRun } from "@/api/types";
import { createQueryClient } from "@/lib/query";
import { backend } from "@/test/backend";
import { sample } from "@/test/fixtures";

import { WorkflowCard } from "./WorkflowCard";

const [crew] = sample.teams as [Team, Team];
const [fixAndShip] = sample.workflows as [Workflow, Workflow, Workflow, Workflow];
const [waiting, done, , failed, budget] = sample.workflowRuns as [
  WorkflowRun,
  WorkflowRun,
  WorkflowRun,
  WorkflowRun,
  WorkflowRun,
];
const TASK = "The tray icon is invisible on a light taskbar.";

/// The card of `run` by itself, of crew's Fix and ship.
function show(run: WorkflowRun) {
  backend.on("team_get", () => crew);
  backend.on("workflow_get", () => fixAndShip);
  backend.on("workflow_check", () => sample.check);
  const view = render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={createQueryClient()}>
      <MemoryRouter>
        <WorkflowCard run={run} />
      </MemoryRouter>
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
  return { ...view, user: userEvent.setup() };
}

const card = () => within(screen.getByRole("article", { name: `Workflow run: ${TASK}` }));
/// The names of the card's steps, each with its state.
const marks = () =>
  within(card().getByRole("list", { name: "Steps" }))
    .getAllByRole("listitem")
    .map((item) => item.getAttribute("aria-label"));

describe("a workflow's card in the chat", () => {
  it("shows the team, the workflow, the task, the status, the spend and the steps' marks", async () => {
    show(waiting);
    expect(await card().findByText("· Fix and ship")).toBeVisible();
    expect(card().getByText("crew")).toBeVisible();
    expect(card().getByText(TASK)).toBeVisible();
    expect(card().getByText("Needs your check")).toHaveAttribute("data-status", "waiting");
    expect(card().getByText("3 runs · 120k tokens")).toBeVisible();
    expect(card().getByRole("list", { name: "Steps" })).toBeVisible();
    // The branch took its "fixed" path: its steps stand in its place.
    expect(marks()).toEqual(["Build: Done", "Review: Waiting for you", "Ship: Not reached"]);
    // The check's actions, one Stop for the card, and Open run.
    const check = within(card().getByRole("region", { name: "Your check" }));
    expect(await check.findByRole("button", { name: "Approve" })).toBeEnabled();
    expect(
      check.getByRole("button", { name: "View diff of dots/nightly-tests/01J0RUN5" }),
    ).toBeVisible();
    expect(check.queryByRole("button", { name: "Stop" })).toBeNull();
    expect(card().getAllByRole("button", { name: "Stop" })).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(card().getByRole("link", { name: "Open run" })).toHaveAttribute(
      "href",
      "/workflow-runs/01J0WFR",
    );
    expect(card().queryByRole("button", { name: "Retry step" })).toBeNull();
  });

  it("approves the check with the branches and commits it showed", async () => {
    backend.on("workflow_approve", () => ({ ...waiting, status: "running", waiting_on: null }));
    const { user } = show(waiting);
    await user.click(await card().findByRole("button", { name: "Approve" }));
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_approve")).toEqual([
      {
        id: "01J0WFR",
        note: null,
        tips: sample.check.branches.map(({ branch, commit }) => ({ branch, commit })),
      },
    ]);
  });

  it("offers Add budget at the budget, with one Stop, for a run whose workflow was deleted", async () => {
    show(budget);
    expect(await card().findByText("crew")).toBeVisible();
    expect(card().getByText("· A deleted workflow")).toBeVisible();
    expect(card().getByRole("region", { name: "Budget reached" })).toBeVisible();
    expect(card().getByRole("button", { name: "Add budget" })).toBeEnabled();
    expect(card().getAllByRole("button", { name: "Stop" })).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(card().queryByRole("region", { name: "Your check" })).toBeNull();
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_get")).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("shows a failed step's error on a test run, and retries the step", async () => {
    backend.on("workflow_retry_step", () => ({ ...failed, status: "running", waiting_on: null }));
    const { user } = show(failed);
    expect(card().getByText("Test")).toBeVisible();
    expect(card().getByText("A step failed")).toBeVisible();
    expect(card().getByText("Ship: gh: could not create the pull request")).toBeVisible();
    // Before the branch decided, the path whose step ran stands in its place.
    expect(marks()).toEqual(["Build: Not reached", "Review: Not reached", "Ship: Failed"]);
    await user.click(card().getByRole("button", { name: "Retry step" }));
    expect(backend.callsTo("workflow_retry_step")).toEqual([{ id: "01J0WFR4" }]);
  });

  it("spins on a running step, and leaves a finished run with Open run only", async () => {
    const steps = waiting.steps.map((s) =>
      s.step_id === "review" ? { ...s, status: "running" as const } : s,
    );
    const running: WorkflowRun = { ...waiting, status: "running", waiting_on: null, steps };
    const { unmount } = show(running);
    const review = card().getByRole("listitem", { name: "Review: Running" });
    expect(review).toHaveAttribute("data-status", "running");
    expect(review.querySelector("svg")).toHaveClass("animate-spin", "motion-reduce:animate-none");
    expect(card().getByRole("button", { name: "Stop" })).toBeVisible();
    expect(card().queryByRole("region", { name: "Your check" })).toBeNull();
    unmount();

    show(done);
    expect(card().getByText("Succeeded")).toBeVisible();
    expect(marks()).toEqual(["Build: Done", "Review: Done", "Ship: Done"]);
    expect(card().queryAllByRole("button")).toEqual([]);
    expect(card().getByRole("link", { name: "Open run" })).toHaveAttribute(
      "href",
      "/workflow-runs/01J0WFR2",
    );
  });
});
```

**Create `ui/src/screens/teams/run/WorkflowCard.tsx`:**

```tsx
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { cn } from "cn";
import { LoaderCircle } from "lucide-react";
import { Link } from "react-router";

import { commands } from "@/api/commands";
import type { WorkflowRun } from "@/api/types";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { keys } from "@/lib/query";

import { formatSpend, taskLine } from "../format";
import { TestBadge, WorkflowRunBadge } from "../WorkflowRunBadge";
import { inFlight, RetryStep, StopRun } from "./actions";
import { CheckPanel } from "./CheckPanel";
import { LimitPanel } from "./LimitPanel";
import { cardSteps, type CardStep, latestStepRuns } from "./steps";

/// The workflow runs that the runs `runIds` started (teams spec section 10.4), by the run that
/// started each, oldest first: one query for every run a conversation shows. Every workflow
/// run's event marks it stale (`lib/runtime-events.ts`), so a new run's card shows and each
/// card follows its run.
export function useStartedWorkflows(runIds: string[]): {
  byRun: ReadonlyMap<string, WorkflowRun[]>;
  error: Error | null;
} {
  const started = useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.workflowRunsForOrigins(runIds),
    queryFn: () => commands.workflowRunsForOrigins(runIds),
    enabled: runIds.length > 0,
  });
  const byRun = new Map<string, WorkflowRun[]>();
  for (const run of started.data ?? []) {
    if (run.origin_run_id === null) continue;
    byRun.set(run.origin_run_id, [...(byRun.get(run.origin_run_id) ?? []), run]);
  }
  return { byRun, error: started.isError ? started.error : null };
}

/// The look of a step's mark: done, waiting on the person, running (spinning), failed, stopped
/// or skipped, not reached.
const MARK: Record<CardStep["status"], { glyph: string; className: string }> = {
  succeeded: { glyph: "✓", className: "text-success" },
  waiting: { glyph: "●", className: "text-needs" },
  running: { glyph: "", className: "text-primary" },
  failed: { glyph: "✕", className: "text-destructive" },
  cancelled: { glyph: "–", className: "text-muted-foreground" },
  skipped: { glyph: "–", className: "text-muted-foreground" },
  not_reached: { glyph: "○", className: "text-muted-foreground" },
};

/// One step on the card's line: its mark, then its name. The item is named by the step and its
/// state.
function StepMark({ step }: { step: CardStep }) {
  const mark = MARK[step.status];
  return (
    <li
      aria-label={`${step.name}: ${step.label}`}
      data-status={step.status}
      title={step.label}
      className={cn("flex items-center gap-1", step.status === "waiting" && "text-needs")}
    >
      {step.status === "running" ? (
        <LoaderCircle
          aria-hidden
          className={cn("size-3 animate-spin motion-reduce:animate-none", mark.className)}
        />
      ) : (
        <span aria-hidden className={mark.className}>
          {mark.glyph}
        </span>
      )}
      {step.name}
    </li>
  );
}

/// A workflow run as a card in the conversation of the chat that started it (section 10.4,
/// option B), live: the team and the workflow, the task's first line, the status, the spend, the
/// steps as one line of marks, and what the person can do now: the check (View diff, Send back,
/// Approve), a limit (Add budget or one more send-back), a failed step (Retry step), Stop while
/// it is in flight, and always Open run. The actions call the workflow commands only: none of
/// them wakes the chat's dot.
export function WorkflowCard({ run }: { run: WorkflowRun }) {
  const team = useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.team(run.team_id),
    queryFn: () => commands.teamGet(run.team_id),
  });
  const workflowId = run.workflow_id;
  const workflow = useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.workflow(workflowId ?? ""),
    queryFn: () => commands.workflowGet(workflowId!),
    enabled: workflowId !== null,
  });
  const workflowName =
    workflowId === null || workflow.isError ? "A deleted workflow" : (workflow.data?.name ?? "");
  const task = taskLine(run.task);
  const stepFailed = run.status === "waiting" && run.waiting_on === "step_failed";
  // A failed step's error, or the run's when it failed; a step that failed says it once.
  const failedStep =
    stepFailed && run.current_step ? latestStepRuns(run).get(run.current_step) : undefined;
  const error =
    run.status === "failed" || stepFailed ? (run.error ?? failedStep?.error ?? null) : null;
  return (
    <article
      aria-label={`Workflow run: ${task || "No task."}`}
      data-status={run.status}
      className={cn(
        "flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-xl border bg-card px-4 py-3",
        run.status === "waiting" && "border-needs-border",
      )}
    >
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <span className="font-semibold">{team.data?.name ?? "Team"}</span>
        {workflowName && <span className="text-muted-foreground">· {workflowName}</span>}
        <span className="flex-1" />
        {run.test && <TestBadge />}
        <WorkflowRunBadge status={run.status} waitingOn={run.waiting_on} />
        <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{formatSpend(run)}</span>
      </div>
      <p className="truncate">{task || "No task."}</p>
      <ol aria-label="Steps" className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-xs">
        {cardSteps(run).map((step) => (
          <StepMark key={step.id} step={step} />
        ))}
      </ol>
      {error && <p className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p>}
      <CheckPanel run={run} stop={false} />
      <LimitPanel run={run} stop={false} />
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <Button asChild size="sm" variant="outline">
          <Link to={`/workflow-runs/${run.id}`}>Open run</Link>
        </Button>
        {stepFailed && <RetryStep run={run} />}
        {inFlight(run) && <StopRun run={run} />}
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}
```

```ts
}
```

**with:**

```ts
  return agentsBefore(steps, checkId).at(-1);
}

/// A step on the chat card's line of steps (section 10.4): its name and its newest step run's
/// state, as the card says it.
export type CardStep = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  status: StepRunStatus | "not_reached";
  label: string;
};

/// The chat card's line of steps (section 10.4), in the definition's order. A branch step gives
/// way to the steps of the path it took; before it decided, to a path one of whose steps already
/// ran, else it stands for its paths, not reached. A path not taken is left out.
export function cardSteps(run: Pick<WorkflowRun, "definition" | "steps">): CardStep[] {
  const latest = latestStepRuns(run);
  const ran = (steps: Step[]) => allSteps(steps).some((step) => latest.has(step.id));
  const out: CardStep[] = [];
  const walk = (steps: Step[]) => {
    for (const step of steps) {
      if (step.kind === "branch") {
        const taken = pathTaken(latest.get(step.id));
        const path =
          taken === undefined
            ? [...step.paths.map((p) => p.steps), step.otherwise].find(ran)
            : taken === null
              ? step.otherwise
              : step.paths.find((p) => p.label === taken)?.steps;
        if (path) {
          walk(path);
          continue;
        }
      }
      const status = latest.get(step.id)?.status ?? "not_reached";
      out.push({
        id: step.id,
        name: step.name.trim() || step.id,
        status,
        label:
          step.kind === "check" && status === "waiting"
            ? "Waiting for you"
            : STEP_STATUS_LABEL[status],
      });
    }
  };
  walk(run.definition.steps);
  return out;
}
```

### Task 14: Waiting workflow runs are listed in the Inbox and counted in the rail

The Inbox gets a "Workflows" section after Apps to review, one row per waiting run (task, team, workflow, step, started, Test, what it waits on, Open the run), and is no longer shown empty while only workflow runs wait. The rail's Inbox count adds `workflows` and the `team_scope` it missed, so it equals the core's `inbox_count`. The team page lists its own waiting runs under "Waiting", above Members. UI only.

**Files:**
- Create: `ui/src/screens/teams/WaitingRuns.tsx`
- Modify: `ui/src/screens/InboxScreen.tsx`, `ui/src/app/Layout.tsx`, `ui/src/screens/teams/TeamScreen.tsx`
- Test: `ui/src/screens/InboxWorkflows.test.tsx` (new), `ui/src/app/Layout.test.tsx`, `ui/src/screens/teams/TeamScreen.test.tsx`; counts brought up to date (the rail now counts 2 more kinds: `sample.inbox` has one of each): `ui/src/app/Layout.test.tsx` (8 to 10, three tests), `ui/src/screens/home/HomeRoutes.test.tsx` (8 to 10), `ui/src/screens/InboxMessages.test.tsx` (10 to 12)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 5's `Inbox.workflows` and `WaitingWorkflowRun`; Task 8's `TeamSection`, `WorkflowRunBadge`, `TestBadge`, `WAITING_LABEL`, `taskLine`; `keys.inbox` (marked stale by `workflow_run_updated`).
- Produces: `WaitingRunRow({ item: WaitingWorkflowRun, team? = true })` (`<li aria-label="<workflow> of <team> waiting">`, the link `Open the run`), `TeamWaiting({ teamId })` (`null` while none waits; a `TeamSection` id `team-waiting`, title `Waiting`). Labels: the section `Workflows`, `· at <step>`, `· started <relative time>`, and the Inbox's empty text `Nothing waits for you. Questions and notes from dots, approvals a dot asks for, apps sent for review, workflow runs that wait for you, schedules that need attention, paused watchers and finished runs to review show up here.`.
